'use strict';

const BASE = Object.freeze({
  name: 'compat',
  strictConfiguration: false,
  failFast: false,
  freezeDefinitions: false,
  allowLateRegistration: true,
  cardinalityPolicy: 'drop',
  queueOverflow: 'drop-oldest',
  exporterErrorPolicy: 'isolate',
  resourceErrorPolicy: 'isolate',
  healthErrorPolicy: 'report',
  captureErrorStacks: false,
  captureRequestHeaders: false,
  captureRequestBodies: false,
  studioDefaultEnabled: true,
  studioProductionEnabled: false,
  requireStudioAuthorization: false,
  requireSignedRemoteIngest: false,
  metricsEnabled: true,
  tracingEnabled: true,
  alertsEnabled: true,
  incidentsEnabled: true,
  sloEnabled: true,
  systemMetricsEnabled: true,
  analysisEnabled: true,
  syntheticsEnabled: false,
  pluginsEnabled: true,
  persistenceEnabled: false,
});

export const MONITOR_COMPATIBILITY_PROFILES = Object.freeze({
  compat: BASE,
  enterprise: Object.freeze({
    ...BASE,
    name: 'enterprise',
    freezeDefinitions: true,
    allowLateRegistration: false,
    studioDefaultEnabled: true,
    requireStudioAuthorization: true,
    persistenceEnabled: true,
  }),
  production: Object.freeze({
    ...BASE,
    name: 'production',
    strictConfiguration: true,
    failFast: false,
    freezeDefinitions: true,
    allowLateRegistration: false,
    queueOverflow: 'drop-new',
    captureErrorStacks: false,
    studioDefaultEnabled: false,
    requireStudioAuthorization: true,
    requireSignedRemoteIngest: true,
    persistenceEnabled: true,
  }),
  strict: Object.freeze({
    ...BASE,
    name: 'strict',
    strictConfiguration: true,
    failFast: true,
    freezeDefinitions: true,
    allowLateRegistration: false,
    cardinalityPolicy: 'error',
    queueOverflow: 'reject',
    exporterErrorPolicy: 'propagate',
    resourceErrorPolicy: 'propagate',
    healthErrorPolicy: 'propagate',
    studioDefaultEnabled: false,
    requireStudioAuthorization: true,
    requireSignedRemoteIngest: true,
    persistenceEnabled: true,
  }),
  test: Object.freeze({
    ...BASE,
    name: 'test',
    systemMetricsEnabled: false,
    alertsEnabled: false,
    incidentsEnabled: false,
    analysisEnabled: false,
    syntheticsEnabled: false,
    persistenceEnabled: false,
  }),
});

export function resolveMonitorCompatibilityProfile(profile = 'compat', overrides = {}) {
  const supplied = profile && typeof profile === 'object' ? profile : null;
  const requested = typeof profile === 'string' ? profile : supplied?.name || 'compat';
  const preset = MONITOR_COMPATIBILITY_PROFILES[requested] || MONITOR_COMPATIBILITY_PROFILES.compat;
  return Object.freeze({ ...preset, ...(supplied || {}), ...(overrides || {}), name: preset.name });
}

export default resolveMonitorCompatibilityProfile;

================================================
FILE PATH: ./metrics/PrometheusFormatter.js
================================================

'use strict';

import { sanitizeLabelName, sanitizeMetricName } from '../support.js';

function escapeHelp(value) { return String(value || '').replace(/\\/g, '\\\\').replace(/\n/g, '\\n'); }
function escapeLabel(value) { return String(value ?? '').replace(/\\/g, '\\\\').replace(/\n/g, '\\n').replace(/"/g, '\\"'); }
function labelsText(labels = {}, extra = null) {
  const entries = [...Object.entries(labels), ...(extra ? Object.entries(extra) : [])];
  if (!entries.length) return '';
  return `{${entries.map(([key, value]) => `${sanitizeLabelName(key)}="${escapeLabel(value)}"`).join(',')}}`;
}
function number(value) { return Number.isFinite(Number(value)) ? String(Number(value)) : '0'; }
function exemplarText(exemplar) {
  if (!exemplar?.traceId) return '';
  const labels = { trace_id: exemplar.traceId };
  if (exemplar.spanId) labels.span_id = exemplar.spanId;
  return ` # ${labelsText(labels).slice(1, -1)} ${number(exemplar.value)} ${Math.trunc(exemplar.timestamp)}`;
}

export function formatPrometheus(snapshot, options = {}) {
  const lines = [];
  for (const metric of Object.values(snapshot?.metrics || snapshot?.metrics?.metrics || {})) {
    const name = sanitizeMetricName(metric.name);
    if (metric.description) lines.push(`# HELP ${name} ${escapeHelp(metric.description)}`);
    const type = metric.type === 'timer' || metric.type === 'histogram' ? 'histogram' : metric.type === 'counter' ? 'counter' : 'gauge';
    lines.push(`# TYPE ${name} ${type}`);
    if (metric.unit) lines.push(`# UNIT ${name} ${sanitizeMetricName(metric.unit)}`);

    for (const series of metric.series || []) {
      if (type === 'histogram') {
        for (const bucket of series.buckets || []) lines.push(`${name}_bucket${labelsText(series.labels, { le: bucket.upperBound })} ${number(bucket.count)}`);
        lines.push(`${name}_bucket${labelsText(series.labels, { le: '+Inf' })} ${number(series.count)}`);
        lines.push(`${name}_sum${labelsText(series.labels)} ${number(series.sum)}`);
        lines.push(`${name}_count${labelsText(series.labels)} ${number(series.count)}${options.exemplars !== false ? exemplarText(series.exemplar) : ''}`);
      } else {
        lines.push(`${name}${labelsText(series.labels)} ${number(series.value)}${options.exemplars !== false ? exemplarText(series.exemplar) : ''}`);
      }
    }
  }
  return `${lines.join('\n')}\n`;
}

export default formatPrometheus;


================================================
FILE PATH: ./metrics/MetricHistory.js
================================================

'use strict';

export class MetricHistory {
  constructor(options = {}) {
    this.limit = Math.max(1, Number(options.limit) || 720);
    this.items = new Array(this.limit);
    this.cursor = 0;
    this.length = 0;
  }

  push(snapshot) {
    this.items[this.cursor] = snapshot;
    this.cursor = (this.cursor + 1) % this.limit;
    this.length = Math.min(this.limit, this.length + 1);
    return snapshot;
  }

  all(options = {}) {
    const output = [];
    const start = (this.cursor - this.length + this.limit) % this.limit;
    for (let index = 0; index < this.length; index++) output.push(this.items[(start + index) % this.limit]);
    const since = Number(options.since || 0);
    const until = Number(options.until || Infinity);
    const filtered = output.filter((item) => Number(item?.timestamp || 0) >= since && Number(item?.timestamp || 0) <= until);
    return options.limit ? filtered.slice(-Math.max(1, Number(options.limit))) : filtered;
  }

  latest() { return this.length ? this.items[(this.cursor - 1 + this.limit) % this.limit] : null; }
  clear() { this.items.fill(undefined); this.cursor = 0; this.length = 0; }
  snapshot() { return Object.freeze({ limit: this.limit, length: this.length, oldest: this.all({ limit: this.length })[0]?.timestamp || null, latest: this.latest()?.timestamp || null }); }
}

export default MetricHistory;

FILE PATH: ./metrics/MetricRegistry.js

'use strict';

import { Counter, Gauge, Histogram, ObservableGauge, Timer, UpDownCounter } from './Instruments.js';
import { MetricDefinitionError } from '../errors.js';
import { boundedInteger, deepFreeze, sanitizeMetricName } from '../support.js';

export class MetricRegistry {
  constructor(options = {}) {
    this.options = Object.freeze({
      enabled: options.enabled !== false,
      prefix: options.prefix ? sanitizeMetricName(options.prefix) : '',
      maxInstruments: boundedInteger(options.maxInstruments, 10_000, 1, 1_000_000),
      maxSeries: boundedInteger(options.maxSeries, 100_000, 1, 10_000_000),
      maxSeriesPerInstrument: boundedInteger(options.maxSeriesPerInstrument, 2_000, 1, 1_000_000),
      maxLabels: boundedInteger(options.maxLabels, 24, 0, 256),
      maxLabelValueBytes: boundedInteger(options.maxLabelValueBytes, 256, 1, 64 * 1024),
      cardinalityPolicy: options.cardinalityPolicy || 'drop',
      allowLateRegistration: options.allowLateRegistration !== false,
      allowLateSeries: options.allowLateSeries !== false,
      exemplars: options.exemplars !== false,
      histogramBuckets: options.histogramBuckets || null,
    });
    this.labelOptions = Object.freeze({ maxLabels: this.options.maxLabels, maxValueBytes: this.options.maxLabelValueBytes });
    this.instruments = new Map();
    this.seriesKeys = new Set();
    this.seriesCount = 0;
    this.frozen = false;
    this.contextProvider = options.contextProvider || (() => null);
    this.stats = { registrations: 0, duplicateRegistrations: 0, globalSeriesRejected: 0, collections: 0, collectionErrors: 0 };
  }

  counter(name, options = {}) { return this._instrument('counter', name, options, Counter); }
  upDownCounter(name, options = {}) { return this._instrument('updowncounter', name, options, UpDownCounter); }
  gauge(name, options = {}) { return this._instrument('gauge', name, options, Gauge); }
  histogram(name, options = {}) { return this._instrument('histogram', name, options, Histogram); }
  timer(name, options = {}) { return this._instrument('timer', name, options, Timer); }
  observableGauge(name, callback, options = {}) { return this._instrument('observable_gauge', name, { ...options, callback }, ObservableGauge, callback); }

  _instrument(type, inputName, options, Instrument, callback = null) {
    const name = this._name(inputName);
    const existing = this.instruments.get(name);
    if (existing) {
      if (existing.type !== type) throw new MetricDefinitionError(`Metric '${name}' is already registered as '${existing.type}', not '${type}'.`);
      this.stats.duplicateRegistrations++;
      return existing;
    }
    if (this.frozen || (!this.options.allowLateRegistration && this.instruments.size > 0)) throw new MetricDefinitionError(`Metric registry is frozen; '${name}' cannot be registered.`);
    if (this.instruments.size >= this.options.maxInstruments) throw new MetricDefinitionError(`Metric instrument limit of ${this.options.maxInstruments} exceeded.`);
    const instrument = callback ? new Instrument(this, name, callback, options) : new Instrument(this, name, options);
    this.instruments.set(name, instrument);
    this.stats.registrations++;
    return instrument;
  }

  get(name) { return this.instruments.get(this._name(name)) || this.instruments.get(String(name)) || null; }
  has(name) { return !!this.get(name); }
  remove(name) {
    const key = this._name(name);
    const instrument = this.instruments.get(key);
    if (!instrument) return false;
    this._releaseSeries(instrument.series.size);
    return this.instruments.delete(key);
  }
  names() { return Array.from(this.instruments.keys()).sort(); }
  freeze() { this.frozen = true; for (const instrument of this.instruments.values()) instrument.freeze(); return this; }

  async collect(context = {}) {
    this.stats.collections++;
    for (const instrument of this.instruments.values()) {
      if (typeof instrument.collect !== 'function') continue;
      try { await instrument.collect(context); }
      catch { this.stats.collectionErrors++; }
    }
    return this.snapshot();
  }

  snapshot(options = {}) {
    const names = options.names ? new Set(options.names.map((name) => this._name(name))) : null;
    const metrics = Object.create(null);
    for (const [name, instrument] of this.instruments) if (!names || names.has(name)) metrics[name] = instrument.snapshot();
    return deepFreeze({
      timestamp: Date.now(),
      prefix: this.options.prefix,
      instruments: this.instruments.size,
      series: this.seriesCount,
      frozen: this.frozen,
      metrics,
      stats: { ...this.stats },
    });
  }

  reset(options = {}) {
    for (const instrument of this.instruments.values()) instrument.reset();
    if (options.definitions === true) this.instruments.clear();
    this.seriesKeys.clear();
    this.seriesCount = 0;
    return this;
  }

  _name(value) {
    const name = sanitizeMetricName(value);
    return this.options.prefix && !name.startsWith(`${this.options.prefix}_`) && !name.startsWith(`${this.options.prefix}:`)
      ? `${this.options.prefix}_${name}`
      : name;
  }

  _admitSeries(instrument, key) {
    if (this.seriesCount >= this.options.maxSeries) { this.stats.globalSeriesRejected++; return false; }
    return !this.seriesKeys.has(`${instrument.name}\0${key}`);
  }
  _commitSeries(instrument, key) { this.seriesKeys.add(`${instrument.name}\0${key}`); this.seriesCount++; }
  _releaseSeries(count) { this.seriesCount = Math.max(0, this.seriesCount - count); }

  _exemplar(value, options = {}) {
    if (!this.options.exemplars) return null;
    const context = options.context || this.contextProvider?.() || null;
    if (!context?.traceId) return null;
    return Object.freeze({ traceId: context.traceId, spanId: context.spanId || null, value, timestamp: Date.now() });
  }

  definitionManifest() { return Object.freeze(this.names().map((name) => this.instruments.get(name).definition())); }
}

export default MetricRegistry;


================================================
FILE PATH: ./metrics/Instruments.js
================================================

'use strict';

import { DEFAULT_HISTOGRAM_BUCKETS } from '../constants.js';
import { CardinalityLimitError, MetricDefinitionError } from '../errors.js';
import { labelsKey, normalizeLabels, sanitizeMetricName } from '../support.js';

class BaseInstrument {
  constructor(registry, type, name, options = {}) {
    this.registry = registry;
    this.type = type;
    this.name = sanitizeMetricName(name);
    this.description = options.description || '';
    this.unit = options.unit || '';
    this.monotonic = options.monotonic === true;
    this.maxSeries = Math.max(1, Number(options.maxSeries) || registry.options.maxSeriesPerInstrument);
    this.constantLabels = normalizeLabels(options.labels || options.constantLabels || {}, registry.labelOptions);
    this.series = new Map();
    this.createdAt = Date.now();
    this.updatedAt = this.createdAt;
    this.droppedSeries = 0;
    this.invalidValues = 0;
    this._frozen = false;
  }

  _labels(input = {}) {
    return normalizeLabels({ ...this.constantLabels, ...(input || {}) }, this.registry.labelOptions);
  }

  _series(labels, create) {
    const normalized = this._labels(labels);
    const key = labelsKey(normalized);
    let series = this.series.get(key);
    if (series) return series;
    if (this._frozen && !this.registry.options.allowLateSeries) return null;
    if (this.series.size >= this.maxSeries || !this.registry._admitSeries(this, key)) {
      this.droppedSeries++;
      if (this.registry.options.cardinalityPolicy === 'error') {
        throw new CardinalityLimitError(`Metric '${this.name}' exceeded its cardinality limit.`, { details: { metric: this.name, maxSeries: this.maxSeries } });
      }
      if (this.registry.options.cardinalityPolicy === 'aggregate') {
        const overflowLabels = Object.freeze({ _overflow: 'true' });
        const overflowKey = labelsKey(overflowLabels);
        series = this.series.get(overflowKey);
        if (!series) {
          series = create(overflowLabels);
          this.series.set(overflowKey, series);
          this.registry._commitSeries(this, overflowKey);
        }
        return series;
      }
      return null;
    }
    series = create(normalized);
    this.series.set(key, series);
    this.registry._commitSeries(this, key);
    return series;
  }

  _number(value) {
    const number = Number(value);
    if (!Number.isFinite(number)) { this.invalidValues++; return null; }
    return number;
  }

  freeze() { this._frozen = true; return this; }
  reset() { this.registry._releaseSeries(this.series.size); this.series.clear(); this.updatedAt = Date.now(); return this; }

  definition() {
    return Object.freeze({ type: this.type, name: this.name, description: this.description, unit: this.unit, monotonic: this.monotonic, createdAt: this.createdAt, maxSeries: this.maxSeries });
  }

  _baseSnapshot() {
    return {
      ...this.definition(),
      updatedAt: this.updatedAt,
      cardinality: this.series.size,
      droppedSeries: this.droppedSeries,
      invalidValues: this.invalidValues,
    };
  }
}

export class Counter extends BaseInstrument {
  constructor(registry, name, options = {}) { super(registry, 'counter', name, { ...options, monotonic: true }); }
  _createSeries(labels) { return this._series(labels, (normalized) => ({ labels: normalized, value: 0, count: 0, updatedAt: 0, exemplar: null })); }
  _addSeries(series, value = 1, options = {}) {
    const number = this._number(value);
    if (number == null || !series) return false;
    if (number < 0 && options.allowNegative !== true) throw new MetricDefinitionError(`Counter '${this.name}' cannot decrease.`);
    series.value += number;
    series.count++;
    series.updatedAt = this.updatedAt = Date.now();
    series.exemplar = this.registry._exemplar(number, options);
    return true;
  }
  add(value = 1, labels = {}, options = {}) { return this._addSeries(this._createSeries(labels), value, options); }
  inc(labels = {}, options = {}) { return this.add(1, labels, options); }
  labels(labels = {}) {
    const series = this._createSeries(labels);
    return Object.freeze({
      labels: series?.labels || this._labels(labels),
      add: (value = 1, options = {}) => this._addSeries(series, value, options),
      inc: (options = {}) => this._addSeries(series, 1, options),
    });
  }
  bind(labels = {}) { return this.labels(labels); }
  snapshot() { return Object.freeze({ ...this._baseSnapshot(), series: Object.freeze(Array.from(this.series.values(), (entry) => Object.freeze({ ...entry }))) }); }
}

export class UpDownCounter extends BaseInstrument {
  constructor(registry, name, options = {}) { super(registry, 'updowncounter', name, options); }
  _createSeries(labels) { return this._series(labels, (normalized) => ({ labels: normalized, value: 0, count: 0, updatedAt: 0, exemplar: null })); }
  _addSeries(series, value = 1, options = {}) {
    const number = this._number(value);
    if (number == null || !series) return false;
    series.value += number;
    series.count++;
    series.updatedAt = this.updatedAt = Date.now();
    series.exemplar = this.registry._exemplar(number, options);
    return true;
  }
  add(value = 1, labels = {}, options = {}) { return this._addSeries(this._createSeries(labels), value, options); }
  inc(labels = {}, options = {}) { return this.add(1, labels, options); }
  dec(labels = {}, options = {}) { return this.add(-1, labels, options); }
  labels(labels = {}) {
    const series = this._createSeries(labels);
    return Object.freeze({
      labels: series?.labels || this._labels(labels),
      add: (value = 1, options = {}) => this._addSeries(series, value, options),
      inc: (options = {}) => this._addSeries(series, 1, options),
      dec: (options = {}) => this._addSeries(series, -1, options),
    });
  }
  bind(labels = {}) { return this.labels(labels); }
  snapshot() { return Object.freeze({ ...this._baseSnapshot(), series: Object.freeze(Array.from(this.series.values(), (entry) => Object.freeze({ ...entry }))) }); }
}

export class Gauge extends BaseInstrument {
  constructor(registry, name, options = {}) { super(registry, 'gauge', name, options); }
  _createSeries(labels) { return this._series(labels, (normalized) => ({ labels: normalized, value: 0, count: 0, updatedAt: 0, exemplar: null })); }
  _setSeries(series, value, options = {}) {
    const number = this._number(value);
    if (number == null || !series) return false;
    series.value = number;
    series.count++;
    series.updatedAt = this.updatedAt = Date.now();
    series.exemplar = this.registry._exemplar(number, options);
    return true;
  }
  _addSeries(series, value, options = {}) {
    const number = this._number(value);
    if (number == null || !series) return false;
    series.value += number;
    series.count++;
    series.updatedAt = this.updatedAt = Date.now();
    series.exemplar = this.registry._exemplar(number, options);
    return true;
  }
  set(value, labels = {}, options = {}) { return this._setSeries(this._createSeries(labels), value, options); }
  add(value, labels = {}, options = {}) { return this._addSeries(this._createSeries(labels), value, options); }
  labels(labels = {}) {
    const series = this._createSeries(labels);
    return Object.freeze({
      labels: series?.labels || this._labels(labels),
      set: (value, options = {}) => this._setSeries(series, value, options),
      add: (value, options = {}) => this._addSeries(series, value, options),
    });
  }
  bind(labels = {}) { return this.labels(labels); }
  snapshot() { return Object.freeze({ ...this._baseSnapshot(), series: Object.freeze(Array.from(this.series.values(), (entry) => Object.freeze({ ...entry }))) }); }
}

export class Histogram extends BaseInstrument {
  constructor(registry, name, options = {}) {
    super(registry, 'histogram', name, options);
    const input = options.buckets || registry.options.histogramBuckets || DEFAULT_HISTOGRAM_BUCKETS;
    this.buckets = Object.freeze(Array.from(new Set(input.map(Number).filter(Number.isFinite))).sort((a, b) => a - b));
    if (!this.buckets.length) throw new MetricDefinitionError(`Histogram '${this.name}' requires at least one bucket.`);
  }

  _createSeries(labels) {
    return this._series(labels, (normalized) => ({
      labels: normalized,
      count: 0,
      sum: 0,
      min: Infinity,
      max: -Infinity,
      buckets: new Array(this.buckets.length).fill(0),
      updatedAt: 0,
      exemplar: null,
    }));
  }

  _recordSeries(series, value, options = {}) {
    const number = this._number(value);
    if (number == null || !series) return false;
    series.count++;
    series.sum += number;
    series.min = Math.min(series.min, number);
    series.max = Math.max(series.max, number);
    for (let index = 0; index < this.buckets.length; index++) if (number <= this.buckets[index]) series.buckets[index]++;
    series.updatedAt = this.updatedAt = Date.now();
    series.exemplar = this.registry._exemplar(number, options);
    return true;
  }

  record(value, labels = {}, options = {}) { return this._recordSeries(this._createSeries(labels), value, options); }
  observe(value, labels = {}, options = {}) { return this.record(value, labels, options); }
  labels(labels = {}) {
    const series = this._createSeries(labels);
    return Object.freeze({
      labels: series?.labels || this._labels(labels),
      record: (value, options = {}) => this._recordSeries(series, value, options),
      observe: (value, options = {}) => this._recordSeries(series, value, options),
    });
  }
  bind(labels = {}) { return this.labels(labels); }

  snapshot() {
    const series = Array.from(this.series.values(), (entry) => Object.freeze({
      labels: entry.labels,
      count: entry.count,
      sum: entry.sum,
      min: Number.isFinite(entry.min) ? entry.min : null,
      max: Number.isFinite(entry.max) ? entry.max : null,
      mean: entry.count ? entry.sum / entry.count : 0,
      buckets: Object.freeze(this.buckets.map((upperBound, index) => Object.freeze({ upperBound, count: entry.buckets[index] }))),
      updatedAt: entry.updatedAt,
      exemplar: entry.exemplar,
    }));
    return Object.freeze({ ...this._baseSnapshot(), buckets: this.buckets, series: Object.freeze(series) });
  }
}

export class Timer extends Histogram {
  constructor(registry, name, options = {}) { super(registry, name, { unit: options.unit || 'seconds', ...options }); this.type = 'timer'; }
  start(labels = {}, options = {}) {
    const series = this._createSeries(labels);
    const started = process.hrtime.bigint();
    let stopped = false;
    return (extraLabels = null, extraOptions = null) => {
      if (stopped) return 0;
      stopped = true;
      const seconds = Number(process.hrtime.bigint() - started) / 1e9;
      if (extraLabels && Object.keys(extraLabels).length) this.record(seconds, { ...labels, ...extraLabels }, { ...options, ...(extraOptions || {}) });
      else this._recordSeries(series, seconds, { ...options, ...(extraOptions || {}) });
      return seconds;
    };
  }
  _startSeries(series, options = {}) {
    const started = process.hrtime.bigint();
    let stopped = false;
    return (extraOptions = null) => {
      if (stopped) return 0;
      stopped = true;
      const seconds = Number(process.hrtime.bigint() - started) / 1e9;
      this._recordSeries(series, seconds, { ...options, ...(extraOptions || {}) });
      return seconds;
    };
  }
  async time(callback, labels = {}, options = {}) {
    const stop = this.start(labels, options);
    try { return await callback(); }
    finally { stop(); }
  }
  labels(labels = {}) {
    const series = this._createSeries(labels);
    return Object.freeze({
      labels: series?.labels || this._labels(labels),
      record: (value, options = {}) => this._recordSeries(series, value, options),
      observe: (value, options = {}) => this._recordSeries(series, value, options),
      start: (options = {}) => this._startSeries(series, options),
      time: async (callback, options = {}) => { const stop = this._startSeries(series, options); try { return await callback(); } finally { stop(); } },
    });
  }
  bind(labels = {}) { return this.labels(labels); }
}

export class ObservableGauge extends BaseInstrument {
  constructor(registry, name, callback, options = {}) {
    super(registry, 'observable_gauge', name, options);
    if (typeof callback !== 'function') throw new MetricDefinitionError(`Observable gauge '${this.name}' requires a callback.`);
    this.callback = callback;
    this.lastError = null;
  }

  async collect(context = {}) {
    const observations = [];
    const observe = (value, labels = {}) => observations.push({ value, labels });
    try {
      const result = await this.callback({ observe, instrument: this, context });
      if (typeof result === 'number') observations.push({ value: result, labels: {} });
      else if (Array.isArray(result)) observations.push(...result);
      for (const item of observations) this._set(item.value, item.labels);
      this.lastError = null;
    } catch (error) { this.lastError = { code: error.code || null, message: error.message || String(error), at: Date.now() }; }
    return this.snapshot();
  }

  _set(value, labels = {}) {
    const number = this._number(value);
    if (number == null) return false;
    const series = this._series(labels, (normalized) => ({ labels: normalized, value: 0, count: 0, updatedAt: 0, exemplar: null }));
    if (!series) return false;
    series.value = number;
    series.count++;
    series.updatedAt = this.updatedAt = Date.now();
    return true;
  }

  snapshot() { return Object.freeze({ ...this._baseSnapshot(), lastError: this.lastError, series: Object.freeze(Array.from(this.series.values(), (entry) => Object.freeze({ ...entry }))) }); }
}

export { BaseInstrument };

'use strict';

import { EventEmitter } from 'node:events';
import { performance } from 'node:perf_hooks';
import BoundedSignalQueue from './BoundedSignalQueue.js';
import { bytesOf, cooperativeYield, errorRecord, retry, withTimeout } from '../support.js';

export class SignalPipeline extends EventEmitter {
  constructor(options = {}) {
    super();
    this.queue = options.queue || new BoundedSignalQueue(options);
    this.batchSize = Math.max(1, Number(options.batchSize) || 512);
    this.flushIntervalMs = Math.max(1, Number(options.flushIntervalMs) || 500);
    this.budgetMs = Math.max(0.25, Number(options.budgetMs) || 8);
    this.maxInFlightBatches = Math.max(1, Number(options.maxInFlightBatches) || 2);
    this.deliveryAttempts = Math.max(1, Number(options.deliveryAttempts) || 3);
    this.retryBaseMs = Math.max(0, Number(options.retryBaseMs) || 50);
    this.retryMaxMs = Math.max(this.retryBaseMs, Number(options.retryMaxMs) || 2_000);
    this.failureHistoryLimit = Math.max(1, Number(options.failureHistoryLimit) || 256);
    this.errorPolicy = options.errorPolicy || 'isolate';
    this.sinks = new Map();
    this.timer = null;
    this.scheduled = false;
    this.running = false;
    this.closing = false;
    this.closed = false;
    this.inFlight = new Set();
    this.failureHistory = [];
    this.stats = {
      batches: 0,
      signals: 0,
      sinkWrites: 0,
      sinkErrors: 0,
      sinkRetries: 0,
      failedBatches: 0,
      failedSignals: 0,
      dropped: 0,
      flushes: 0,
      maxBatchMs: 0,
    };
  }

  addSink(name, sink, options = {}) {
    if (!name || !sink) throw new TypeError('SignalPipeline.addSink(name, sink) requires both values.');
    const record = {
      name: String(name),
      sink,
      required: options.required === true,
      signals: options.signals ? new Set(options.signals) : null,
      priority: Number(options.priority || 0),
      maxAttempts: Math.max(1, Number(options.maxAttempts) || this.deliveryAttempts),
      retryBaseMs: Math.max(0, Number(options.retryBaseMs) || this.retryBaseMs),
      retryMaxMs: Math.max(0, Number(options.retryMaxMs) || this.retryMaxMs),
      retryWhen: typeof options.retryWhen === 'function' ? options.retryWhen : null,
    };
    this.sinks.set(record.name, record);
    return this;
  }

  removeSink(name) { return this.sinks.delete(String(name)); }

  start() {
    if (this.running || this.closed || this.closing) return this;
    this.running = true;
    this.timer = setInterval(() => this.schedule(), this.flushIntervalMs);
    this.timer.unref?.();
    return this;
  }

  admit(envelope, options = {}) {
    if (this.closing || this.closed) return Object.freeze({ accepted: false, reason: this.closed ? 'closed' : 'closing' });
    const result = this.queue.tryPush(envelope, this._queueOptions(envelope, options));
    this._afterAdmission(envelope, result);
    return result;
  }

  async admitAsync(envelope, options = {}) {
    if (this.closing || this.closed) return Object.freeze({ accepted: false, reason: this.closed ? 'closed' : 'closing' });
    const result = await this.queue.push(envelope, this._queueOptions(envelope, options));
    this._afterAdmission(envelope, result);
    return result;
  }

  _queueOptions(envelope, options) {
    return {
      ...options,
      bytes: options.bytes ?? bytesOf(envelope, this.queue.maxSignalBytes + 1),
      onDrop: (value, reason) => {
        this.stats.dropped++;
        this.emit('dropped', value, reason);
        options.onDrop?.(value, reason);
      },
    };
  }

  _afterAdmission(envelope, result) {
    if (!result.accepted) return;
    this.emit('admitted', envelope, result);
    if (this.queue.length >= this.batchSize) this.schedule();
  }

  schedule() {
    if (!this.running || this.closed || this.scheduled || !this.queue.length) return false;
    this.scheduled = true;
    const handle = setImmediate(() => {
      this.scheduled = false;
      void this.drain().catch((error) => this._pipelineError(error));
    });
    handle.unref?.();
    return true;
  }

  async drain(options = {}) {
    if (!this.running || this.closed) return this.snapshot();
    if (this.inFlight.size >= this.maxInFlightBatches) return this.snapshot();
    const started = performance.now();
    do {
      const batch = this.queue.shiftMany(this.batchSize);
      if (!batch.length) break;
      const task = this._deliver(batch, options);
      this.inFlight.add(task);
      task.finally(() => { this.inFlight.delete(task); }).catch(() => {});
      if (this.inFlight.size >= this.maxInFlightBatches) await Promise.race(this.inFlight);
      if (performance.now() - started >= this.budgetMs) await cooperativeYield(options.signal);
    } while (this.queue.length && !options.once);
    if (this.queue.length && !this.closing) this.schedule();
    return this.snapshot();
  }

  async _deliver(batch, options = {}) {
    const started = performance.now();
    this.stats.batches++;
    this.stats.signals += batch.length;
    const sinks = Array.from(this.sinks.values()).sort((a, b) => a.priority - b.priority || a.name.localeCompare(b.name));
    for (const record of sinks) {
      const selected = record.signals ? batch.filter((entry) => record.signals.has(entry.kind)) : batch;
      if (!selected.length) continue;
      try {
        await retry(async (attempt) => {
          if (attempt > 1) this.stats.sinkRetries++;
          await this._writeSink(record, selected, options);
        }, {
          attempts: record.maxAttempts,
          baseMs: record.retryBaseMs,
          maxMs: Math.max(record.retryBaseMs, record.retryMaxMs),
          signal: options.signal,
          retryWhen: (error, attempt) => record.retryWhen ? record.retryWhen(error, attempt, record) : error?.retryable !== false,
        });
        this.stats.sinkWrites += selected.length;
      } catch (error) {
        this.stats.sinkErrors++;
        this.emit('sinkError', error, record.name, selected);
        if (record.required || this.errorPolicy === 'propagate') {
          this._recordFailedBatch(selected, error, record);
          throw error;
        }
      }
    }
    this.stats.maxBatchMs = Math.max(this.stats.maxBatchMs, performance.now() - started);
    this.emit('batch', batch.length);
  }

  async _writeSink(record, selected, options) {
    const sink = record.sink;
    if (typeof sink.export === 'function') return await sink.export(selected, options);
    if (typeof sink.writeMany === 'function') return await sink.writeMany(selected, options);
    if (typeof sink.write === 'function') {
      for (const item of selected) await sink.write(item, options);
      return { written: selected.length };
    }
    if (typeof sink === 'function') return await sink(selected, options);
    throw new TypeError(`Monitor pipeline sink '${record.name}' has no export/write method.`);
  }

  _recordFailedBatch(batch, error, record) {
    this.stats.failedBatches++;
    this.stats.failedSignals += batch.length;
    this.failureHistory.push(Object.freeze({
      at: Date.now(),
      sink: record.name,
      required: record.required,
      count: batch.length,
      signals: Object.freeze(batch.slice(0, 100).map((signal) => Object.freeze({
        id: signal?.id || null,
        kind: signal?.kind || null,
        traceId: signal?.traceId || signal?.context?.traceId || null,
        timestamp: signal?.timestamp || null,
      }))),
      truncated: batch.length > 100,
      error: errorRecord(error, { stack: false }),
    }));
    if (this.failureHistory.length > this.failureHistoryLimit) this.failureHistory.splice(0, this.failureHistory.length - this.failureHistoryLimit);
    this.emit('failedBatch', this.failureHistory.at(-1));
  }

  async flush(options = {}) {
    this.stats.flushes++;
    if (!this.running && !this.closed) {
      if (this.closing) this.running = true;
      else this.start();
    }
    const timeoutMs = Math.max(1, Number(options.timeoutMs) || 10_000);
    const deliveryFailures = [];
    await withTimeout(async () => {
      while (this.queue.length || this.inFlight.size) {
        if (this.queue.length) {
          try { await this.drain({ once: false, signal: options.signal }); }
          catch (error) { deliveryFailures.push(error); }
        }
        if (this.inFlight.size) {
          const settled = await Promise.allSettled([...this.inFlight]);
          for (const result of settled) if (result.status === 'rejected') deliveryFailures.push(result.reason);
        }
      }
      for (const record of this.sinks.values()) {
        try { await record.sink?.flush?.(options); }
        catch (error) {
          this.stats.sinkErrors++;
          this.emit('sinkError', error, record.name, []);
          if (record.required || this.errorPolicy === 'propagate') deliveryFailures.push(error);
        }
      }
    }, timeoutMs, { signal: options.signal, message: `Monitor pipeline flush exceeded ${timeoutMs}ms.` });
    if (deliveryFailures.length && (options.throwOnError === true || this.errorPolicy === 'propagate')) {
      const error = new AggregateError(deliveryFailures, 'Monitor pipeline flush completed with required delivery failures.');
      error.code = 'MONITOR_PIPELINE_FLUSH_FAILED';
      throw error;
    }
    return this.snapshot();
  }

  _pipelineError(error) {
    const record = errorRecord(error, { stack: true });
    if (this.listenerCount('error') > 0) this.emit('error', record);
    else this.emit('pipelineError', record);
  }

  async close(options = {}) {
    if (this.closed) return this.snapshot();
    this.closing = true;
    clearInterval(this.timer);
    this.timer = null;
    try { await this.flush(options); }
    catch (error) { if (options.throwOnError) throw error; }
    clearInterval(this.timer);
    this.timer = null;
    this.running = false;
    this.closed = true;
    this.queue.close();
    const failures = [];
    for (const record of Array.from(this.sinks.values()).reverse()) {
      try { await record.sink?.close?.(options); }
      catch (error) {
        this.stats.sinkErrors++;
        if (record.required || options.throwOnError || this.errorPolicy === 'propagate') failures.push(error);
      }
    }
    this.closing = false;
    if (failures.length && options.throwOnError) {
      const error = new AggregateError(failures, 'Monitor pipeline close completed with required sink failures.');
      error.code = 'MONITOR_PIPELINE_CLOSE_FAILED';
      throw error;
    }
    return this.snapshot();
  }

  snapshot() {
    return Object.freeze({
      running: this.running,
      closing: this.closing,
      closed: this.closed,
      scheduled: this.scheduled,
      inFlight: this.inFlight.size,
      queue: this.queue.snapshot(),
      sinks: Object.freeze(Array.from(this.sinks.values(), (entry) => ({
        name: entry.name,
        required: entry.required,
        signals: entry.signals ? [...entry.signals] : null,
        maxAttempts: entry.maxAttempts,
      }))),
      failures: Object.freeze([...this.failureHistory]),
      stats: Object.freeze({ ...this.stats }),
    });
  }
}

export default SignalPipeline;

FILE PATH: ./pipeline/BoundedSignalQueue.js

'use strict';

import { MonitorCapacityError } from '../errors.js';
import { bytesOf, boundedInteger } from '../support.js';

const EMPTY = Symbol('monitor.queue.empty');

export class BoundedSignalQueue {
  constructor(options = {}) {
    this.maxItems = boundedInteger(options.maxItems, 50_000, 1, 10_000_000);
    this.maxBytes = boundedInteger(options.maxBytes, 64 * 1024 * 1024, 1_024, 16 * 1024 * 1024 * 1024);
    this.maxSignalBytes = boundedInteger(options.maxSignalBytes, 2 * 1024 * 1024, 1, this.maxBytes);
    this.maxWaiters = boundedInteger(options.maxWaiters, 100_000, 1, 10_000_000);
    this.overflow = options.overflow || 'drop-oldest';
    this.sizeOf = options.sizeOf || ((value) => bytesOf(value, this.maxSignalBytes + 1));
    this.buffer = new Array(this.maxItems).fill(EMPTY);
    this.head = 0;
    this.tail = 0;
    this.length = 0;
    this.bytes = 0;
    this.waiters = [];
    this.closed = false;
    this.releasingWaiters = false;
    this.stats = {
      admitted: 0,
      shifted: 0,
      blocked: 0,
      waitTimeouts: 0,
      waiterLimit: 0,
      droppedNew: 0,
      droppedOldest: 0,
      rejected: 0,
      oversized: 0,
      highWaterItems: 0,
      highWaterBytes: 0,
      highWaterWaiters: 0,
    };
  }

  tryPush(value, options = {}) {
    if (this.closed) return this._reject('closed', 0, value, options);
    const bytes = options.bytes ?? this.sizeOf(value);
    if (!Number.isSafeInteger(bytes) || bytes < 0) throw new TypeError('Signal byte size must be a non-negative safe integer.');
    if (bytes > this.maxSignalBytes || bytes > this.maxBytes) {
      this.stats.oversized++;
      return this._reject('oversized', bytes, value, options);
    }

    while (this.length >= this.maxItems || this.bytes + bytes > this.maxBytes) {
      if (this.overflow === 'drop-oldest') {
        const dropped = this._shiftEntry({ releaseWaiters: false });
        if (!dropped) break;
        this.stats.droppedOldest++;
        options.onDrop?.(dropped.value, { reason: 'drop-oldest', bytes: dropped.bytes });
        continue;
      }
      if (this.overflow === 'reject') return this._reject('capacity', bytes, value, { ...options, throw: true });
      if (this.overflow === 'block') {
        this.stats.blocked++;
        return Object.freeze({ accepted: false, reason: 'would-block', bytes, queuedItems: this.length, queuedBytes: this.bytes, waiters: this.waiters.length });
      }
      this.stats.droppedNew++;
      return this._reject('capacity', bytes, value, options);
    }

    this.buffer[this.tail] = { value, bytes };
    this.tail = (this.tail + 1) % this.maxItems;
    this.length++;
    this.bytes += bytes;
    this.stats.admitted++;
    this.stats.highWaterItems = Math.max(this.stats.highWaterItems, this.length);
    this.stats.highWaterBytes = Math.max(this.stats.highWaterBytes, this.bytes);
    return Object.freeze({ accepted: true, bytes, queuedItems: this.length, queuedBytes: this.bytes });
  }

  async push(value, options = {}) {
    const bytes = options.bytes ?? this.sizeOf(value);
    const admitted = this.tryPush(value, { ...options, bytes });
    if (admitted.accepted || admitted.reason !== 'would-block') return admitted;
    if (this.waiters.length >= this.maxWaiters) {
      this.stats.waiterLimit++;
      throw new MonitorCapacityError('Monitor signal queue waiter limit exceeded.', {
        code: 'MONITOR_QUEUE_WAITER_LIMIT',
        details: { maxWaiters: this.maxWaiters },
      });
    }
    if (options.signal?.aborted) throw abortReason(options.signal);

    const timeoutMs = boundedInteger(options.timeoutMs, 5_000, 1, 300_000);
    return await new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      const waiter = { value, bytes, options: { ...options, bytes }, resolve, reject, timer: null, abort: null, settled: false };
      const cleanup = () => {
        clearTimeout(waiter.timer);
        if (waiter.abort) options.signal?.removeEventListener?.('abort', waiter.abort);
      };
      waiter.resolve = (result) => {
        if (waiter.settled) return;
        waiter.settled = true;
        cleanup();
        resolve(result);
      };
      waiter.reject = (error) => {
        if (waiter.settled) return;
        waiter.settled = true;
        cleanup();
        reject(error);
      };
      waiter.timer = setTimeout(() => {
        this.waiters = this.waiters.filter((item) => item !== waiter);
        this.stats.waitTimeouts++;
        waiter.reject(new MonitorCapacityError('Timed out waiting for Monitor signal queue capacity.', {
          code: 'MONITOR_QUEUE_WAIT_TIMEOUT',
          details: { timeoutMs },
        }));
      }, timeoutMs);
      waiter.timer.unref?.();
      if (options.signal) {
        waiter.abort = () => {
          this.waiters = this.waiters.filter((item) => item !== waiter);
          waiter.reject(abortReason(options.signal));
        };
        options.signal.addEventListener('abort', waiter.abort, { once: true });
      }
      this.waiters.push(waiter);
      this.stats.highWaterWaiters = Math.max(this.stats.highWaterWaiters, this.waiters.length);
    });
  }

  shift() { return this._shiftEntry()?.value; }

  shiftMany(limit = 1_000) {
    const output = [];
    while (output.length < limit) {
      const entry = this._shiftEntry({ releaseWaiters: false });
      if (!entry) break;
      output.push(entry.value);
    }
    this._releaseWaiters();
    return output;
  }

  _shiftEntry(options = {}) {
    if (!this.length) return null;
    const entry = this.buffer[this.head];
    this.buffer[this.head] = EMPTY;
    this.head = (this.head + 1) % this.maxItems;
    this.length--;
    this.bytes -= entry.bytes;
    this.stats.shifted++;
    if (options.releaseWaiters !== false) this._releaseWaiters();
    return entry;
  }

  _releaseWaiters() {
    if (this.releasingWaiters || this.closed) return;
    this.releasingWaiters = true;
    try {
      while (this.waiters.length) {
        const waiter = this.waiters[0];
        const result = this.tryPush(waiter.value, waiter.options);
        if (!result.accepted) {
          if (result.reason !== 'would-block') {
            this.waiters.shift();
            waiter.reject(new MonitorCapacityError(`Monitor queued signal rejected: ${result.reason}.`, { details: result }));
            continue;
          }
          break;
        }
        this.waiters.shift();
        waiter.resolve(result);
      }
    } finally {
      this.releasingWaiters = false;
    }
  }

  _reject(reason, bytes, value, options = {}) {
    this.stats.rejected++;
    const result = Object.freeze({ accepted: false, reason, bytes, queuedItems: this.length, queuedBytes: this.bytes });
    options.onDrop?.(value, result);
    if (options.throw === true) throw new MonitorCapacityError(`Monitor signal rejected: ${reason}.`, { details: result });
    return result;
  }

  clear(onDrop = null) {
    let dropped = 0;
    while (this.length) {
      const entry = this._shiftEntry({ releaseWaiters: false });
      dropped++;
      onDrop?.(entry.value, { reason: 'clear', bytes: entry.bytes });
    }
    this._releaseWaiters();
    return dropped;
  }

  close() {
    this.closed = true;
    for (const waiter of this.waiters.splice(0)) {
      waiter.reject(new MonitorCapacityError('Monitor signal queue closed.', { code: 'MONITOR_QUEUE_CLOSED' }));
    }
  }

  snapshot() {
    return Object.freeze({
      length: this.length,
      bytes: this.bytes,
      maxItems: this.maxItems,
      maxBytes: this.maxBytes,
      maxSignalBytes: this.maxSignalBytes,
      maxWaiters: this.maxWaiters,
      waiters: this.waiters.length,
      overflow: this.overflow,
      closed: this.closed,
      stats: Object.freeze({ ...this.stats }),
    });
  }
}

function abortReason(signal) {
  return signal?.reason instanceof Error
    ? signal.reason
    : Object.assign(new Error('Signal admission aborted.'), { code: 'ABORT_ERR' });
}

export default BoundedSignalQueue;

'use strict';
export * from './BoundedSignalQueue.js';
export * from './SignalPipeline.js';

'use strict';

import { renderMonitorStudioPage } from './MonitorStudioPage.js';
import { errorRecord } from '../support.js';

export class MonitorStudioController {
  constructor(options = {}) { this.studio = options.studio; this.assets = options.assets; this.prefix = options.prefix; this.maxBodyBytes = Math.max(1_024, Number(options.maxBodyBytes) || 1024 * 1024); }

  page = async (req, res) => {
    const auth = await this._authorize(req, res); if (!auth) return;
    return html(res, 200, renderMonitorStudioPage({ prefix: this.prefix, pollIntervalMs: this.studio.config.pollIntervalMs, service: this.studio.monitor.configuration.get('service'), environment: this.studio.monitor.configuration.get('environment'), tokenRequired: !!this.studio.authorizer.token }));
  };
  asset = async (req, res) => { const auth = await this._authorize(req, res); if (!auth) return; const record = await this.assets.read(param(req, 'asset')); if (!record) return json(res, 404, { ok: false, error: { code: 'MONITOR_ASSET_NOT_FOUND', message: 'Asset not found.' } }); return send(res, 200, record.body, record.type, { 'cache-control': this.studio.monitor.configuration.get('production') ? 'public, max-age=3600, immutable' : 'no-cache' }); };
  overview = this._get(() => this.studio.overview());
  metrics = this._get((req) => this.studio.metrics({ history: query(req).history === '1', limit: number(query(req).limit, 100) }));
  prometheus = async (req, res) => { const auth = await this._authorize(req, res); if (!auth) return; return send(res, 200, this.studio.monitor.prometheus(), 'text/plain; version=0.0.4; charset=utf-8', { 'cache-control': 'no-store' }); };
  traces = this._get((req) => this.studio.traces({ limit: number(query(req).limit, 100), traceId: query(req).traceId, status: query(req).status, minDurationMs: query(req).minDurationMs }));
  trace = this._get((req) => this.studio.trace(param(req, 'traceId')));
  signals = this._get((req) => this.studio.signals({ limit: number(query(req).limit, 100), kind: query(req).kind, traceId: query(req).traceId, since: query(req).since, until: query(req).until }));
  health = this._get((req) => this.studio.health({ fresh: query(req).fresh === '1' }));
  resources = this._get((req) => this.studio.resources(param(req, 'name') || null));
  alerts = this._get((req) => this.studio.alerts({ state: query(req).state, severity: query(req).severity, limit: number(query(req).limit, 100) }));
  acknowledgeAlert = this._mutate(async (req, auth) => ({ ok: this.studio.acknowledgeAlert(param(req, 'id'), actor(auth)) }));
  silence = this._mutate(async (req, auth) => this.studio.silence(await body(req, this.maxBodyBytes), actor(auth)));
  removeSilence = this._mutate(async (req) => ({ ok: this.studio.removeSilence(param(req, 'id')) }));
  slos = this._get((req) => this.studio.slos(param(req, 'id') || null));
  incidents = this._get((req) => this.studio.incidents({ state: query(req).state, severity: query(req).severity, limit: number(query(req).limit, 100) }));
  acknowledgeIncident = this._mutate(async (req, auth) => this.studio.acknowledgeIncident(param(req, 'id'), { ...(await body(req, this.maxBodyBytes)), actor: actor(auth) }));
  resolveIncident = this._mutate(async (req, auth) => this.studio.resolveIncident(param(req, 'id'), { ...(await body(req, this.maxBodyBytes)), actor: actor(auth) }));
  annotateIncident = this._mutate(async (req, auth) => this.studio.annotateIncident(param(req, 'id'), { ...(await body(req, this.maxBodyBytes)), actor: actor(auth) }));
  fleet = this._get(() => this.studio.fleet());
  deployments = this._get((req) => this.studio.deployments({ limit: number(query(req).limit, 100) }));
  anomalies = this._get((req) => this.studio.anomalies({ includeBaselines: query(req).baselines === '1', includeSeries: query(req).series === '1' }));
  synthetics = this._get((req) => this.studio.synthetics({ history: query(req).history === '1', limit: number(query(req).limit, 100) }));
  runSynthetic = this._mutate(async (req) => this.studio.runSynthetic(param(req, 'id'), await body(req, this.maxBodyBytes)));
  correlate = this._mutate(async (req) => { const input = await body(req, this.maxBodyBytes); return this.studio.correlate(input, input.options || {}); });
  diagnostics = this._get((req) => this.studio.diagnostics({ includeSignals: query(req).signals === '1', signalLimit: number(query(req).limit, 100), includeSyntheticHistory: query(req).synthetics === '1' }));
  doctor = this._get(() => this.studio.doctor());
  bundle = this._mutate(async (req) => this.studio.bundle(await body(req, this.maxBodyBytes)));
  stream = async (req, res) => { const auth = await this._authorize(req, res); if (!auth) return; return this.studio.stream(req, res); };
  ingest = async (req, res) => {
    if (!this.studio.monitor.configuration.get('security.allowRemoteIngest')) return json(res, 404, { ok: false, error: { code: 'MONITOR_INGEST_DISABLED', message: 'Remote ingestion is disabled.' } });
    try { const raw = await rawBody(req, this.maxBodyBytes); this.studio.monitor.authenticator.assert(raw, req.headers || {}); const parsed = JSON.parse(raw.toString('utf8')); return json(res, 202, { ok: true, ...(await this.studio.monitor.ingest(parsed, { remote: true })) }); }
    catch (error) { return failure(res, error); }
  };

  _get(handler) { return async (req, res) => { const auth = await this._authorize(req, res); if (!auth) return; try { return json(res, 200, { ok: true, data: await handler(req, auth) }); } catch (error) { return failure(res, error); } }; }
  _mutate(handler) { return async (req, res) => { const auth = await this._authorize(req, res, { mutate: true }); if (!auth) return; try { return json(res, 200, { ok: true, data: await handler(req, auth) }); } catch (error) { return failure(res, error); } }; }
  async _authorize(req, res, options = {}) { const result = await this.studio.authorize(req, options); if (result.ok) { secure(res); return result; } for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(result.headers || {})) setHeader(res, key, value); json(res, result.status || 403, { ok: false, error: { code: result.code, message: result.message } }); return null; }
}

function param(req, name) { return req.params?.[name] ?? req.param?.(name) ?? null; }
function query(req) { if (req.query && typeof req.query === 'object') return req.query; try { return Object.fromEntries(new URL(req.url || '/', 'http://rawnode.local').searchParams); } catch { return {}; } }
function actor(auth) { return auth?.actor?.id || auth?.actor?.userId || auth?.actor || null; }
function number(value, fallback) { const n = Number(value); return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : fallback; }
async function body(req, maxBytes) { if (req.body && typeof req.body === 'object') return req.body; const raw = await rawBody(req, maxBytes); if (!raw.length) return {}; return JSON.parse(raw.toString('utf8')); }
async function rawBody(req, maxBytes) { if (Buffer.isBuffer(req.rawBody)) return req.rawBody; const raw = req.raw || req; if (!raw?.[Symbol.asyncIterator]) return Buffer.alloc(0); const chunks = []; let bytes = 0; for await (const chunk of raw) { const value = Buffer.from(chunk); bytes += value.length; if (bytes > maxBytes) throw Object.assign(new Error('Monitor request body is too large.'), { code: 'MONITOR_BODY_TOO_LARGE', status: 413 }); chunks.push(value); } return Buffer.concat(chunks); }
function failure(res, error) { return json(res, Number(error?.status) || 500, { ok: false, error: errorRecord(error) }); }
function secure(res) { for (const [key, value] of Object.entries({ 'x-content-type-options': 'nosniff', 'x-frame-options': 'DENY', 'referrer-policy': 'no-referrer', 'permissions-policy': 'camera=(), microphone=(), geolocation=()', 'content-security-policy': "default-src 'self'; script-src 'self'; style-src 'self'; img-src 'self' data:; connect-src 'self'; frame-ancestors 'none'; base-uri 'none'; form-action 'self'", 'cache-control': 'no-store' })) setHeader(res, key, value); }
function setHeader(res, name, value) { try { (res.setHeader || res.raw?.setHeader)?.call(res.setHeader ? res : res.raw, name, value); } catch {} }
function json(res, status, value) { secure(res); if (typeof res.status === 'function') { const target = res.status(status); if (typeof target.json === 'function') return target.json(value); if (typeof target.send === 'function') return target.send(JSON.stringify(value)); } const raw = res.raw || res; raw.statusCode = status; raw.setHeader?.('content-type', 'application/json; charset=utf-8'); return raw.end?.(JSON.stringify(value)); }
function html(res, status, value) { return send(res, status, value, 'text/html; charset=utf-8'); }
function send(res, status, value, type, headers = {}) { secure(res); for (const [key, item] of Object.entries(headers)) setHeader(res, key, item); if (typeof res.status === 'function') { const target = res.status(status); target.type?.(type); if (typeof target.send === 'function') return target.send(value); } const raw = res.raw || res; raw.statusCode = status; raw.setHeader?.('content-type', type); return raw.end?.(value); }
export default MonitorStudioController;

'use strict';

import { EventEmitter } from 'node:events';
import BoundedSignalQueue from '../pipeline/BoundedSignalQueue.js';
import MonitorStudioAuthorizer from './MonitorStudioAuthorizer.js';
import MonitorStudioRateLimiter from './MonitorStudioRateLimiter.js';
import { bytesOf, cooperativeYield, deepFreeze, elapsedMs, errorRecord, nowNs } from '../support.js';

export class MonitorStudioManager extends EventEmitter {
  constructor(options = {}) {
    super();
    if (!options.monitor) throw new TypeError('MonitorStudioManager requires monitor.');
    this.monitor = options.monitor;
    this.config = { ...this.monitor.configuration.get('studio', {}), ...(options.config || {}) };
    this.authorizer = options.authorizer || new MonitorStudioAuthorizer({ ...this.config, authorize: options.authorize });
    this.limiter = options.limiter || new MonitorStudioRateLimiter(this.config.rateLimit || {});
    this.clients = new Set();
    this.closed = false;
    this.closing = false;
    const eventQueueBytes = Number(this.config.eventQueueBytes) || 2 * 1024 * 1024;
    const eventMaxBytes = Math.min(Number(this.config.eventMaxBytes) || 64 * 1024, eventQueueBytes);
    this.eventQueue = new BoundedSignalQueue({
      maxItems: Number(this.config.eventQueueItems) || 2_000,
      maxBytes: eventQueueBytes,
      maxSignalBytes: eventMaxBytes,
      overflow: 'drop-oldest',
      sizeOf: (value) => bytesOf(value, eventMaxBytes + 1),
    });
    this.eventBatchSize = Math.max(1, Number(this.config.eventBatchSize) || 100);
    this.eventBudgetMs = Math.max(0.25, Number(this.config.eventBudgetMs) || 4);
    this.eventDrainPromise = null;
    this.stats = { requests: 0, unauthorized: 0, rateLimited: 0, streams: 0, streamDrops: 0, streamEventDrops: 0, streamEventBatches: 0 };
    this._signalListener = (signal) => this._queueSignal(summarizeSignal(signal));
    this.monitor.on('signal', this._signalListener);
  }

  async authorize(req, options = {}) {
    const key = req.ip || req.socket?.remoteAddress || 'anonymous';
    const admission = this.limiter.consume(key, options.mutate ? 5 : 1);
    if (!admission.ok) { this.stats.rateLimited++; return { ok: false, status: 429, code: 'MONITOR_STUDIO_RATE_LIMIT', message: 'Monitor Studio rate limit exceeded.', headers: { 'retry-after': String(Math.ceil(admission.retryAfterMs / 1_000)) } }; }
    const result = await this.authorizer.check(req, options);
    this.stats.requests++; if (!result.ok) this.stats.unauthorized++; return result;
  }

  async overview(options = {}) {
    const runtime = this.monitor.snapshot({ includeRecent: false, limit: options.limit || 50 });
    return deepFreeze({
      generatedAt: Date.now(),
      identity: runtime.identity,
      state: runtime.state,
      lifecycle: runtime.lifecycle,
      health: runtime.health,
      pipeline: runtime.pipeline,
      system: this.monitor.system.snapshot().current,
      fleet: runtime.fleet,
      deployment: runtime.deployment,
      alertSummary: summarizeAlerts(runtime.alerts),
      incidentSummary: summarizeIncidents(runtime.incidents),
      sloSummary: runtime.slos,
      anomalySummary: { active: runtime.anomalies?.length || 0, leaks: runtime.leaks?.length || 0 },
      syntheticSummary: { checks: runtime.synthetics?.states?.length || 0, failing: runtime.synthetics?.states?.filter?.((item) => item.firing).length || 0 },
      pluginSummary: { total: runtime.plugins?.plugins?.length || 0, failed: runtime.plugins?.plugins?.filter?.((item) => item.state === 'failed').length || 0 },
      resourceSummary: summarizeResources(runtime.resources),
      stats: runtime.stats,
    });
  }

  metrics(options = {}) { return this.monitor.metricsSnapshot(options); }
  traces(options = {}) { return this.monitor.tracer.query({ ...options, limit: Math.min(this.config.maxQueryLimit || 1_000, Number(options.limit) || 100) }); }
  trace(traceId) { return this.monitor.tracer.traceById(traceId); }
  signals(options = {}) { return this.monitor.query.signals({ ...options, limit: Math.min(this.config.maxQueryLimit || 1_000, Number(options.limit) || 100) }); }
  health(options = {}) { return this.monitor.health(options); }
  resources(name = null) { return name ? this.monitor.resources.latestSnapshot(name) : this.monitor.resources.snapshot(); }
  alerts(options = {}) { return { current: this.monitor.alerts.current(options), history: this.monitor.alerts.historySnapshot({ limit: options.limit || 100 }), silences: this.monitor.alerts.silences.snapshot() }; }
  acknowledgeAlert(id, actor) { return this.monitor.alerts.acknowledge(id, actor); }
  silence(input, actor) { return this.monitor.alerts.silences.create({ ...input, createdBy: actor?.id || actor?.userId || actor || null }); }
  removeSilence(id) { return this.monitor.alerts.silences.remove(id); }
  slos(id = null) { return id ? this.monitor.slos.get(id) : this.monitor.slos.snapshot(); }
  incidents(options = {}) { return { incidents: this.monitor.incidents.list(options), history: this.monitor.incidents.historySnapshot({ limit: options.limit || 100 }) }; }
  acknowledgeIncident(id, input = {}) { return this.monitor.incidents.acknowledge(id, input); }
  resolveIncident(id, input = {}) { return this.monitor.incidents.resolve(id, input); }
  annotateIncident(id, input = {}) { return this.monitor.incidents.annotate(id, input); }
  fleet() { return this.monitor.fleet.snapshot(); }
  deployments(options = {}) { return this.monitor.deployments.list(options); }
  anomalies(options = {}) { return { anomalies: this.monitor.anomalies.snapshot(options), leaks: this.monitor.leaks.snapshot(options) }; }
  synthetics(options = {}) { return this.monitor.synthetics.snapshot({ includeHistory: options.history === true, limit: Math.min(this.config.maxQueryLimit || 1_000, Number(options.limit) || 100) }); }
  runSynthetic(id, options = {}) { return id ? this.monitor.synthetics.run(id, options) : this.monitor.synthetics.runAll(options); }
  correlate(input = {}, options = {}) { return this.monitor.correlate(input, { ...options, limit: Math.min(this.config.maxQueryLimit || 1_000, Number(options.limit) || 500) }); }
  diagnostics(options = {}) { return this.monitor.diagnostics(options); }
  bundle(options = {}) { return this.monitor.diagnosticBundle(options); }
  doctor(options = {}) { return this.monitor.doctor(options); }

  stream(req, res) {
    const raw = res.raw || res;
    raw.writeHead?.(200, { 'content-type': 'text/event-stream; charset=utf-8', 'cache-control': 'no-cache, no-store', connection: 'keep-alive', 'x-accel-buffering': 'no' });
    const client = {
      raw,
      bytes: 0,
      maxBytes: Number(this.config.streamMaxBytes) || 1024 * 1024,
      closed: false,
      heartbeat: null,
      close: null,
      drain: null,
    };
    client.drain = () => { client.bytes = 0; };
    client.close = () => this._removeClient(client);
    this.clients.add(client);
    this.stats.streams++;
    raw.on?.('drain', client.drain);
    raw.once?.('close', client.close);
    raw.once?.('error', client.close);
    this._writeClient(client, `event: ready\ndata: ${JSON.stringify({ timestamp: Date.now(), state: this.monitor.state })}\n\n`);
    client.heartbeat = setInterval(() => this._writeClient(client, ': heartbeat\n\n'), 15_000);
    client.heartbeat.unref?.();
    return client;
  }

  broadcast(event, data) {
    const text = `event: ${event}\ndata: ${JSON.stringify(data)}\n\n`;
    for (const client of this.clients) this._writeClient(client, text);
  }

  async drainEvents() {
    do {
      if (this.eventQueue.length && !this.eventDrainPromise) this._scheduleEventDrain();
      const active = this.eventDrainPromise;
      if (active) await active;
    } while (this.eventQueue.length || this.eventDrainPromise);
    return this.eventQueue.snapshot();
  }

  _queueSignal(signal) {
    if (this.closed || this.closing || !this.clients.size) return false;
    const result = this.eventQueue.tryPush(signal, { onDrop: () => { this.stats.streamEventDrops++; } });
    if (!result.accepted) return false;
    this._scheduleEventDrain();
    return true;
  }

  _scheduleEventDrain() {
    if (this.eventDrainPromise || this.closed) return;
    this.eventDrainPromise = new Promise((resolve) => queueMicrotask(resolve))
      .then(() => this._drainEventQueue())
      .catch((error) => { if (this.listenerCount('error') > 0) this.emit('error', error); else this.emit('streamError', errorRecord(error)); })
      .finally(() => {
        this.eventDrainPromise = null;
        if (this.eventQueue.length && !this.closed) this._scheduleEventDrain();
      });
  }

  async _drainEventQueue() {
    let sliceStarted = nowNs();
    while (!this.closed && this.eventQueue.length) {
      const batch = this.eventQueue.shiftMany(this.eventBatchSize);
      this.stats.streamEventBatches++;
      for (const signal of batch) this.broadcast('signal', signal);
      if (this.eventQueue.length && elapsedMs(sliceStarted) >= this.eventBudgetMs) {
        await cooperativeYield();
        sliceStarted = nowNs();
      }
    }
  }

  _writeClient(client, text) {
    if (client.closed) return false;
    const bytes = Buffer.byteLength(text);
    if (client.bytes + bytes > client.maxBytes) {
      this.stats.streamDrops++;
      this._removeClient(client);
      return false;
    }
    try {
      const accepted = client.raw.write?.(text);
      if (client.bytes > 0 || accepted === false) client.bytes += bytes;
      return accepted !== false;
    } catch {
      this._removeClient(client);
      return false;
    }
  }

  _removeClient(client) {
    if (client.closed) return;
    client.closed = true;
    clearInterval(client.heartbeat);
    client.heartbeat = null;
    this.clients.delete(client);
    client.raw.off?.('drain', client.drain);
    client.raw.off?.('close', client.close);
    client.raw.off?.('error', client.close);
    try { client.raw.end?.(); } catch {}
  }

  snapshot() { return deepFreeze({ closed: this.closed, closing: this.closing, clients: this.clients.size, eventQueue: this.eventQueue.snapshot(), limiter: this.limiter.snapshot(), stats: { ...this.stats } }); }
  async close() {
    if (this.closed || this.closing) return;
    this.closing = true;
    this.monitor.off?.('signal', this._signalListener);
    await this.drainEvents().catch(() => {});
    this.closed = true;
    this.closing = false;
    this.eventQueue.close();
    for (const client of [...this.clients]) this._removeClient(client);
  }
}
function summarizeSignal(signal) { return { id: signal.id, kind: signal.kind, timestamp: signal.timestamp, service: signal.service, instanceId: signal.instanceId, traceId: signal.traceId, priority: signal.priority }; }
function summarizeAlerts(rows = []) { return rows.reduce((out, row) => { out.total++; out[row.state] = (out[row.state] || 0) + 1; return out; }, { total: 0 }); }
function summarizeIncidents(rows = []) { return rows.reduce((out, row) => { out.total++; out[row.state] = (out[row.state] || 0) + 1; out[row.severity] = (out[row.severity] || 0) + 1; return out; }, { total: 0 }); }
function summarizeResources(resources = {}) { return Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(resources || {}).map(([name, value]) => [name, { ok: value?.health?.ok !== false && !value?.error, durationMs: value?.durationMs || null, error: value?.error ? errorRecord(value.error) : null }])); }
export default MonitorStudioManager;

'use strict';

import { timingSafeEqual } from 'node:crypto';
import { MonitorSecurityError } from '../errors.js';

export class MonitorStudioAuthorizer {
  constructor(options = {}) {
    this.token = options.token || null;
    this.localOnly = options.localOnly !== false;
    this.authorize = options.authorize || null;
    this.allowedOrigins = new Set(options.allowedOrigins || []);
    this.trustProxy = options.trustProxy === true;
    this.requireCsrf = options.requireCsrf !== false;
  }

  async check(req, options = {}) {
    const address = clientAddress(req, this.trustProxy);
    if (this.localOnly && !isLoopback(address)) return denied('MONITOR_STUDIO_LOCAL_ONLY', 403, 'Monitor Studio is restricted to local connections.');
    if (this.authorize) {
      const result = await this.authorize(req, options);
      if (result === true || result?.ok === true) return { ok: true, actor: result?.actor || req.user || null, method: 'custom' };
      return denied('MONITOR_STUDIO_FORBIDDEN', 403, 'Monitor Studio access is forbidden.');
    }
    if (this.token) {
      const presented = extractToken(req);
      if (!presented || !safeEqual(presented, this.token)) return denied('MONITOR_STUDIO_UNAUTHORIZED', 401, 'Monitor Studio authorization is required.');
    } else if (!this.localOnly) return denied('MONITOR_STUDIO_UNCONFIGURED', 503, 'Monitor Studio authorization is not configured.');
    if (options.mutate && this.requireCsrf && !sameOrigin(req, this.allowedOrigins)) return denied('MONITOR_STUDIO_CSRF', 403, 'Monitor Studio request failed origin validation.');
    return { ok: true, actor: req.user || req.auth?.payload || null, method: this.token ? 'token' : 'local' };
  }

  async assert(req, options = {}) { const result = await this.check(req, options); if (!result.ok) throw new MonitorSecurityError(result.message, { code: result.code, status: result.status }); return result; }
}
function denied(code, status, message) { return { ok: false, code, status, message }; }
function extractToken(req) { const authorization = req.headers?.authorization || req.headers?.Authorization || ''; const bearer = /^Bearer\s+(.+)$/i.exec(String(authorization))?.[1]; return bearer || req.headers?.['x-rawnode-monitor-token'] || req.headers?.['x-monitor-token'] || null; }
function safeEqual(left, right) { const a = Buffer.from(String(left)); const b = Buffer.from(String(right)); return a.length === b.length && timingSafeEqual(a, b); }
function clientAddress(req, trustProxy) { if (trustProxy) return String(req.headers?.['x-forwarded-for'] || '').split(',')[0].trim() || req.ip || req.socket?.remoteAddress || ''; return req.ip || req.socket?.remoteAddress || ''; }
function isLoopback(value) { const address = String(value || '').replace(/^::ffff:/, ''); return address === '127.0.0.1' || address === '::1' || address === 'localhost' || address.startsWith('127.'); }
function sameOrigin(req, allowedOrigins) {
  const fetchSite = String(req.headers?.['sec-fetch-site'] || '').toLowerCase();
  if (fetchSite === 'cross-site') return false;
  const origin = req.headers?.origin;
  if (!origin) return !fetchSite || ['same-origin', 'same-site', 'none'].includes(fetchSite);
  if (allowedOrigins.has(origin)) return true;
  const host = req.headers?.host;
  try { const parsed = new URL(origin); return parsed.host === host; }
  catch { return false; }
}
export default MonitorStudioAuthorizer;

'use strict';

import { readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
import path from 'node:path';

export class MonitorStudioAssets {
  constructor(options = {}) { this.root = path.resolve(options.root || process.cwd()); this.cache = new Map(); this.production = options.production === true; }
  async read(name) {
    const safe = String(name || '');
    const definitions = { 'studio.js': ['resources/assets/js/monitor/studio.js', 'application/javascript; charset=utf-8'], 'studio.css': ['resources/assets/css/monitor/studio.css', 'text/css; charset=utf-8'] };
    const definition = definitions[safe]; if (!definition) return null;
    if (this.cache.has(safe)) return this.cache.get(safe);
    const file = path.resolve(this.root, definition[0]); const relative = path.relative(this.root, file);
    if (relative.startsWith('..') || path.isAbsolute(relative)) throw new Error('Monitor Studio asset resolves outside the application root.');
    const record = Object.freeze({ name: safe, type: definition[1], body: await readFile(file, 'utf8') });
    if (this.production) this.cache.set(safe, record);
    return record;
  }
  clear() { this.cache.clear(); }
}
export default MonitorStudioAssets;

'use strict';

import MonitorStudioController from './MonitorStudioController.js';
import MonitorStudioAssets from './MonitorStudioAssets.js';

export function registerMonitorStudioRoutes(router, options = {}) {
  if (!router || typeof router.get !== 'function') throw new TypeError('registerMonitorStudioRoutes requires an HTTP router.');
  const studio = options.studio;
  if (!studio) throw new TypeError('registerMonitorStudioRoutes requires studio.');
  const prefix = normalize(options.prefix || studio.config.prefix || '/__rawnode/monitor');
  const assets = options.assets || new MonitorStudioAssets({ root: options.root || studio.monitor.configuration.get('root'), production: studio.monitor.configuration.get('production') });
  const controller = options.controller || new MonitorStudioController({ studio, assets, prefix, maxBodyBytes: options.maxBodyBytes });
  const group = typeof router.group === 'function' ? (callback) => router.group({ prefix, name: 'monitor.' }, callback) : (callback) => callback(prefixedRouter(router, prefix));
  group((route) => {
    route.get('/', controller.page);
    route.get('/assets/:asset', controller.asset);

    route.get('/api/metrics', controller.metrics);

    route.get('/api/resources', controller.resources);

    route.post('/api/incidents/:id/acknowledge', controller.acknowledgeIncident);
    route.post('/api/incidents/:id/resolve', controller.resolveIncident);

}
function normalize(value) { const text = String(value || '/__rawnode/monitor').trim(); const path = `/${text.replace(/^\/+|\/+$/g, '')}`; return path === '/' ? '/__rawnode/monitor' : path; }
function prefixedRouter(router, prefix) { return new Proxy(router, { get(target, property) { if (['get', 'post', 'put', 'patch', 'delete'].includes(String(property))) return (path, ...handlers) => target[property](`${prefix}${path === '/' ? '' : path}`, ...handlers); return Reflect.get(target, property); } }); }

FILE PATH: ./studio/MonitorStudioRateLimiter.js

'use strict';

/** Bounded per-client token bucket used by the Monitor Studio/API. */
export class MonitorStudioRateLimiter {
  constructor(options = {}) {
    this.capacity = Math.max(1, Number(options.capacity ?? options.max) || 300);
    this.windowMs = Math.max(1_000, Number(options.windowMs) || 60_000);
    this.refillPerSecond = Math.max(0.001, Number(options.refillPerSecond) || this.capacity / (this.windowMs / 1_000));
    this.maxKeys = Math.max(1, Number(options.maxKeys ?? options.maxClients) || 10_000);
    this.idleTtlMs = Math.max(this.windowMs, Number(options.idleTtlMs) || this.windowMs * 5);
    this.buckets = new Map();
    this.stats = { allowed: 0, rejected: 0, pruned: 0, evicted: 0 };
  }

  consume(key, cost = 1, now = Date.now()) {
    this.prune(now);
    const id = String(key || 'anonymous');
    const amount = Math.max(0, Number(cost) || 0);
    let bucket = this.buckets.get(id);
    if (!bucket) bucket = { tokens: this.capacity, updatedAt: now, lastSeenAt: now };
    this._refill(bucket, now);
    bucket.lastSeenAt = now;

    if (amount > bucket.tokens) {
      const deficit = amount - bucket.tokens;
      const retryAfterMs = Math.ceil((deficit / this.refillPerSecond) * 1_000);
      this.stats.rejected++;
      this.buckets.set(id, bucket);
      return Object.freeze({ ok: false, remaining: Math.floor(bucket.tokens), retryAfterMs, resetAt: now + retryAfterMs });
    }

    bucket.tokens -= amount;
    this.buckets.set(id, bucket);
    this.stats.allowed++;
    if (this.buckets.size > this.maxKeys) this._evictOldest();
    return Object.freeze({ ok: true, remaining: Math.floor(bucket.tokens), resetAt: now + Math.ceil(((this.capacity - bucket.tokens) / this.refillPerSecond) * 1_000) });
  }

  _refill(bucket, now) {
    const elapsedSeconds = Math.max(0, now - bucket.updatedAt) / 1_000;
    bucket.tokens = Math.min(this.capacity, bucket.tokens + elapsedSeconds * this.refillPerSecond);
    bucket.updatedAt = now;
  }

  _evictOldest() {
    let oldestKey = null;
    let oldest = Infinity;
    for (const [key, bucket] of this.buckets) {
      if (bucket.lastSeenAt < oldest) { oldest = bucket.lastSeenAt; oldestKey = key; }
    }
    if (oldestKey != null) { this.buckets.delete(oldestKey); this.stats.evicted++; }
  }

  prune(now = Date.now()) {
    let removed = 0;
    for (const [key, bucket] of this.buckets) {
      if (now - bucket.lastSeenAt >= this.idleTtlMs) { this.buckets.delete(key); removed++; }
    }
    this.stats.pruned += removed;
    return removed;
  }

  snapshot() {
    return Object.freeze({
      capacity: this.capacity,
      refillPerSecond: this.refillPerSecond,
      maxKeys: this.maxKeys,
      keys: this.buckets.size,
      stats: Object.freeze({ ...this.stats }),
    });
  }
}
export default MonitorStudioRateLimiter;

FILE PATH: ./tracing/TraceContext.js
