'use strict';

import { EventEmitter } from 'node:events';
import { MonitorState } from '../constants.js';

const ALLOWED = Object.freeze({
  [MonitorState.IDLE]: new Set([MonitorState.STARTING, MonitorState.STOPPED]),
  [MonitorState.STARTING]: new Set([MonitorState.RUNNING, MonitorState.FAILED, MonitorState.STOPPING]),
  [MonitorState.RUNNING]: new Set([MonitorState.DRAINING, MonitorState.STOPPING, MonitorState.FAILED]),
  [MonitorState.DRAINING]: new Set([MonitorState.RUNNING, MonitorState.STOPPING, MonitorState.FAILED]),
  [MonitorState.STOPPING]: new Set([MonitorState.STOPPED, MonitorState.FAILED]),
  [MonitorState.STOPPED]: new Set([MonitorState.STARTING]),
  [MonitorState.FAILED]: new Set([MonitorState.STARTING, MonitorState.STOPPING, MonitorState.STOPPED]),
});

export class MonitorLifecycle extends EventEmitter {
  constructor() {
    super();
    this.state = MonitorState.IDLE;
    this.startedAt = 0;
    this.stoppedAt = 0;
    this.failure = null;
    this.transitions = [];
  }

  transition(next, meta = null) {
    if (next === this.state) return this.snapshot();
    if (!ALLOWED[this.state]?.has(next)) throw new Error(`Invalid Monitor lifecycle transition ${this.state} -> ${next}.`);
    const previous = this.state;
    this.state = next;
    const at = Date.now();
    if (next === MonitorState.RUNNING && !this.startedAt) this.startedAt = at;
    if (next === MonitorState.STOPPED) this.stoppedAt = at;
    if (next === MonitorState.FAILED) this.failure = meta?.error || meta || null;
    this.transitions.push(Object.freeze({ previous, next, at, meta }));
    if (this.transitions.length > 128) this.transitions.shift();
    this.emit('transition', { previous, next, at, meta });
    this.emit(next, { previous, at, meta });
    return this.snapshot();
  }

  snapshot() {
    return Object.freeze({
      state: this.state,
      startedAt: this.startedAt || null,
      stoppedAt: this.stoppedAt || null,
      uptimeMs: this.startedAt ? Math.max(0, Date.now() - this.startedAt) : 0,
      failure: this.failure ? { name: this.failure.name, code: this.failure.code, message: this.failure.message || String(this.failure) } : null,
      transitions: Object.freeze(this.transitions.slice()),
    });
  }
}

export default MonitorLifecycle;

'use strict';

import { MONITOR_SCHEMA_VERSION, MONITOR_VERSION, SignalKind } from '../constants.js';
import { deepFreeze, randomId } from '../support.js';

const SIGNALS = new Set(Object.values(SignalKind));

export class TelemetryEnvelope {
  constructor(kind, payload, options = {}) {
    const normalized = String(kind || '').toLowerCase();
    if (!SIGNALS.has(normalized)) throw new TypeError(`Unsupported telemetry signal kind '${kind}'.`);
    this.schemaVersion = MONITOR_SCHEMA_VERSION;
    this.monitorVersion = MONITOR_VERSION;
    this.id = options.id || randomId('sig');
    this.kind = normalized;
    this.timestamp = Number(options.timestamp || Date.now());
    this.observedAt = Number(options.observedAt || Date.now());
    this.service = options.service || null;
    this.environment = options.environment || null;
    this.instanceId = options.instanceId || null;
    this.region = options.region || null;
    this.zone = options.zone || null;
    this.tenantId = options.tenantId || options.context?.tenantId || null;
    this.traceId = options.traceId || options.context?.traceId || payload?.traceId || null;
    this.spanId = options.spanId || options.context?.spanId || payload?.spanId || null;
    this.correlationId = options.correlationId || options.context?.correlationId || null;
    this.context = options.context ? deepFreeze({ ...options.context }) : null;
    this.resource = options.resource ? deepFreeze({ ...options.resource }) : null;
    this.attributes = options.attributes ? deepFreeze({ ...options.attributes }) : null;
    this.payload = payload;
    this.sequence = options.sequence ?? null;
    this.priority = Number(options.priority || 0);
    Object.freeze(this);
  }

  toJSON() { return { ...this }; }
}

export function createTelemetryEnvelope(kind, payload, options = {}) {
  return payload instanceof TelemetryEnvelope ? payload : new TelemetryEnvelope(kind, payload, options);
}

export default TelemetryEnvelope;

'use strict';

import { AsyncLocalStorage, AsyncResource } from 'node:async_hooks';
import { randomId } from '../support.js';

const EMPTY = Object.freeze(Object.create(null));

export class MonitorContextManager {
  constructor(options = {}) {
    this.enabled = options.enabled !== false;
    this.storage = options.storage || new AsyncLocalStorage();
    this.base = Object.freeze({ ...(options.base || {}) });
  }

  active() { return this.enabled ? (this.storage.getStore() || this.base || EMPTY) : this.base || EMPTY; }

  merge(values = {}) {
    const output = Object.create(null);
    Object.assign(output, this.base, this.active(), values || {});
    for (const key of Object.keys(output)) if (output[key] === undefined || output[key] === null || output[key] === '') delete output[key];
    return Object.freeze(output);
  }

  run(context, callback, ...args) {
    if (typeof callback !== 'function') throw new TypeError('MonitorContextManager.run() requires a callback.');
    if (!this.enabled) return callback(...args);
    return this.storage.run(this.merge(context), callback, ...args);
  }

  enter(context) { if (this.enabled) this.storage.enterWith(this.merge(context)); return this.active(); }

  bind(callback, context = this.active()) {
    if (typeof callback !== 'function') throw new TypeError('MonitorContextManager.bind() requires a callback.');
    const resource = new AsyncResource('RawNodeMonitorContext');
    const manager = this;
    return function boundMonitorContext(...args) {
      return manager.run(context, () => resource.runInAsyncScope(callback, this, ...args));
    };
  }

  createRequestContext(req = {}, extra = {}) {
    const headers = req.headers || {};
    const user = req.user || req.auth || null;
    return this.merge({
      requestId: req.requestId || req.id || headers['x-request-id'] || randomId('req'),
      correlationId: req.correlationId || headers['x-correlation-id'] || null,
      traceId: req.traceId || headers['x-trace-id'] || null,
      spanId: req.spanId || headers['x-span-id'] || null,
      tenantId: req.tenantId || req.tenant?.id || headers['x-tenant-id'] || null,
      userId: user?.id || user?._id || user?.sub || null,
      method: req.method || null,
      path: (typeof req.path === 'function' ? req.path() : req.path) || req.url || null,
      route: req.route?.name || req.route?.path || req.routeName || null,
      protocol: req.protocol || (req.socket?.encrypted ? 'https' : 'http'),
      remoteAddress: req.ip || req.socket?.remoteAddress || null,
      ...extra,
    });
  }

  createJobContext(job = {}, extra = {}) {
    return this.merge({
      jobId: job.id || job.jobId || randomId('job'),
      queue: job.queue || job.queueName || null,
      attempt: job.attempt || job.attempts || null,
      maxAttempts: job.maxAttempts || job.tries || null,
      tenantId: job.tenantId || job.tenant_id || null,
      traceId: job.traceId || job.trace_id || null,
      correlationId: job.correlationId || job.correlation_id || null,
      ...extra,
    });
  }

  disable() { this.enabled = false; this.storage.disable(); }
}

export const globalMonitorContext = new MonitorContextManager();
export default MonitorContextManager;

'use strict';
export * from './MonitorContextManager.js';
export * from './TelemetryEnvelope.js';

================================================
FILE PATH: ./analysis/LeakDetector.js
================================================

'use strict';

import { EventEmitter } from 'node:events';
import { SignalKind } from '../constants.js';
import { boundedInteger, deepFreeze, labelsKey, normalizeLabels, randomId } from '../support.js';

/** Bounded trend detector for monotonic memory/resource leaks. */
export class LeakDetector extends EventEmitter {
  constructor(options = {}) {
    super();
    this.enabled = options.enabled !== false;
    this.signal = options.signal || null;
    this.maxSeries = boundedInteger(options.maxSeries, 2_000, 1, 100_000);
    this.maxSamples = boundedInteger(options.maxSamples, 120, 4, 10_000);
    this.rules = new Map();
    this.series = new Map();
    this.states = new Map();
    this.frozen = false;
    this.stats = { observations: 0, evaluations: 0, leaks: 0, recoveries: 0, seriesRejected: 0 };
  }

  register(input = {}, options = {}) {
    if (this.frozen) throw new Error('Leak detector definitions are frozen.');
    const rule = normalizeRule(input, this.maxSamples);
    if (this.rules.has(rule.id) && options.replace !== true) throw new Error(`Leak rule '${rule.id}' is already registered.`);
    this.rules.set(rule.id, rule);
    return rule;
  }

  observe(metric, value, labels = {}, timestamp = Date.now(), context = {}) {
    if (!this.enabled || !Number.isFinite(Number(value))) return [];
    this.stats.observations++;
    const output = [];
    for (const rule of this.rules.values()) {
      if (!match(rule.metric, metric) || !labelsMatch(rule.labels, labels)) continue;
      const normalized = normalizeLabels(labels, { maxLabels: 32, maxValueBytes: 256 });
      const key = `${rule.id}\0${labelsKey(normalized)}`;
      let samples = this.series.get(key);
      if (!samples) {
        if (this.series.size >= this.maxSeries) { this.stats.seriesRejected++; continue; }
        samples = [];
        this.series.set(key, samples);
      }
      samples.push({ value: Number(value), timestamp: Number(timestamp) || Date.now() });
      if (samples.length > rule.windowSamples) samples.splice(0, samples.length - rule.windowSamples);
      if (samples.length < rule.minSamples) continue;
      const analysis = regression(samples);
      this.stats.evaluations++;
      const growth = samples[0].value === 0 ? (samples.at(-1).value > 0 ? Infinity : 0) : (samples.at(-1).value - samples[0].value) / Math.abs(samples[0].value);
      const firing = analysis.slopePerMinute >= rule.minSlopePerMinute && growth >= rule.minGrowthRatio && analysis.r2 >= rule.minR2;
      const previous = this.states.get(key) || { firing: false, lastEmittedAt: 0 };
      const now = Date.now();
      if (firing && (!previous.firing || now - previous.lastEmittedAt >= rule.cooldownMs)) {
        const record = deepFreeze({ id: randomId('leak'), ruleId: rule.id, name: rule.name, metric: String(metric), labels: normalized, state: 'firing', severity: rule.severity, analysis: { ...analysis, growthRatio: growth, samples: samples.length }, timestamp: now, context, runbook: rule.runbook, tags: rule.tags });
        previous.firing = true; previous.lastEmittedAt = now; previous.last = record;
        this.stats.leaks++; this.emit('leak', record); this.signal?.(SignalKind.ANOMALY, { type: 'leak', ...record }, { timestamp: now, priority: rule.severity === 'critical' ? 20 : 10, context }); output.push(record);
      } else if (!firing && previous.firing) {
        const record = deepFreeze({ id: randomId('leak'), ruleId: rule.id, name: rule.name, metric: String(metric), labels: normalized, state: 'recovered', severity: rule.severity, analysis: { ...analysis, growthRatio: growth, samples: samples.length }, timestamp: now, context, runbook: rule.runbook, tags: rule.tags });
        previous.firing = false; previous.last = record; this.stats.recoveries++; this.emit('recovery', record); this.signal?.(SignalKind.ANOMALY, { type: 'leak', ...record }, { timestamp: now, priority: 2, context }); output.push(record);
      }
      this.states.set(key, previous);
    }
    return output;
  }

  observeSnapshot(snapshot = {}, context = {}) {
    const output = [];
    for (const [metric, definition] of Object.entries(snapshot.metrics || {})) {
      for (const series of definition.series || []) {
        const value = ['histogram', 'timer'].includes(definition.type) ? series.mean : series.value;
        output.push(...this.observe(metric, value, series.labels, snapshot.timestamp, context));
      }
    }
    return output;
  }


  alertRule(id, options = {}) {
    const rule = this.rules.get(String(id));
    if (!rule) return null;
    return {
      id: options.id || `leak:${rule.id}`,
      name: options.name || `Leak trend: ${rule.name}`,
      severity: options.severity || rule.severity,
      source: 'custom',
      incident: options.incident ?? rule.incident,
      runbook: options.runbook || rule.runbook,
      tags: [...rule.tags, 'leak'],
      evaluate: () => {
        const firing = this.current().filter((item) => item.key.startsWith(`${rule.id}\0`));
        return { firing: firing.length > 0, value: firing.length, details: { series: firing.slice(0, 100) } };
      },
    };
  }

  current() { return deepFreeze(Array.from(this.states, ([key, state]) => ({ key, ...state })).filter((item) => item.firing)); }
  freeze() { this.frozen = true; return this; }
  close() { this.removeAllListeners(); this.rules.clear(); this.series.clear(); this.states.clear(); }
  snapshot(options = {}) { return deepFreeze({ enabled: this.enabled, frozen: this.frozen, rules: Array.from(this.rules.values()), active: this.current(), series: options.includeSeries ? Array.from(this.series, ([key, samples]) => ({ key, samples: [...samples] })) : undefined, stats: { ...this.stats } }); }
}

function normalizeRule(input, maxSamples) {
  const id = String(input.id || input.name || '').trim(); if (!id) throw new TypeError('Leak rules require an id.');
  return deepFreeze({ id, name: String(input.name || id), metric: input.metric instanceof RegExp ? input.metric : String(input.metric || '*'), labels: normalizeLabels(input.labels || {}, { maxLabels: 32, maxValueBytes: 256 }), windowSamples: boundedInteger(input.windowSamples, maxSamples, 4, 10_000), minSamples: boundedInteger(input.minSamples, 20, 4, 10_000), minSlopePerMinute: Math.max(0, Number(input.minSlopePerMinute || 1)), minGrowthRatio: Math.max(0, Number(input.minGrowthRatio ?? 0.1)), minR2: Math.max(0, Math.min(1, Number(input.minR2 ?? 0.8))), cooldownMs: boundedInteger(input.cooldownMs, 10 * 60_000, 0, 24 * 60 * 60_000), severity: String(input.severity || 'warning'), incident: input.incident === true, alert: input.alert !== false, runbook: input.runbook || null, tags: Object.freeze(Array.from(new Set((input.tags || []).map(String)))) });
}
function regression(samples) { const t0 = samples[0].timestamp; const points = samples.map((sample) => ({ x: (sample.timestamp - t0) / 60_000, y: sample.value })); const n = points.length; const meanX = points.reduce((sum, point) => sum + point.x, 0) / n; const meanY = points.reduce((sum, point) => sum + point.y, 0) / n; let sxx = 0; let sxy = 0; let syy = 0; for (const point of points) { const dx = point.x - meanX; const dy = point.y - meanY; sxx += dx * dx; sxy += dx * dy; syy += dy * dy; } const slope = sxx > 0 ? sxy / sxx : 0; const intercept = meanY - slope * meanX; const r2 = sxx > 0 && syy > 0 ? Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (sxy * sxy) / (sxx * syy))) : 0; return { slopePerMinute: slope, intercept, r2, durationMinutes: points.at(-1).x, startValue: samples[0].value, endValue: samples.at(-1).value }; }
function match(pattern, value) { if (pattern instanceof RegExp) return pattern.test(value); if (pattern === '*') return true; if (!String(pattern).includes('*')) return String(pattern) === String(value); const escaped = String(pattern).replace(/[.+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&').replace(/\*/g, '.*'); return new RegExp(`^${escaped}$`).test(value); }
function labelsMatch(expected, actual) { for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(expected || {})) if (String(actual?.[key] ?? '') !== String(value)) return false; return true; }
export default LeakDetector;

'use strict';
export * from './StreamingBaseline.js';
export * from './AnomalyDetector.js';
export * from './LeakDetector.js';
export * from './CorrelationEngine.js';

FILE PATH: ./analysis/StreamingBaseline.js

'use strict';

import { boundedInteger, boundedNumber, deepFreeze } from '../support.js';

/**
 * Bounded streaming baseline with EWMA and robust median/MAD statistics.
 * The ring never grows after construction and all updates are O(1); robust
 * statistics are calculated only when an analysis or snapshot is requested.
 */
export class StreamingBaseline {
  constructor(options = {}) {
    this.maxSamples = boundedInteger(options.maxSamples, 256, 4, 100_000);
    this.minSamples = boundedInteger(options.minSamples, 20, 2, this.maxSamples);
    this.alpha = boundedNumber(options.alpha, 0.2, 0.001, 1);
    this.epsilon = boundedNumber(options.epsilon, 1e-9, Number.MIN_VALUE, 1);
    this.values = new Float64Array(this.maxSamples);
    this.timestamps = new Float64Array(this.maxSamples);
    this.length = 0;
    this.cursor = 0;
    this.total = 0;
    this.ewma = null;
    this.ewVariance = 0;
    this.min = Infinity;
    this.max = -Infinity;
    this.last = null;
  }

  observe(input, timestamp = Date.now()) {
    const value = Number(input);
    if (!Number.isFinite(value)) return false;
    if (this.ewma == null) this.ewma = value;
    else {
      const delta = value - this.ewma;
      this.ewma += this.alpha * delta;
      this.ewVariance = (1 - this.alpha) * (this.ewVariance + this.alpha * delta * delta);
    }
    this.values[this.cursor] = value;
    this.timestamps[this.cursor] = Number(timestamp) || Date.now();
    this.cursor = (this.cursor + 1) % this.maxSamples;
    this.length = Math.min(this.maxSamples, this.length + 1);
    this.total++;
    this.min = Math.min(this.min, value);
    this.max = Math.max(this.max, value);
    this.last = value;
    return true;
  }

  analyze(input, options = {}) {
    const value = Number(input);
    if (!Number.isFinite(value)) return deepFreeze({ valid: false, warm: false, value: null });
    const stats = this.statistics();
    const method = String(options.method || 'mad').toLowerCase();
    const center = method === 'zscore' ? stats.ewma : stats.median;
    const spread = method === 'zscore' ? stats.ewStddev : (stats.robustStddev || stats.stddev);
    const delta = value - (Number.isFinite(center) ? center : value);
    const zScore = delta / Math.max(Math.abs(spread || 0), this.epsilon);
    const relativeDeviation = Math.abs(delta) / Math.max(Math.abs(center || 0), this.epsilon);
    return deepFreeze({
      valid: true,
      warm: this.length >= (options.minSamples || this.minSamples),
      value,
      method,
      center,
      spread,
      delta,
      zScore,
      absoluteScore: Math.abs(zScore),
      relativeDeviation,
      samples: this.length,
      totalSamples: this.total,
      statistics: stats,
    });
  }

  observeAndAnalyze(input, options = {}) {
    const analysis = this.analyze(input, options);
    this.observe(input, options.timestamp);
    return analysis;
  }

  samples() {
    const output = [];
    const start = this.length < this.maxSamples ? 0 : this.cursor;
    for (let index = 0; index < this.length; index++) {
      const position = (start + index) % this.maxSamples;
      output.push({ value: this.values[position], timestamp: this.timestamps[position] });
    }
    return output;
  }

  statistics() {
    if (!this.length) return deepFreeze({ count: 0, mean: null, stddev: 0, median: null, mad: 0, robustStddev: 0, ewma: null, ewStddev: 0, min: null, max: null, last: null });
    const values = this.samples().map((item) => item.value).sort((a, b) => a - b);
    const mean = values.reduce((sum, value) => sum + value, 0) / values.length;
    const variance = values.length > 1 ? values.reduce((sum, value) => sum + (value - mean) ** 2, 0) / (values.length - 1) : 0;
    const median = quantile(values, 0.5);
    const deviations = values.map((value) => Math.abs(value - median)).sort((a, b) => a - b);
    const mad = quantile(deviations, 0.5);
    return deepFreeze({
      count: values.length,
      mean,
      stddev: Math.sqrt(Math.max(0, variance)),
      median,
      mad,
      robustStddev: mad * 1.4826,
      ewma: this.ewma,
      ewStddev: Math.sqrt(Math.max(0, this.ewVariance)),
      min: values[0],
      max: values.at(-1),
      last: this.last,
    });
  }

  reset() {
    this.values.fill(0);
    this.timestamps.fill(0);
    this.length = 0;
    this.cursor = 0;
    this.total = 0;
    this.ewma = null;
    this.ewVariance = 0;
    this.min = Infinity;
    this.max = -Infinity;
    this.last = null;
    return this;
  }

  snapshot(options = {}) {
    return deepFreeze({
      maxSamples: this.maxSamples,
      minSamples: this.minSamples,
      alpha: this.alpha,
      statistics: this.statistics(),
      samples: options.includeSamples === true ? this.samples() : undefined,
    });
  }
}

function quantile(sorted, value) {
  if (!sorted.length) return null;
  const position = (sorted.length - 1) * value;
  const lower = Math.floor(position);
  const upper = Math.ceil(position);
  if (lower === upper) return sorted[lower];
  return sorted[lower] + (sorted[upper] - sorted[lower]) * (position - lower);
}

export default StreamingBaseline;

FILE PATH: ./analysis/CorrelationEngine.js

'use strict';

import { deepFreeze, safeGet } from '../support.js';

const DEFAULT_KINDS = Object.freeze(['span', 'log', 'event', 'health', 'alert', 'incident', 'slo', 'resource', 'deployment', 'anomaly', 'synthetic']);

/** Builds a ranked incident/request timeline across otherwise separate signals. */
export class CorrelationEngine {
  constructor(options = {}) {
    this.store = options.store || null;
    this.tracer = options.tracer || null;
    this.deployments = options.deployments || null;
    this.maxKinds = Math.max(1, Number(options.maxKinds) || 32);
    this.maxCandidates = Math.max(10, Number(options.maxCandidates) || 10_000);
    this.windowMs = Math.max(1_000, Number(options.windowMs) || 15 * 60_000);
    this.stats = { correlations: 0, candidates: 0, returned: 0, errors: 0 };
  }

  async correlate(input = {}, options = {}) {
    const timestamp = Number(input.timestamp || input.at || input.createdAt || Date.now());
    const windowMs = Math.max(1_000, Number(options.windowMs || input.windowMs || this.windowMs));
    const since = timestamp - windowMs;
    const until = timestamp + windowMs;
    const traceId = input.traceId || safeGet(input, 'context.traceId') || safeGet(input, 'payload.traceId');
    const requestId = input.requestId || safeGet(input, 'context.requestId') || safeGet(input, 'payload.context.requestId');
    const tenantId = input.tenantId || safeGet(input, 'context.tenantId') || safeGet(input, 'payload.tenantId');
    const service = input.service || safeGet(input, 'context.service');
    const kinds = Array.from(new Set(options.kinds || DEFAULT_KINDS)).slice(0, this.maxKinds);
    const perKind = Math.max(1, Math.min(Number(options.perKindLimit) || 250, Math.floor(this.maxCandidates / Math.max(1, kinds.length))));
    const candidates = [];
    this.stats.correlations++;

    if (traceId && this.tracer?.trace) {
      const trace = this.tracer.trace(traceId);
      if (trace) for (const span of trace) candidates.push({ kind: 'span', timestamp: span.startTimeUnixNano ? Number(BigInt(span.startTimeUnixNano) / 1_000_000n) : timestamp, payload: span, traceId });
    }

    if (this.store?.query) {
      const results = await Promise.all(kinds.map(async (kind) => {
        try { return await this.store.query({ kind, traceId, since, until, limit: perKind }); }
        catch { this.stats.errors++; return { items: [] }; }
      }));
      for (const result of results) candidates.push(...(result?.items || []));
    }

    const scored = [];
    const seen = new Set();
    for (const candidate of candidates.slice(0, this.maxCandidates)) {
      const id = candidate.id || `${candidate.kind}:${candidate.timestamp}:${JSON.stringify(candidate.payload || {}).slice(0, 200)}`;
      if (seen.has(id)) continue;
      seen.add(id);
      const score = correlationScore(candidate, { timestamp, windowMs, traceId, requestId, tenantId, service });
      if (score.score <= 0 && options.includeUnmatched !== true) continue;
      scored.push({ ...candidate, correlation: score });
    }
    this.stats.candidates += candidates.length;
    scored.sort((left, right) => right.correlation.score - left.correlation.score || Number(left.timestamp || 0) - Number(right.timestamp || 0));
    const limit = Math.max(1, Number(options.limit) || 500);
    const items = scored.slice(0, limit);
    this.stats.returned += items.length;
    const deployment = this.deployments?.around?.(timestamp, { windowMs }) || this.deployments?.latest?.() || null;
    return deepFreeze({ timestamp, windowMs, query: { traceId: traceId || null, requestId: requestId || null, tenantId: tenantId || null, service: service || null }, deployment, totalCandidates: candidates.length, matched: scored.length, items });
  }

  snapshot() { return deepFreeze({ windowMs: this.windowMs, maxKinds: this.maxKinds, maxCandidates: this.maxCandidates, stats: { ...this.stats } }); }
}

function correlationScore(candidate, input) {
  let score = 0; const reasons = [];
  const traceId = candidate.traceId || safeGet(candidate, 'context.traceId') || safeGet(candidate, 'payload.traceId') || safeGet(candidate, 'payload.context.traceId');
  const requestId = candidate.requestId || safeGet(candidate, 'context.requestId') || safeGet(candidate, 'payload.requestId') || safeGet(candidate, 'payload.context.requestId');
  const tenantId = candidate.tenantId || safeGet(candidate, 'context.tenantId') || safeGet(candidate, 'payload.tenantId') || safeGet(candidate, 'payload.context.tenantId');
  const service = candidate.service || safeGet(candidate, 'context.service');
  if (input.traceId && String(traceId) === String(input.traceId)) { score += 100; reasons.push('trace'); }
  if (input.requestId && String(requestId) === String(input.requestId)) { score += 80; reasons.push('request'); }
  if (input.tenantId && String(tenantId) === String(input.tenantId)) { score += 30; reasons.push('tenant'); }
  if (input.service && String(service) === String(input.service)) { score += 10; reasons.push('service'); }
  const distance = Math.abs(Number(candidate.timestamp || input.timestamp) - input.timestamp);
  if (distance <= input.windowMs) { const temporal = Math.max(0, 20 * (1 - distance / input.windowMs)); score += temporal; reasons.push('time'); }
  if (candidate.kind === 'deployment') { score += 15; reasons.push('deployment'); }
  if (['alert', 'incident', 'anomaly'].includes(candidate.kind)) { score += 10; reasons.push('operational-signal'); }
  return deepFreeze({ score: Number(score.toFixed(6)), reasons: Object.freeze(reasons), distanceMs: distance });
}
export default CorrelationEngine;

FILE PATH: ./analysis/AnomalyDetector.js
================================================

'use strict';

import { EventEmitter } from 'node:events';
import { SignalKind } from '../constants.js';
import StreamingBaseline from './StreamingBaseline.js';
import { boundedInteger, boundedNumber, deepFreeze, labelsKey, normalizeLabels, randomId } from '../support.js';

const METHODS = new Set(['mad', 'zscore', 'relative', 'absolute']);
const DIRECTIONS = new Set(['both', 'high', 'low']);

/** Adaptive, bounded anomaly detector for metric series. */
export class AnomalyDetector extends EventEmitter {
  constructor(options = {}) {
    super();
    this.enabled = options.enabled !== false;
    this.signal = options.signal || null;
    this.maxRules = boundedInteger(options.maxRules, 1_000, 1, 100_000);
    this.maxSeries = boundedInteger(options.maxSeries, 10_000, 1, 1_000_000);
    this.baselineSamples = boundedInteger(options.baselineSamples, 256, 4, 100_000);
    this.warmupSamples = boundedInteger(options.warmupSamples, 20, 2, this.baselineSamples);
    this.alpha = boundedNumber(options.alpha, 0.2, 0.001, 1);
    this.defaultCooldownMs = boundedInteger(options.cooldownMs, 5 * 60_000, 0, 24 * 60 * 60_000);
    this.emitRecoveries = options.emitRecoveries !== false;
    this.rules = new Map();
    this.baselines = new Map();
    this.lastValues = new Map();
    this.states = new Map();
    this.frozen = false;
    this.stats = { observations: 0, evaluated: 0, anomalies: 0, recoveries: 0, suppressed: 0, seriesRejected: 0, invalid: 0 };
  }

  register(input = {}, options = {}) {
    if (this.frozen) throw new Error('Anomaly detector definitions are frozen.');
    if (this.rules.size >= this.maxRules && !this.rules.has(String(input.id))) throw new Error(`Anomaly rule limit of ${this.maxRules} exceeded.`);
    const rule = normalizeRule(input, { baselineSamples: this.baselineSamples, warmupSamples: this.warmupSamples, alpha: this.alpha, cooldownMs: this.defaultCooldownMs });
    if (this.rules.has(rule.id) && options.replace !== true) throw new Error(`Anomaly rule '${rule.id}' is already registered.`);
    this.rules.set(rule.id, rule);
    return rule;
  }

  unregister(id) {
    const key = String(id);
    for (const seriesKey of this.baselines.keys()) if (seriesKey.startsWith(`${key}\0`)) this.baselines.delete(seriesKey);
    for (const seriesKey of this.lastValues.keys()) if (seriesKey.startsWith(`${key}\0`)) this.lastValues.delete(seriesKey);
    for (const seriesKey of this.states.keys()) if (seriesKey.startsWith(`${key}\0`)) this.states.delete(seriesKey);
    return this.rules.delete(key);
  }

  observe(metric, input, labels = {}, context = {}) {
    if (!this.enabled) return [];
    const value = Number(input);
    if (!Number.isFinite(value)) { this.stats.invalid++; return []; }
    this.stats.observations++;
    const output = [];
    for (const rule of this.rules.values()) {
      if (!matchesMetric(rule.metric, metric) || !matchesLabels(rule.labels, labels)) continue;
      const result = this._observeRule(rule, String(metric), value, labels, context);
      if (result) output.push(result);
    }
    return output;
  }

  observeSnapshot(snapshot = {}, context = {}) {
    const output = [];
    for (const [metric, definition] of Object.entries(snapshot.metrics || {})) {
      for (const series of definition.series || []) {
        const value = metricValue(definition, series);
        if (value == null) continue;
        output.push(...this.observe(metric, value, series.labels || {}, { ...context, statistic: definition.type === 'histogram' || definition.type === 'timer' ? 'mean' : 'value', snapshotTimestamp: snapshot.timestamp }));
      }
    }
    return output;
  }

  _observeRule(rule, metric, rawValue, labels, context) {
    const normalizedLabels = normalizeLabels(labels, { maxLabels: 32, maxValueBytes: 256 });
    const seriesIdentity = labelsKey(normalizedLabels);
    const key = `${rule.id}\0${seriesIdentity}`;
    let value = rawValue;
    if (rule.delta) {
      const previous = this.lastValues.get(key);
      this.lastValues.set(key, rawValue);
      if (previous == null) return null;
      value = rawValue - previous;
      if (rule.monotonic && value < 0) return null;
    }
    let baseline = this.baselines.get(key);
    if (!baseline) {
      if (this.baselines.size >= this.maxSeries) { this.stats.seriesRejected++; return null; }
      baseline = new StreamingBaseline({ maxSamples: rule.baselineSamples, minSamples: rule.warmupSamples, alpha: rule.alpha });
      this.baselines.set(key, baseline);
    }
    const analysis = baseline.observeAndAnalyze(value, { method: rule.method, minSamples: rule.warmupSamples, timestamp: context.snapshotTimestamp || Date.now() });
    this.stats.evaluated++;
    const state = this.states.get(key) || { firing: false, lastEmittedAt: 0, normalSamples: 0, last: null };
    const firing = analysis.warm && isAnomalous(rule, analysis);
    const now = Date.now();
    let emitted = null;

    if (firing) {
      state.normalSamples = 0;
      const cooldownElapsed = now - state.lastEmittedAt >= rule.cooldownMs;
      if (!state.firing || cooldownElapsed) {
        emitted = deepFreeze({
          id: randomId('anomaly'),
          ruleId: rule.id,
          name: rule.name,
          metric,
          labels: normalizedLabels,
          severity: rule.severity,
          state: 'firing',
          value,
          rawValue,
          analysis,
          timestamp: now,
          context,
          tags: rule.tags,
          runbook: rule.runbook,
        });
        state.lastEmittedAt = now;
        this.stats.anomalies++;
        this.emit('anomaly', emitted);
        this.signal?.(SignalKind.ANOMALY, emitted, { timestamp: now, priority: priority(rule.severity), context });
      } else this.stats.suppressed++;
      state.firing = true;
    } else if (state.firing) {
      state.normalSamples++;
      if (state.normalSamples >= rule.recoverySamples) {
        state.firing = false;
        state.normalSamples = 0;
        if (this.emitRecoveries) {
          emitted = deepFreeze({ id: randomId('anomaly'), ruleId: rule.id, name: rule.name, metric, labels: normalizedLabels, severity: rule.severity, state: 'recovered', value, rawValue, analysis, timestamp: now, context, tags: rule.tags, runbook: rule.runbook });
          this.stats.recoveries++;
          this.emit('recovery', emitted);
          this.signal?.(SignalKind.ANOMALY, emitted, { timestamp: now, priority: 2, context });
        }
      }
    }
    state.last = { value, rawValue, analysis, timestamp: now };
    this.states.set(key, state);
    return emitted;
  }

  current(ruleId = null) {
    const output = [];
    for (const [key, state] of this.states) {
      const [id, series] = key.split('\0');
      if (ruleId && id !== String(ruleId)) continue;
      output.push({ ruleId: id, series, firing: state.firing, lastEmittedAt: state.lastEmittedAt, last: state.last });
    }
    return deepFreeze(output);
  }

  alertRule(id, options = {}) {
    const rule = this.rules.get(String(id));
    if (!rule) return null;
    return {
      id: options.id || `anomaly:${rule.id}`,
      name: options.name || `Adaptive anomaly: ${rule.name}`,
      severity: options.severity || rule.severity,
      source: 'custom',
      incident: options.incident ?? rule.incident,
      runbook: options.runbook || rule.runbook,
      tags: [...rule.tags, 'anomaly'],
      evaluate: () => {
        const firing = this.current(rule.id).filter((item) => item.firing);
        return { firing: firing.length > 0, value: firing.length, details: { series: firing.slice(0, 100) } };
      },
    };
  }

  freeze() { this.frozen = true; return this; }
  close() { this.removeAllListeners(); this.rules.clear(); this.baselines.clear(); this.lastValues.clear(); this.states.clear(); }
  snapshot(options = {}) { return deepFreeze({ enabled: this.enabled, frozen: this.frozen, rules: Array.from(this.rules.values()), active: this.current(), baselines: options.includeBaselines ? Array.from(this.baselines, ([key, value]) => ({ key, baseline: value.snapshot() })) : undefined, stats: { ...this.stats } }); }
}

function normalizeRule(input, defaults) {
  const id = String(input.id || input.name || '').trim();
  if (!id) throw new TypeError('Anomaly rules require an id.');
  const method = METHODS.has(String(input.method).toLowerCase()) ? String(input.method).toLowerCase() : 'mad';
  const direction = DIRECTIONS.has(String(input.direction).toLowerCase()) ? String(input.direction).toLowerCase() : 'both';
  const threshold = Number.isFinite(Number(input.threshold)) ? Number(input.threshold) : method === 'relative' ? 0.5 : method === 'absolute' ? 1 : 3.5;
  return deepFreeze({
    id,
    name: String(input.name || id),
    metric: input.metric instanceof RegExp ? input.metric : String(input.metric || '*'),
    labels: normalizeLabels(input.labels || {}, { maxLabels: 32, maxValueBytes: 256 }),
    method,
    direction,
    threshold: Math.max(0, threshold),
    minimum: Math.max(0, Number(input.minimum || input.minAbsolute || 0)),
    baselineSamples: boundedInteger(input.baselineSamples, defaults.baselineSamples, 4, 100_000),
    warmupSamples: boundedInteger(input.warmupSamples, defaults.warmupSamples, 2, 100_000),
    alpha: boundedNumber(input.alpha, defaults.alpha, 0.001, 1),
    cooldownMs: boundedInteger(input.cooldownMs, defaults.cooldownMs, 0, 24 * 60 * 60_000),
    recoverySamples: boundedInteger(input.recoverySamples, 3, 1, 10_000),
    delta: input.delta === true,
    monotonic: input.monotonic !== false,
    severity: String(input.severity || 'warning'),
    incident: input.incident === true,
    alert: input.alert !== false,
    runbook: input.runbook || null,
    tags: Object.freeze(Array.from(new Set((input.tags || []).map(String)))),
  });
}
function matchesMetric(pattern, metric) { if (pattern instanceof RegExp) return pattern.test(metric); if (pattern === '*') return true; if (!String(pattern).includes('*')) return String(pattern) === String(metric); const escaped = String(pattern).replace(/[.+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&').replace(/\*/g, '.*'); return new RegExp(`^${escaped}$`).test(metric); }
function matchesLabels(expected, actual) { for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(expected || {})) if (String(actual?.[key] ?? '') !== String(value)) return false; return true; }
function metricValue(definition, series) { if (['histogram', 'timer'].includes(definition.type)) return Number.isFinite(Number(series.mean)) ? Number(series.mean) : null; return Number.isFinite(Number(series.value)) ? Number(series.value) : null; }
function isAnomalous(rule, analysis) { const delta = analysis.delta; if (Math.abs(delta) < rule.minimum) return false; const direction = rule.direction === 'high' ? delta > 0 : rule.direction === 'low' ? delta < 0 : true; if (!direction) return false; if (rule.method === 'relative') return analysis.relativeDeviation >= rule.threshold; if (rule.method === 'absolute') return Math.abs(delta) >= rule.threshold; return analysis.absoluteScore >= rule.threshold; }
function priority(severity) { return severity === 'critical' ? 20 : severity === 'error' ? 15 : severity === 'warning' ? 10 : 5; }
export default AnomalyDetector;


================================================
FILE PATH: ./config/MonitorConfiguration.js
================================================

'use strict';

const OVERFLOW = new Set(['drop-new', 'drop-oldest', 'reject', 'block']);
const STORAGE_DRIVERS = new Set(['memory', 'file', 'rawmongo', 'composite', 'none']);

function merge(base, overlay) {
  const output = { ...(isPlainObject(base) ? base : {}) };
  for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(isPlainObject(overlay) ? overlay : {})) {
    output[key] = isPlainObject(value) && isPlainObject(output[key]) ? merge(output[key], value) : value;
  }
  return output;
}

export class MonitorConfiguration {
  constructor(input = {}, options = {}) {
    const source = merge(DEFAULTS, input);
    const requested = String(source.profile || options.profile || DEFAULTS.profile).toLowerCase();
    if (!PROFILES.has(requested)) throw new MonitorConfigurationError(`Unsupported Monitor profile '${requested}'.`, { details: { supported: [...PROFILES] } });
    const profile = resolveMonitorCompatibilityProfile(requested, options.profileOverrides);
    const environment = String(source.environment || process.env.APP_ENV || process.env.NODE_ENV || 'development').toLowerCase();
    const production = environment === 'production' || environment === 'prod';
    const root = path.resolve(options.root || source.root || process.cwd());
    const overflow = String(source.pipeline?.overflow || profile.queueOverflow);
    if (!OVERFLOW.has(overflow)) throw new MonitorConfigurationError(`Unsupported monitor queue overflow policy '${overflow}'.`);
    const storageDriver = String(source.storage?.driver || 'memory').toLowerCase();
    if (!STORAGE_DRIVERS.has(storageDriver)) throw new MonitorConfigurationError(`Unsupported monitor storage driver '${storageDriver}'.`);

    this.value = deepFreeze({
      ...source,
      enabled: bool(source.enabled, true),
      profile: profile.name,
      compatibility: profile,
      root,
      service: String(source.service || process.env.APP_NAME || 'rawnode'),
      environment,
      production,
      bootSummary: bool(source.bootSummary, true),
      failFast: bool(source.failFast, profile.failFast),
      collectionIntervalMs: parseDuration(source.collectionIntervalMs, 10_000, 250, 3_600_000),
      resourceIntervalMs: parseDuration(source.resourceIntervalMs, 15_000, 250, 3_600_000),
      shutdownTimeoutMs: parseDuration(source.shutdownTimeoutMs, 15_000, 100, 600_000),
      flushTimeoutMs: parseDuration(source.flushTimeoutMs, 10_000, 100, 600_000),
      metrics: deepFreeze({
        ...source.metrics,
        enabled: bool(source.metrics?.enabled, profile.metricsEnabled),
        prefix: String(source.metrics?.prefix || 'rawnode'),
        maxInstruments: boundedInteger(source.metrics?.maxInstruments, 10_000, 1, 1_000_000),
        maxSeries: boundedInteger(source.metrics?.maxSeries, 100_000, 1, 10_000_000),
        maxSeriesPerInstrument: boundedInteger(source.metrics?.maxSeriesPerInstrument, 2_000, 1, 1_000_000),
        maxLabels: boundedInteger(source.metrics?.maxLabels, 24, 0, 256),
        maxLabelValueBytes: parseBytes(source.metrics?.maxLabelValueBytes, 256, 1, 64 * 1024),
        historyLimit: boundedInteger(source.metrics?.historyLimit, 720, 1, 1_000_000),
        exemplars: bool(source.metrics?.exemplars, true),
      }),
      tracing: deepFreeze({
        ...source.tracing,
        enabled: bool(source.tracing?.enabled, profile.tracingEnabled),
        sampleRate: boundedNumber(
          source.tracing?.sampleRate == null
            ? (production ? 0.05 : (profile.name === 'enterprise' ? 0.1 : 1))
            : source.tracing.sampleRate,
          production ? 0.05 : (profile.name === 'enterprise' ? 0.1 : 1),
          0,
          1,
        ),
        parentBased: bool(source.tracing?.parentBased, true),
        maxAttributes: boundedInteger(source.tracing?.maxAttributes, 64, 0, 4_096),
        maxAttributeBytes: parseBytes(source.tracing?.maxAttributeBytes, 4_096, 16, 1024 * 1024),
        maxEvents: boundedInteger(source.tracing?.maxEvents, 64, 0, 10_000),
        maxLinks: boundedInteger(source.tracing?.maxLinks, 16, 0, 10_000),
        maxSpansInMemory: boundedInteger(source.tracing?.maxSpansInMemory, 20_000, 1, 10_000_000),
        maxTraceAgeMs: parseDuration(source.tracing?.maxTraceAgeMs, 15 * 60_000, 1_000, 30 * 24 * 60 * 60_000),
        captureStack: bool(source.tracing?.captureStack, profile.captureErrorStacks),
        propagateBaggage: bool(source.tracing?.propagateBaggage, true),
      }),
      pipeline: deepFreeze({
        ...source.pipeline,
        maxItems: boundedInteger(source.pipeline?.maxItems, 50_000, 1, 10_000_000),
        maxBytes: parseBytes(source.pipeline?.maxBytes, 64 * 1024 * 1024, 1_024, 16 * 1024 * 1024 * 1024),
        maxWaiters: boundedInteger(source.pipeline?.maxWaiters, 100_000, 1, 10_000_000),
        batchSize: boundedInteger(source.pipeline?.batchSize, 512, 1, 100_000),
        flushIntervalMs: parseDuration(source.pipeline?.flushIntervalMs, 500, 1, 60_000),
        budgetMs: boundedNumber(source.pipeline?.budgetMs, 8, 0.25, 1_000),
        overflow,
        maxSignalBytes: parseBytes(source.pipeline?.maxSignalBytes, 2 * 1024 * 1024, 256, 256 * 1024 * 1024),
        maxInFlightBatches: boundedInteger(source.pipeline?.maxInFlightBatches, 2, 1, 64),
        deliveryAttempts: boundedInteger(source.pipeline?.deliveryAttempts, 3, 1, 100),
        retryBaseMs: parseDuration(source.pipeline?.retryBaseMs, 50, 0, 60_000),
        retryMaxMs: parseDuration(source.pipeline?.retryMaxMs, 2_000, 0, 300_000),
        failureHistoryLimit: boundedInteger(source.pipeline?.failureHistoryLimit, 256, 1, 100_000),
      }),
      health: deepFreeze({
        ...source.health,
        enabled: bool(source.health?.enabled, true),
        timeoutMs: parseDuration(source.health?.timeoutMs, 5_000, 10, 300_000),
        concurrency: boundedInteger(source.health?.concurrency, 16, 1, 1_024),
        historyLimit: boundedInteger(source.health?.historyLimit, 256, 1, 100_000),
        cacheTtlMs: parseDuration(source.health?.cacheTtlMs, 1_000, 0, 300_000),
      }),
      resources: deepFreeze({
        ...source.resources,
        enabled: bool(source.resources?.enabled, true),
        autoDiscover: bool(source.resources?.autoDiscover, true),
        intervalMs: parseDuration(source.resources?.intervalMs, source.resourceIntervalMs || 15_000, 250, 3_600_000),
        timeoutMs: parseDuration(source.resources?.timeoutMs, 5_000, 10, 300_000),
        maxPayloadBytes: parseBytes(source.resources?.maxPayloadBytes, 512 * 1024, 1_024, 64 * 1024 * 1024),
        include: Object.freeze(list(source.resources?.include)),
        exclude: Object.freeze(list(source.resources?.exclude)),
        required: Object.freeze(list(source.resources?.required)),
      }),
      storage: deepFreeze({
        ...source.storage,
        enabled: bool(source.storage?.enabled, profile.persistenceEnabled),
        driver: storageDriver,
        drivers: Object.freeze(list(source.storage?.drivers)),
        queryStore: source.storage?.queryStore ? String(source.storage.queryStore) : null,
        path: path.resolve(root, source.storage?.path || 'storage/framework/monitor'),
        maxMemoryItems: boundedInteger(source.storage?.maxMemoryItems, 50_000, 1, 10_000_000),
        maxMemoryBytes: parseBytes(source.storage?.maxMemoryBytes, 128 * 1024 * 1024, 1_024, 16 * 1024 * 1024 * 1024),
        maxFileBytes: parseBytes(source.storage?.maxFileBytes, 128 * 1024 * 1024, 1_024, 16 * 1024 * 1024 * 1024),
        maxReadBytes: parseBytes(source.storage?.maxReadBytes, 64 * 1024 * 1024, 1_024, 4 * 1024 * 1024 * 1024),
        maxLineBytes: parseBytes(source.storage?.maxLineBytes, 2 * 1024 * 1024, 1_024, 256 * 1024 * 1024),
        retentionMs: parseDuration(source.storage?.retentionMs, 30 * 24 * 60 * 60_000, 60_000, 10 * 365 * 24 * 60 * 60_000),
        flushIntervalMs: parseDuration(source.storage?.flushIntervalMs, 1_000, 1, 60_000),
        collectionPrefix: String(source.storage?.collectionPrefix || 'monitor_'),
        required: bool(source.storage?.required, false),
      }),
      exporters: normalizeExporters(source.exporters, profile),
      alerts: deepFreeze({
        ...source.alerts,
        enabled: bool(source.alerts?.enabled, profile.alertsEnabled),
        intervalMs: parseDuration(source.alerts?.intervalMs, 15_000, 250, 3_600_000),
        concurrency: boundedInteger(source.alerts?.concurrency, 8, 1, 1_024),
        historyLimit: boundedInteger(source.alerts?.historyLimit, 5_000, 1, 1_000_000),
        defaultPendingForMs: parseDuration(source.alerts?.defaultPendingForMs, 0, 0, 30 * 24 * 60 * 60_000),
        defaultResolveAfterMs: parseDuration(source.alerts?.defaultResolveAfterMs, 0, 0, 30 * 24 * 60 * 60_000),
        defaultCooldownMs: parseDuration(source.alerts?.defaultCooldownMs, 5 * 60_000, 0, 30 * 24 * 60 * 60_000),
        autoIncidentSeverities: Object.freeze(list(source.alerts?.autoIncidentSeverities, ['critical'])),
      }),
      slo: deepFreeze({ ...source.slo, enabled: bool(source.slo?.enabled, profile.sloEnabled), intervalMs: parseDuration(source.slo?.intervalMs, 60_000, 1_000, 3_600_000), historyLimit: boundedInteger(source.slo?.historyLimit, 44_640, 1, 10_000_000) }),
      incidents: deepFreeze({ ...source.incidents, enabled: bool(source.incidents?.enabled, profile.incidentsEnabled), maxOpen: boundedInteger(source.incidents?.maxOpen, 10_000, 1, 1_000_000), historyLimit: boundedInteger(source.incidents?.historyLimit, 20_000, 1, 1_000_000), captureDiagnostics: bool(source.incidents?.captureDiagnostics, true), diagnosticsTimeoutMs: parseDuration(source.incidents?.diagnosticsTimeoutMs, 10_000, 100, 300_000) }),
      system: deepFreeze({ ...source.system, enabled: bool(source.system?.enabled, profile.systemMetricsEnabled), eventLoopResolutionMs: boundedInteger(source.system?.eventLoopResolutionMs, 20, 10, 1_000), gc: bool(source.system?.gc, true), activeResources: bool(source.system?.activeResources, true), resourceUsage: bool(source.system?.resourceUsage, true) }),
      analysis: deepFreeze({
        ...source.analysis,
        enabled: bool(source.analysis?.enabled, profile.analysisEnabled),
        defaults: bool(source.analysis?.defaults, true),
        maxRules: boundedInteger(source.analysis?.maxRules, 1_000, 1, 100_000),
        maxSeries: boundedInteger(source.analysis?.maxSeries, 10_000, 1, 1_000_000),
        baselineSamples: boundedInteger(source.analysis?.baselineSamples, 256, 4, 100_000),
        warmupSamples: boundedInteger(source.analysis?.warmupSamples, 20, 2, 100_000),
        alpha: boundedNumber(source.analysis?.alpha, 0.2, 0.001, 1),
        cooldownMs: parseDuration(source.analysis?.cooldownMs, 5 * 60_000, 0, 24 * 60 * 60_000),
        emitRecoveries: bool(source.analysis?.emitRecoveries, true),
        rules: Object.freeze(Array.isArray(source.analysis?.rules) ? [...source.analysis.rules] : []),
        leakRules: Object.freeze(Array.isArray(source.analysis?.leakRules) ? [...source.analysis.leakRules] : []),
        correlationWindowMs: parseDuration(source.analysis?.correlationWindowMs, 15 * 60_000, 1_000, 30 * 24 * 60 * 60_000),
        correlationMaxCandidates: boundedInteger(source.analysis?.correlationMaxCandidates, 10_000, 10, 1_000_000),
      }),
      synthetics: deepFreeze({
        ...source.synthetics,
        enabled: bool(source.synthetics?.enabled, profile.syntheticsEnabled),
        concurrency: boundedInteger(source.synthetics?.concurrency, 8, 1, 1_024),
        maxChecks: boundedInteger(source.synthetics?.maxChecks, 10_000, 1, 100_000),
        intervalMs: parseDuration(source.synthetics?.intervalMs, 60_000, 1_000, 24 * 60 * 60_000),
        timeoutMs: parseDuration(source.synthetics?.timeoutMs, 10_000, 100, 10 * 60_000),
        historyLimit: boundedInteger(source.synthetics?.historyLimit, 5_000, 1, 1_000_000),
        maxResponseBytes: parseBytes(source.synthetics?.maxResponseBytes, 256 * 1024, 1_024, 64 * 1024 * 1024),
        checks: Object.freeze(Array.isArray(source.synthetics?.checks) ? [...source.synthetics.checks] : []),
      }),
      plugins: deepFreeze({
        ...source.plugins,
        enabled: bool(source.plugins?.enabled, profile.pluginsEnabled),
        maxPlugins: boundedInteger(source.plugins?.maxPlugins, 1_000, 1, 100_000),
        errorPolicy: ['isolate', 'propagate'].includes(String(source.plugins?.errorPolicy)) ? String(source.plugins.errorPolicy) : profile.exporterErrorPolicy,
        maxPendingHooks: boundedInteger(source.plugins?.maxPendingHooks, 10_000, 1, 1_000_000),
        maxPendingBytes: parseBytes(source.plugins?.maxPendingBytes, 16 * 1024 * 1024, 1_024, 1024 * 1024 * 1024),
        maxHookBytes: parseBytes(source.plugins?.maxHookBytes, 1024 * 1024, 256, 64 * 1024 * 1024),
        hookBatchSize: boundedInteger(source.plugins?.hookBatchSize, 128, 1, 10_000),
        hookBudgetMs: boundedNumber(source.plugins?.hookBudgetMs, 4, 0.25, 1_000),
        hookOverflow: ['drop-new', 'drop-oldest'].includes(String(source.plugins?.hookOverflow)) ? String(source.plugins.hookOverflow) : 'drop-oldest',
      }),
      security: deepFreeze({
        ...source.security,
        redact: bool(source.security?.redact, true),
        secretKeys: Object.freeze(list(source.security?.secretKeys)),
        hashTenantIds: bool(source.security?.hashTenantIds, false),
        hashUserIds: bool(source.security?.hashUserIds, false),
        maxDepth: boundedInteger(source.security?.maxDepth, 16, 1, 128),
        maxKeys: boundedInteger(source.security?.maxKeys, 4_096, 1, 1_000_000),
        maxArrayItems: boundedInteger(source.security?.maxArrayItems, 10_000, 1, 1_000_000),
        maxStringBytes: parseBytes(source.security?.maxStringBytes, 64 * 1024, 16, 16 * 1024 * 1024),
        maxPayloadBytes: parseBytes(source.security?.maxPayloadBytes, 2 * 1024 * 1024, 1_024, 256 * 1024 * 1024),
        allowRemoteIngest: bool(source.security?.allowRemoteIngest, false),
        allowedOrigins: Object.freeze(list(source.security?.allowedOrigins)),
      }),
      studio: deepFreeze({
        ...source.studio,
        enabled: bool(source.studio?.enabled, profile.studioDefaultEnabled) && (!production || bool(source.studio?.production, profile.studioProductionEnabled)),
        production: bool(source.studio?.production, false),
        prefix: normalizePrefix(source.studio?.prefix || '/__rawnode/monitor'),
        localOnly: bool(source.studio?.localOnly, true),
        pollIntervalMs: parseDuration(source.studio?.pollIntervalMs, 3_000, 500, 60_000),
        maxQueryLimit: boundedInteger(source.studio?.maxQueryLimit, 1_000, 1, 100_000),
        metricsEndpoint: normalizePrefix(source.studio?.metricsEndpoint || '/metrics'),
        registerRoutes: bool(source.studio?.registerRoutes, true),
        streamMaxBytes: parseBytes(source.studio?.streamMaxBytes, 1024 * 1024, 1_024, 64 * 1024 * 1024),
        eventQueueItems: boundedInteger(source.studio?.eventQueueItems, 2_000, 1, 1_000_000),
        eventQueueBytes: parseBytes(source.studio?.eventQueueBytes, 2 * 1024 * 1024, 1_024, 256 * 1024 * 1024),
        eventMaxBytes: parseBytes(source.studio?.eventMaxBytes, 64 * 1024, 256, 16 * 1024 * 1024),
        eventBatchSize: boundedInteger(source.studio?.eventBatchSize, 100, 1, 10_000),
        eventBudgetMs: boundedNumber(source.studio?.eventBudgetMs, 4, 0.25, 1_000),
        rateLimit: deepFreeze({
          capacity: boundedInteger(source.studio?.rateLimit?.capacity, 120, 1, 1_000_000),
          refillPerSecond: boundedNumber(source.studio?.rateLimit?.refillPerSecond, 20, 0.001, 100_000),
          maxKeys: boundedInteger(source.studio?.rateLimit?.maxKeys, 10_000, 1, 1_000_000),
          idleTtlMs: parseDuration(source.studio?.rateLimit?.idleTtlMs, 5 * 60_000, 1_000, 30 * 24 * 60 * 60_000),
        }),
      }),
      integrations: deepFreeze(merge(DEFAULTS.integrations, source.integrations)),
      cluster: deepFreeze({ ...source.cluster, enabled: bool(source.cluster?.enabled, false), mode: String(source.cluster?.mode || 'standalone'), heartbeatMs: parseDuration(source.cluster?.heartbeatMs, 10_000, 1_000, 300_000), leaseMs: parseDuration(source.cluster?.leaseMs, 30_000, 2_000, 600_000) }),
    });

    this.validate();
  }

  get(pathValue, fallback = undefined) {
    let current = this.value;
    for (const part of String(pathValue || '').split('.').filter(Boolean)) current = current?.[part];
    return current === undefined ? fallback : current;
  }

  snapshot() { return this.value; }

  validate() {
    const errors = [];
    const warnings = [];
    const value = this.value;
    if (value.production && value.studio.enabled && !value.studio.token && value.studio.localOnly === false) errors.push({ code: 'MONITOR_STUDIO_PUBLIC_UNAUTHORIZED', message: 'Production Monitor Studio requires a token, an authorizer, or localOnly=true.' });
    if (value.security.allowRemoteIngest && !value.security.ingestSecret && value.compatibility.requireSignedRemoteIngest) errors.push({ code: 'MONITOR_INGEST_SECRET_REQUIRED', message: 'Signed remote ingestion requires security.ingestSecret.' });
    if (value.pipeline.maxBytes < value.pipeline.maxSignalBytes) warnings.push({ code: 'MONITOR_SIGNAL_EXCEEDS_QUEUE', message: 'pipeline.maxSignalBytes is greater than pipeline.maxBytes.' });
    if (value.metrics.maxSeriesPerInstrument > value.metrics.maxSeries) warnings.push({ code: 'MONITOR_SERIES_LIMIT_ORDER', message: 'Per-instrument series limit exceeds the global series limit.' });
    if (errors.length && value.compatibility.strictConfiguration) throw new MonitorConfigurationError('RawNode Monitor configuration is invalid.', { details: { errors, warnings } });
    this.validation = deepFreeze({ ok: errors.length === 0, errors, warnings });
    return this.validation;
  }
}


function normalizeExporters(input, profile) {
  const source = merge(DEFAULTS.exporters, input);
  const http = source.http || {};
  const otlp = source.otlp || {};
  const statsd = source.statsd || {};
  return deepFreeze({
    ...source,
    enabled: bool(source.enabled, true),
    failFast: bool(source.failFast, profile.exporterErrorPolicy === 'propagate'),
    prometheus: deepFreeze({
      ...(source.prometheus || {}),
      enabled: bool(source.prometheus?.enabled, true),
      prefix: String(source.prometheus?.prefix || 'rawnode'),
    }),
    http: deepFreeze({
      ...http,
      enabled: bool(http.enabled, false),
      timeoutMs: parseDuration(http.timeoutMs, 10_000, 100, 300_000),
      compression: bool(http.compression, true),
      compressionThresholdBytes: parseBytes(http.compressionThresholdBytes, 1_024, 256, 16 * 1024 * 1024),
      maxAttempts: boundedInteger(http.maxAttempts, 3, 1, 100),
      retryBaseMs: parseDuration(http.retryBaseMs, 100, 0, 60_000),
      retryMaxMs: parseDuration(http.retryMaxMs, 5_000, 0, 300_000),
      maxPayloadBytes: parseBytes(http.maxPayloadBytes, 8 * 1024 * 1024, 1_024, 256 * 1024 * 1024),
      maxResponseBytes: parseBytes(http.maxResponseBytes, 64 * 1024, 1_024, 16 * 1024 * 1024),
      maxBatchSignals: boundedInteger(http.maxBatchSignals, 256, 1, 100_000),
      rejectUnauthorized: bool(http.rejectUnauthorized, true),
      required: bool(http.required, false),
    }),
    otlp: deepFreeze({
      ...otlp,
      enabled: bool(otlp.enabled, false),
      timeoutMs: parseDuration(otlp.timeoutMs, 10_000, 100, 300_000),
      compression: bool(otlp.compression, true),
      maxAttempts: boundedInteger(otlp.maxAttempts, 3, 1, 100),
      retryBaseMs: parseDuration(otlp.retryBaseMs, 100, 0, 60_000),
      retryMaxMs: parseDuration(otlp.retryMaxMs, 5_000, 0, 300_000),
      maxPayloadBytes: parseBytes(otlp.maxPayloadBytes, 8 * 1024 * 1024, 1_024, 256 * 1024 * 1024),
      maxResponseBytes: parseBytes(otlp.maxResponseBytes, 64 * 1024, 1_024, 16 * 1024 * 1024),
      maxBatchSignals: boundedInteger(otlp.maxBatchSignals, 256, 1, 100_000),
      rejectUnauthorized: bool(otlp.rejectUnauthorized, true),
      required: bool(otlp.required, false),
      headers: deepFreeze(isPlainObject(otlp.headers) ? { ...otlp.headers } : {}),
    }),
    statsd: deepFreeze({
      ...statsd,
      enabled: bool(statsd.enabled, false),
      host: String(statsd.host || '127.0.0.1'),
      port: boundedInteger(statsd.port, 8125, 1, 65_535),
      prefix: String(statsd.prefix || 'rawnode'),
      maxPacketBytes: parseBytes(statsd.maxPacketBytes, 8_192, 512, 65_507),
    }),
  });
}

function normalizePrefix(value) {
  const text = String(value || '').trim();
  if (!text) return '/';
  const output = text.startsWith('/') ? text : `/${text}`;
  return output.length > 1 && output.endsWith('/') ? output.slice(0, -1) : output;
}

export function resolveMonitorConfiguration(input = {}, options = {}) {
  return input instanceof MonitorConfiguration ? input : new MonitorConfiguration(input, options);
}

export { DEFAULTS as MONITOR_DEFAULTS };
export default MonitorConfiguration;

FILE PATH: ./security/TelemetryAuthenticator.js

'use strict';

import { createHmac, timingSafeEqual } from 'node:crypto';
import { MonitorSecurityError } from '../errors.js';

export class TelemetryAuthenticator {
  constructor(options = {}) {
    this.secret = options.secret || null;
    this.header = String(options.header || 'x-rawnode-monitor-signature').toLowerCase();
    this.timestampHeader = String(options.timestampHeader || 'x-rawnode-monitor-timestamp').toLowerCase();
    this.maxSkewMs = Math.max(1_000, Number(options.maxSkewMs) || 5 * 60_000);
    this.algorithm = 'sha256';
  }

  sign(body, timestamp = Date.now()) {
    if (!this.secret) throw new MonitorSecurityError('Monitor ingestion secret is not configured.', { code: 'MONITOR_INGEST_SECRET_MISSING' });
    const text = Buffer.isBuffer(body) ? body : Buffer.from(String(body));
    const signature = createHmac(this.algorithm, this.secret).update(String(timestamp)).update('.').update(text).digest('hex');
    return { timestamp: String(timestamp), signature: `sha256=${signature}` };
  }

  verify(body, headers = {}, now = Date.now()) {
    if (!this.secret) return false;
    const timestamp = Number(headers[this.timestampHeader] ?? headers[this.timestampHeader.toUpperCase()]);
    const presented = String(headers[this.header] ?? headers[this.header.toUpperCase()] ?? '');
    if (!Number.isFinite(timestamp) || Math.abs(now - timestamp) > this.maxSkewMs || !presented.startsWith('sha256=')) return false;
    const expected = this.sign(body, timestamp).signature;
    const left = Buffer.from(expected);
    const right = Buffer.from(presented);
    return left.length === right.length && timingSafeEqual(left, right);
  }

  assert(body, headers = {}) {
    if (!this.verify(body, headers)) throw new MonitorSecurityError('Monitor ingestion signature is invalid or expired.', { code: 'MONITOR_INGEST_SIGNATURE_INVALID', status: 401 });
    return true;
  }
}

export default TelemetryAuthenticator;

'use strict';

import { BLOCKED_OBJECT_KEYS } from '../constants.js';
import { bytesOf, boundedInteger, errorRecord } from '../support.js';

export class SafeTelemetrySerializer {
  constructor(options = {}) {
    this.maxDepth = boundedInteger(options.maxDepth, 16, 1, 128);
    this.maxKeys = boundedInteger(options.maxKeys, 4_096, 1, 1_000_000);
    this.maxArrayItems = boundedInteger(options.maxArrayItems, 10_000, 1, 1_000_000);
    this.maxStringBytes = boundedInteger(options.maxStringBytes, 64 * 1024, 16, 16 * 1024 * 1024);
    this.maxPayloadBytes = boundedInteger(options.maxPayloadBytes, 2 * 1024 * 1024, 1_024, 256 * 1024 * 1024);
    this.circular = options.circular || '[Circular]';
    this.truncated = options.truncated || '[Truncated]';
    this.includeErrorStack = options.includeErrorStack === true;
    this.stats = { serialized: 0, truncated: 0, circular: 0, blockedKeys: 0, errors: 0 };
  }

  sanitize(value, options = {}) {
    const state = { seen: new WeakSet(), keys: 0, bytes: 0, truncated: false };
    try {
      const output = this._visit(value, 0, state, options);
      this.stats.serialized++;
      if (state.truncated) this.stats.truncated++;
      return output;
    } catch (error) {
      this.stats.errors++;
      return { serializationError: error?.message || String(error) };
    }
  }

  stringify(value, options = {}) {
    const sanitized = this.sanitize(value, options);
    const json = JSON.stringify(sanitized);
    if (Buffer.byteLength(json) <= (options.maxPayloadBytes || this.maxPayloadBytes)) return json;
    this.stats.truncated++;
    return JSON.stringify({ truncated: true, bytes: Buffer.byteLength(json), preview: json.slice(0, Math.min(32_768, this.maxStringBytes)) });
  }

  _visit(value, depth, state, options) {
    if (state.bytes >= (options.maxPayloadBytes || this.maxPayloadBytes)) { state.truncated = true; return this.truncated; }
    if (value == null || typeof value === 'boolean' || typeof value === 'number') { state.bytes += 16; return Number.isFinite(value) || typeof value !== 'number' ? value : String(value); }
    if (typeof value === 'bigint') { const text = value.toString(); state.bytes += text.length; return text; }
    if (typeof value === 'string') return this._string(value, state, options);
    if (typeof value === 'symbol' || typeof value === 'function') return `[${typeof value}]`;
    if (depth >= (options.maxDepth || this.maxDepth)) { state.truncated = true; return this.truncated; }
    if (Buffer.isBuffer(value) || value instanceof Uint8Array) {
      const maximum = Math.min(value.byteLength, options.maxBinaryBytes || 4_096);
      state.bytes += maximum;
      return { type: value.constructor?.name || 'Uint8Array', bytes: value.byteLength, base64: Buffer.from(value.subarray(0, maximum)).toString('base64'), truncated: maximum < value.byteLength };
    }
    if (value instanceof Date) return Number.isNaN(value.getTime()) ? null : value.toISOString();
    if (value instanceof URL) return value.toString();
    if (value instanceof RegExp) return String(value);
    if (value instanceof Error) return this._visit(errorRecord(value, { stack: options.includeErrorStack ?? this.includeErrorStack }), depth + 1, state, options);
    if (state.seen.has(value)) { this.stats.circular++; return this.circular; }
    state.seen.add(value);
    try {
      if (Array.isArray(value)) {
        const maximum = Math.min(value.length, options.maxArrayItems || this.maxArrayItems);
        const output = new Array(maximum);
        for (let index = 0; index < maximum; index++) output[index] = this._visit(value[index], depth + 1, state, options);
        if (maximum < value.length) { output.push(this.truncated); state.truncated = true; }
        return output;
      }
      if (value instanceof Map) return this._visit(Object.fromEntries(Array.from(value.entries()).slice(0, this.maxKeys)), depth + 1, state, options);
      if (value instanceof Set) return this._visit(Array.from(value).slice(0, this.maxArrayItems), depth + 1, state, options);
      const output = Object.create(null);
      let keys;
      try { keys = Object.keys(value); } catch { return '[Uninspectable]'; }
      for (const key of keys) {
        if (state.keys >= (options.maxKeys || this.maxKeys)) { output.$truncated = true; state.truncated = true; break; }
        if (BLOCKED_OBJECT_KEYS.has(key)) { this.stats.blockedKeys++; continue; }
        state.keys++;
        let item;
        try { item = value[key]; } catch (error) { item = `[GetterError:${error?.message || error}]`; }
        output[key] = this._visit(item, depth + 1, state, options);
      }
      return output;
    } finally { state.seen.delete(value); }
  }

  _string(value, state, options) {
    const limit = options.maxStringBytes || this.maxStringBytes;
    const bytes = Buffer.byteLength(value);
    state.bytes += Math.min(bytes, limit);
    if (bytes <= limit) return value;
    state.truncated = true;
    let end = Math.min(value.length, limit);
    while (end > 0 && Buffer.byteLength(value.slice(0, end)) > limit) end = Math.floor(end * 0.9);
    return `${value.slice(0, end)}${this.truncated}`;
  }

  snapshot() { return Object.freeze({ ...this.stats }); }
}

export default SafeTelemetrySerializer;


================================================
FILE PATH: ./security/TelemetryRedactor.js
================================================

'use strict';

import { createHash } from 'node:crypto';
import { BLOCKED_OBJECT_KEYS } from '../constants.js';

const DEFAULT_KEYS = Object.freeze([
  'password', 'passwd', 'passphrase', 'secret', 'token', 'authorization', 'cookie',
  'set-cookie', 'api_key', 'apikey', 'private_key', 'privatekey', 'client_secret',
  'credit_card', 'card_number', 'cvv', 'pin', 'session', 'session_id', 'access_token',
  'refresh_token', 'jwt', 'signature', 'pepper', 'salt',
]);
const DEFAULT_PATTERNS = Object.freeze([
  /\bBearer\s+[A-Za-z0-9._~+\/-]+=*/gi,
  /\b(?:sk|pk)_(?:live|test)_[A-Za-z0-9]+\b/g,
]);

export class TelemetryRedactor {
  constructor(options = {}) {
    this.enabled = options.enabled !== false;
    this.placeholder = options.placeholder || '[REDACTED]';
    this.keys = new Set([...DEFAULT_KEYS, ...(options.keys || options.secretKeys || [])].map((key) => String(key).toLowerCase()));
    this.patterns = [...DEFAULT_PATTERNS, ...(options.patterns || [])];
    this.hashTenantIds = options.hashTenantIds === true;
    this.hashUserIds = options.hashUserIds === true;
    this.hashKey = options.hashKey || null;
    this.maxDepth = Number(options.maxDepth) || 20;
    this.stats = { redactedFields: 0, redactedPatterns: 0, hashedIdentifiers: 0 };
  }

  redact(value) {
    if (!this.enabled) return value;
    return this._visit(value, null, 0, new WeakSet());
  }

  _visit(value, key, depth, seen) {
    if (value == null) return value;
    if (key && this._sensitive(key)) { this.stats.redactedFields++; return this.placeholder; }
    if (typeof value === 'string') {
      let output = value;
      for (const pattern of this.patterns) {
        const next = output.replace(pattern, this.placeholder);
        if (next !== output) this.stats.redactedPatterns++;
        output = next;
      }
      if ((key === 'tenantId' || key === 'tenant_id') && this.hashTenantIds) return this.hash(output);
      if ((key === 'userId' || key === 'user_id' || key === 'actorId') && this.hashUserIds) return this.hash(output);
      return output;
    }
    if (typeof value !== 'object' || depth >= this.maxDepth) return value;
    if (seen.has(value)) return '[Circular]';
    seen.add(value);
    try {
      if (Array.isArray(value)) return value.map((item) => this._visit(item, null, depth + 1, seen));
      if (Buffer.isBuffer(value) || value instanceof Uint8Array || value instanceof Date || value instanceof Error) return value;
      const output = Object.create(null);
      for (const property of Object.keys(value)) {
        if (BLOCKED_OBJECT_KEYS.has(property)) continue;
        output[property] = this._visit(value[property], property, depth + 1, seen);
      }
      return output;
    } finally { seen.delete(value); }
  }

  _sensitive(key) {
    const normalized = String(key)
      .replace(/([a-z0-9])([A-Z])/g, '$1_$2')
      .toLowerCase()
      .replace(/[.\-\s]+/g, '_');
    return this.keys.has(normalized) || [...this.keys].some((candidate) => normalized.endsWith(`_${candidate}`));
  }

  hash(value) {
    this.stats.hashedIdentifiers++;
    const hash = createHash('sha256');
    if (this.hashKey) hash.update(String(this.hashKey));
    return `sha256:${hash.update(String(value)).digest('hex').slice(0, 24)}`;
  }

  snapshot() { return Object.freeze({ enabled: this.enabled, keys: this.keys.size, patterns: this.patterns.length, stats: Object.freeze({ ...this.stats }) }); }
}

export default TelemetryRedactor;
