'use strict';
export * from './MonitorPluginManager.js';


================================================
FILE PATH: ./plugins/MonitorPluginManager.js
================================================

'use strict';

import { EventEmitter } from 'node:events';
import BoundedSignalQueue from '../pipeline/BoundedSignalQueue.js';
import {
  boundedInteger,
  boundedNumber,
  bytesOf,
  cooperativeYield,
  deepFreeze,
  elapsedMs,
  errorRecord,
  nowNs,
} from '../support.js';

/** Deterministic lifecycle and bounded hook manager for first-party/userland Monitor plugins. */
export class MonitorPluginManager extends EventEmitter {
  constructor(options = {}) {
    super();
    this.enabled = options.enabled !== false;
    this.errorPolicy = options.errorPolicy || 'isolate';
    this.maxPlugins = boundedInteger(options.maxPlugins, 1_000, 1, 100_000);
    this.hookBatchSize = boundedInteger(options.hookBatchSize, 128, 1, 10_000);
    this.hookBudgetMs = boundedNumber(options.hookBudgetMs, 4, 0.25, 1_000);
    this.maxHookBytes = boundedInteger(options.maxHookBytes, 1024 * 1024, 256, 64 * 1024 * 1024);
    const hookOverflow = ['drop-new', 'drop-oldest'].includes(String(options.hookOverflow)) ? String(options.hookOverflow) : 'drop-oldest';
    const maxPendingBytes = boundedInteger(options.maxPendingBytes, 16 * 1024 * 1024, 1_024, 1024 * 1024 * 1024);
    this.notifications = new BoundedSignalQueue({
      maxItems: boundedInteger(options.maxPendingHooks, 10_000, 1, 1_000_000),
      maxBytes: maxPendingBytes,
      maxSignalBytes: Math.min(this.maxHookBytes, maxPendingBytes),
      overflow: hookOverflow,
      sizeOf: (entry) => Math.min(
        this.maxHookBytes + 1,
        128 + bytesOf(entry?.payload, this.maxHookBytes) + bytesOf(entry?.context, Math.min(this.maxHookBytes, 64 * 1024)),
      ),
    });
    this.plugins = new Map();
    this.hooks = new Map();
    this.order = [];
    this.runtime = null;
    this.setupComplete = false;
    this.started = false;
    this.closed = false;
    this.frozen = false;
    this.notificationDrainPromise = null;
    this.stats = {
      registered: 0,
      setups: 0,
      starts: 0,
      flushes: 0,
      closes: 0,
      hookCalls: 0,
      hookErrors: 0,
      hookNotifications: 0,
      hookNotificationBatches: 0,
      hookNotificationDrops: 0,
    };
  }

  register(input, options = {}) {
    if (!this.enabled) return null;
    if (this.frozen) throw new Error('Monitor plugin registry is frozen.');
    const plugin = normalizePlugin(input, options);
    if (this.plugins.size >= this.maxPlugins && !this.plugins.has(plugin.id)) throw new Error(`Monitor plugin limit of ${this.maxPlugins} exceeded.`);
    if (this.plugins.has(plugin.id) && options.replace !== true) throw new Error(`Monitor plugin '${plugin.id}' is already registered.`);
    this.plugins.set(plugin.id, plugin);
    this.stats.registered++;
    return plugin;
  }

  hook(name, handler, options = {}) {
    if (this.frozen) throw new Error('Monitor plugin hooks are frozen.');
    if (typeof handler !== 'function') throw new TypeError('Monitor plugin hook requires a function.');
    const key = String(name);
    const handlers = this.hooks.get(key) || [];
    const record = Object.freeze({ plugin: options.plugin || 'anonymous', handler, priority: Number(options.priority || 0), once: options.once === true });
    handlers.push(record);
    handlers.sort((left, right) => right.priority - left.priority || left.plugin.localeCompare(right.plugin));
    this.hooks.set(key, handlers);
    return () => this.offHook(key, handler);
  }

  offHook(name, handler) {
    const handlers = this.hooks.get(String(name));
    if (!handlers) return false;
    const index = handlers.findIndex((record) => record.handler === handler);
    if (index === -1) return false;
    handlers.splice(index, 1);
    if (!handlers.length) this.hooks.delete(String(name));
    return true;
  }

  async setup(runtime) {
    if (!this.enabled || this.setupComplete) return this;
    this.runtime = runtime;
    this.order = resolveOrder(this.plugins);
    for (const id of this.order) {
      const record = this.plugins.get(id);
      try {
        const api = createApi(this, runtime, record);
        await record.plugin.setup?.(api);
        for (const [name, handler] of Object.entries(record.plugin.hooks || {})) this.hook(name, handler.bind(record.plugin), { plugin: id, priority: record.priority });
        record.state = 'ready'; record.setupAt = Date.now(); this.stats.setups++;
      } catch (error) { record.state = 'failed'; record.error = errorRecord(error); this._error(error, id, 'setup'); if (record.required || this.errorPolicy === 'propagate') throw error; }
    }
    this.setupComplete = true;
    return this;
  }

  async start(runtime = this.runtime) {
    if (!this.enabled || this.started) return this;
    if (!this.setupComplete) await this.setup(runtime);
    for (const id of this.order) {
      const record = this.plugins.get(id);
      if (record.state === 'failed') continue;
      try { await record.plugin.start?.(createApi(this, runtime, record)); record.state = 'started'; record.startedAt = Date.now(); this.stats.starts++; }
      catch (error) { record.state = 'failed'; record.error = errorRecord(error); this._error(error, id, 'start'); if (record.required || this.errorPolicy === 'propagate') throw error; }
    }
    this.started = true;
    return this;
  }

  async invoke(name, payload, context = {}) {
    if (!this.enabled || this.closed) return [];
    const handlers = [...(this.hooks.get(String(name)) || [])];
    const output = [];
    for (const record of handlers) {
      try { output.push(await record.handler(payload, { ...context, runtime: this.runtime, plugin: record.plugin, hook: String(name) })); this.stats.hookCalls++; if (record.once) this.offHook(name, record.handler); }
      catch (error) { this.stats.hookErrors++; this._error(error, record.plugin, `hook:${name}`); if (this.errorPolicy === 'propagate') throw error; }
    }
    return output;
  }

  notify(name, payload, context = {}) {
    if (!this.enabled || this.closed) return false;
    const hook = String(name);
    if (!this.hooks.has(hook)) return false;
    const result = this.notifications.tryPush(
      { name: hook, payload, context },
      { onDrop: () => { this.stats.hookNotificationDrops++; } },
    );
    if (!result.accepted) return false;
    this.stats.hookNotifications++;
    this._scheduleNotificationDrain();
    return true;
  }

  async drainNotifications() {
    if (!this.enabled || this.closed) return this.notifications.snapshot();
    do {
      if (this.notifications.length && !this.notificationDrainPromise) this._scheduleNotificationDrain();
      const active = this.notificationDrainPromise;
      if (active) await active;
    } while (this.notifications.length || this.notificationDrainPromise);
    return this.notifications.snapshot();
  }

  async flush(options = {}) {
    if (!this.enabled || this.closed) return this.snapshot();
    await this.drainNotifications();
    const failures = [];
    for (const id of this.order) {
      const record = this.plugins.get(id);
      if (record.state === 'failed') continue;
      if (typeof record.plugin.flush !== 'function') continue;
      try { await record.plugin.flush(createApi(this, this.runtime, record), options); this.stats.flushes++; }
      catch (error) {
        record.error = errorRecord(error);
        failures.push({ record, error });
        this._error(error, id, 'flush');
      }
    }
    await this.drainNotifications();
    const fatal = failures.filter(({ record }) => record.required || options.throwOnError === true || this.errorPolicy === 'propagate');
    if (fatal.length) {
      const error = new AggregateError(fatal.map((entry) => entry.error), 'One or more required Monitor plugins failed during flush().');
      error.code = 'MONITOR_PLUGIN_FLUSH_FAILED';
      error.failures = fatal.map(({ record, error: failure }) => ({ plugin: record.id, required: record.required, error: errorRecord(failure) }));
      throw error;
    }
    return deepFreeze({ ok: failures.length === 0, failures: failures.map(({ record, error }) => ({ plugin: record.id, required: record.required, error: errorRecord(error) })), queue: this.notifications.snapshot() });
  }

  freeze() { this.frozen = true; return this; }
  get(id) { return this.plugins.get(String(id)) || null; }
  list() { return deepFreeze(Array.from(this.plugins.values(), publicRecord)); }
  snapshot() { return deepFreeze({ enabled: this.enabled, setupComplete: this.setupComplete, started: this.started, closed: this.closed, frozen: this.frozen, order: [...this.order], plugins: this.list(), hooks: Object.fromEntries(Array.from(this.hooks, ([name, handlers]) => [name, handlers.map((entry) => ({ plugin: entry.plugin, priority: entry.priority, once: entry.once }))])), notifications: this.notifications.snapshot(), stats: { ...this.stats } }); }

  async close(options = {}) {
    if (this.closed) return this.snapshot();
    const failures = [];
    try { await this.flush(options); }
    catch (error) { failures.push(error); }
    for (const id of [...this.order].reverse()) {
      const record = this.plugins.get(id);
      try { await record.plugin.close?.(createApi(this, this.runtime, record)); record.state = 'closed'; record.closedAt = Date.now(); this.stats.closes++; }
      catch (error) { record.error = errorRecord(error); this._error(error, id, 'close'); if (record.required || options.throwOnError === true || this.errorPolicy === 'propagate') failures.push(error); }
    }
    this.started = false;
    this.closed = true;
    this.notifications.close();
    this.hooks.clear();
    this.removeAllListeners();
    if (failures.length && (options.throwOnError === true || this.errorPolicy === 'propagate')) {
      const error = new AggregateError(failures, 'Monitor plugin close completed with lifecycle failures.');
      error.code = 'MONITOR_PLUGIN_CLOSE_FAILED';
      throw error;
    }
    return this.snapshot();
  }

  _scheduleNotificationDrain() {
    if (this.notificationDrainPromise || this.closed) return;
    this.notificationDrainPromise = new Promise((resolve) => queueMicrotask(resolve))
      .then(() => this._drainNotificationQueue())
      .catch((error) => this._error(error, 'manager', 'notification-drain'))
      .finally(() => {
        this.notificationDrainPromise = null;
        if (this.notifications.length && !this.closed) this._scheduleNotificationDrain();
      });
  }

  async _drainNotificationQueue() {
    let sliceStarted = nowNs();
    while (!this.closed && this.notifications.length) {
      const batch = this.notifications.shiftMany(this.hookBatchSize);
      this.stats.hookNotificationBatches++;
      for (const notification of batch) await this.invoke(notification.name, notification.payload, notification.context);
      if (this.notifications.length && elapsedMs(sliceStarted) >= this.hookBudgetMs) {
        await cooperativeYield();
        sliceStarted = nowNs();
      }
    }
  }

  _error(error, plugin, phase) { if (this.listenerCount('error') > 0) this.emit('error', error, plugin, phase); else this.emit('pluginError', { plugin, phase, error: errorRecord(error) }); }
}

function normalizePlugin(input, options) {
  let plugin = input;
  if (typeof input === 'function') {
    try { plugin = new input(options.options || {}); }
    catch { plugin = input(options.options || {}); }
  }
  if (!plugin || typeof plugin !== 'object') throw new TypeError('Monitor plugins must be objects, classes, or factories.');
  const id = String(options.id || plugin.id || plugin.name || plugin.constructor?.name || '').trim();
  if (!id) throw new TypeError('Monitor plugins require a stable id.');
  return { id, plugin, version: String(plugin.version || options.version || '0.0.0'), dependencies: Object.freeze(Array.from(new Set((plugin.dependencies || options.dependencies || []).map(String)))), optionalDependencies: Object.freeze(Array.from(new Set((plugin.optionalDependencies || options.optionalDependencies || []).map(String)))), priority: Number(plugin.priority ?? options.priority ?? 0), required: plugin.required === true || options.required === true, state: 'registered', error: null, registeredAt: Date.now(), setupAt: null, startedAt: null, closedAt: null };
}

function resolveOrder(plugins) {
  const visiting = new Set(); const visited = new Set(); const output = [];
  const visit = (id, trail = []) => {
    if (visited.has(id)) return;
    if (visiting.has(id)) throw Object.assign(new Error(`Monitor plugin dependency cycle: ${[...trail, id].join(' -> ')}`), { code: 'MONITOR_PLUGIN_CYCLE' });
    const record = plugins.get(id); if (!record) throw Object.assign(new Error(`Monitor plugin dependency '${id}' is not registered.`), { code: 'MONITOR_PLUGIN_DEPENDENCY_MISSING' });
    visiting.add(id);
    for (const dependency of record.dependencies) visit(dependency, [...trail, id]);
    for (const dependency of record.optionalDependencies) if (plugins.has(dependency)) visit(dependency, [...trail, id]);
    visiting.delete(id); visited.add(id); output.push(id);
  };
  const ids = Array.from(plugins.values()).sort((left, right) => right.priority - left.priority || left.id.localeCompare(right.id)).map((entry) => entry.id);
  for (const id of ids) visit(id);
  return output;
}

function createApi(manager, runtime, record) { return Object.freeze({ id: record.id, version: record.version, runtime, monitor: runtime, app: runtime?.app || null, config: runtime?.configuration || null, metrics: runtime?.metrics || null, tracer: runtime?.tracer || null, health: runtime?.healthRegistry || null, resources: runtime?.resources || null, alerts: runtime?.alerts || null, incidents: runtime?.incidents || null, slos: runtime?.slos || null, synthetics: runtime?.synthetics || null, anomalies: runtime?.anomalies || null, hook: (name, handler, options = {}) => manager.hook(name, handler, { ...options, plugin: record.id }), emit: (name, payload, context) => manager.invoke(name, payload, context), signal: (kind, payload, options) => runtime?.emitSignal?.(kind, payload, options), resolve: (key, fallback = null) => { try { return runtime?.app?.resolve?.(key) ?? fallback; } catch { return fallback; } } }); }
function publicRecord(record) { return { id: record.id, version: record.version, dependencies: record.dependencies, optionalDependencies: record.optionalDependencies, priority: record.priority, required: record.required, state: record.state, error: record.error, registeredAt: record.registeredAt, setupAt: record.setupAt, startedAt: record.startedAt, closedAt: record.closedAt }; }
export default MonitorPluginManager;

FILE PATH: ./synthetics/SyntheticCheck.js
================================================

'use strict';

import { boundedInteger, deepFreeze } from '../support.js';

const TYPES = new Set(['http', 'tcp', 'dns', 'custom']);

export class SyntheticCheck {
  constructor(input = {}, defaults = {}) {
    const id = String(input.id || input.name || '').trim();
    if (!id) throw new TypeError('Synthetic checks require an id.');
    const type = String(input.type || (input.url ? 'http' : input.port ? 'tcp' : typeof input.run === 'function' ? 'custom' : 'dns')).toLowerCase();
    if (!TYPES.has(type)) throw new TypeError(`Unsupported synthetic check type '${type}'.`);
    if (type === 'http') validateUrl(input.url);
    if (type === 'tcp' && (!input.host || !validPort(input.port))) throw new TypeError(`TCP synthetic check '${id}' requires host and port.`);
    if (type === 'dns' && !input.hostname && !input.host) throw new TypeError(`DNS synthetic check '${id}' requires hostname.`);
    if (type === 'custom' && typeof input.run !== 'function') throw new TypeError(`Custom synthetic check '${id}' requires run().`);
    this.id = id;
    this.name = String(input.name || id);
    this.type = type;
    this.enabled = input.enabled !== false;
    this.intervalMs = boundedInteger(input.intervalMs, defaults.intervalMs || 60_000, 1_000, 24 * 60 * 60_000);
    this.timeoutMs = boundedInteger(input.timeoutMs, defaults.timeoutMs || 10_000, 100, 10 * 60_000);
    this.jitterMs = boundedInteger(input.jitterMs, Math.min(5_000, Math.floor(this.intervalMs * 0.1)), 0, this.intervalMs);
    this.failureThreshold = boundedInteger(input.failureThreshold, 3, 1, 10_000);
    this.recoveryThreshold = boundedInteger(input.recoveryThreshold, 2, 1, 10_000);
    this.severity = String(input.severity || 'warning');
    this.critical = input.critical === true;
    this.alert = input.alert !== false;
    this.incident = input.incident === true || this.severity === 'critical';
    this.runbook = input.runbook || null;
    this.tags = Object.freeze(Array.from(new Set((input.tags || []).map(String))));
    this.options = deepFreeze({ ...input, run: type === 'custom' ? input.run : undefined });
    Object.freeze(this);
  }
}

function validateUrl(input) {
  let url;
  try { url = new URL(String(input || '')); } catch { throw new TypeError('HTTP synthetic checks require a valid absolute URL.'); }
  if (!['http:', 'https:'].includes(url.protocol)) throw new TypeError(`Unsupported synthetic URL protocol '${url.protocol}'.`);
  if (url.username || url.password) throw new TypeError('Synthetic URLs cannot contain embedded credentials.');
}
function validPort(value) { const number = Number(value); return Number.isSafeInteger(number) && number > 0 && number <= 65_535; }
export default SyntheticCheck;

'use strict';
export * from './SyntheticCheck.js';
export * from './SyntheticMonitor.js';


================================================
FILE PATH: ./synthetics/SyntheticMonitor.js

'use strict';

import { EventEmitter } from 'node:events';
import dns from 'node:dns/promises';
import http from 'node:http';
import https from 'node:https';
import net from 'node:net';
import { performance } from 'node:perf_hooks';
import { SignalKind } from '../constants.js';
import SyntheticCheck from './SyntheticCheck.js';
import { boundedInteger, deepFreeze, errorRecord, randomId, withTimeout } from '../support.js';

/** Scheduled zero-dependency HTTP/TCP/DNS/custom synthetic monitoring. */
export class SyntheticMonitor extends EventEmitter {
  constructor(options = {}) {
    super();
    this.enabled = options.enabled === true;
    this.signal = options.signal || null;
    this.registry = options.registry || null;
    this.defaultIntervalMs = boundedInteger(options.intervalMs || options.defaultIntervalMs, 60_000, 1_000, 24 * 60 * 60_000);
    this.defaultTimeoutMs = boundedInteger(options.timeoutMs, 10_000, 100, 10 * 60_000);
    this.maxChecks = boundedInteger(options.maxChecks, 10_000, 1, 100_000);
    this.concurrency = boundedInteger(options.concurrency, 8, 1, 1_024);
    this.historyLimit = boundedInteger(options.historyLimit, 5_000, 1, 1_000_000);
    this.maxResponseBytes = boundedInteger(options.maxResponseBytes, 256 * 1024, 1_024, 64 * 1024 * 1024);
    this.checks = new Map();
    this.states = new Map();
    this.history = [];
    this.timers = new Map();
    this.controllers = new Set();
    this.running = false;
    this.closed = false;
    this.frozen = false;
    this.inFlight = 0;
    this.waiters = [];
    this.stats = { runs: 0, succeeded: 0, failed: 0, skipped: 0, timeouts: 0, alerts: 0, recoveries: 0 };
    this.instruments = this.registry ? registerInstruments(this.registry) : null;
  }

  register(input, options = {}) {
    if (this.frozen) throw new Error('Synthetic monitor definitions are frozen.');
    const check = input instanceof SyntheticCheck ? input : new SyntheticCheck(input, { intervalMs: this.defaultIntervalMs, timeoutMs: this.defaultTimeoutMs });
    if (this.checks.size >= this.maxChecks && !this.checks.has(check.id)) throw new Error(`Synthetic check limit of ${this.maxChecks} exceeded.`);
    if (this.checks.has(check.id) && options.replace !== true) throw new Error(`Synthetic check '${check.id}' is already registered.`);
    this.checks.set(check.id, check);
    this.states.set(check.id, this.states.get(check.id) || state(check));
    if (this.running && check.enabled) this._schedule(check, options.immediate !== false ? 0 : check.intervalMs);
    return check;
  }

  unregister(id) {
    const key = String(id);
    clearTimeout(this.timers.get(key));
    this.timers.delete(key);
    this.states.delete(key);
    return this.checks.delete(key);
  }

  start(options = {}) {
    if (!this.enabled || this.running || this.closed) return this;
    this.running = true;
    for (const check of this.checks.values()) if (check.enabled) this._schedule(check, options.immediate === false ? check.intervalMs : randomJitter(check.jitterMs));
    return this;
  }

  _schedule(check, wait) {
    clearTimeout(this.timers.get(check.id));
    if (!this.running || this.closed || !check.enabled) return;
    const timer = setTimeout(async () => {
      this.timers.delete(check.id);
      try { await this.run(check.id); } catch {}
      this._schedule(check, check.intervalMs + randomJitter(check.jitterMs));
    }, Math.max(0, wait));
    timer.unref?.();
    this.timers.set(check.id, timer);
  }

  async run(id, options = {}) {
    const check = typeof id === 'string' ? this.checks.get(id) : id;
    if (!check) throw new Error(`Synthetic check '${String(id)}' is not registered.`);
    if (!check.enabled && options.force !== true) { this.stats.skipped++; return null; }
    await this._acquire(options.signal);
    const controller = new AbortController();
    this.controllers.add(controller);
    const external = options.signal;
    const abort = () => controller.abort(external.reason);
    external?.addEventListener?.('abort', abort, { once: true });
    const started = performance.now();
    const startedAt = Date.now();
    let result;
    try {
      const details = await withTimeout((signal) => execute(check, { signal, maxResponseBytes: this.maxResponseBytes }), options.timeoutMs || check.timeoutMs, { signal: controller.signal, message: `Synthetic check '${check.id}' exceeded ${options.timeoutMs || check.timeoutMs}ms.` });
      result = createResult(check, true, startedAt, performance.now() - started, details, null);
      this.stats.succeeded++;
    } catch (error) {
      result = createResult(check, false, startedAt, performance.now() - started, null, errorRecord(error));
      this.stats.failed++;
      if (error?.code === 'MONITOR_TIMEOUT') this.stats.timeouts++;
    } finally {
      external?.removeEventListener?.('abort', abort);
      this.controllers.delete(controller);
      this._release();
    }
    this.stats.runs++;
    this._record(result);
    this._transition(check, result);
    this.emit('result', result);
    this.signal?.(SignalKind.SYNTHETIC, result, { timestamp: startedAt, priority: result.ok ? 1 : check.severity === 'critical' ? 20 : 10 });
    return result;
  }

  async runAll(options = {}) {
    const checks = Array.from(this.checks.values()).filter((check) => check.enabled || options.force === true);
    return await Promise.all(checks.map((check) => this.run(check, options).catch((error) => ({ ok: false, checkId: check.id, error: errorRecord(error) }))));
  }

  _transition(check, result) {
    const current = this.states.get(check.id) || state(check);
    current.last = result;
    current.lastRunAt = result.startedAt;
    if (result.ok) {
      current.consecutiveSuccesses++;
      current.consecutiveFailures = 0;
      if (current.firing && current.consecutiveSuccesses >= check.recoveryThreshold) {
        current.firing = false;
        current.recoveredAt = Date.now();
        this.stats.recoveries++;
        const event = deepFreeze({ checkId: check.id, check: check.name, state: 'recovered', severity: check.severity, result, timestamp: Date.now() });
        this.emit('recovery', event);
        this.signal?.(SignalKind.SYNTHETIC, event, { priority: 2 });
      }
    } else {
      current.consecutiveFailures++;
      current.consecutiveSuccesses = 0;
      if (!current.firing && current.consecutiveFailures >= check.failureThreshold) {
        current.firing = true;
        current.firedAt = Date.now();
        this.stats.alerts++;
        const event = deepFreeze({ checkId: check.id, check: check.name, state: 'firing', severity: check.severity, result, timestamp: Date.now() });
        this.emit('failureThreshold', event);
        this.signal?.(SignalKind.SYNTHETIC, event, { priority: check.severity === 'critical' ? 20 : 10 });
      }
    }
    this.states.set(check.id, current);
  }

  _record(result) {
    this.history.push(result);
    if (this.history.length > this.historyLimit) this.history.splice(0, this.history.length - this.historyLimit);
    const labels = { check: result.checkId, type: result.type };
    this.instruments?.runs.inc({ ...labels, outcome: result.ok ? 'success' : 'failure' });
    this.instruments?.duration.record(result.durationMs / 1_000, labels);
    this.instruments?.up.set(result.ok ? 1 : 0, labels);
  }

  async _acquire(signal) {
    signal?.throwIfAborted?.();
    if (this.inFlight < this.concurrency) { this.inFlight++; return; }
    await new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      const waiter = { resolve, reject, signal, abort: null };
      waiter.abort = () => { const index = this.waiters.indexOf(waiter); if (index !== -1) this.waiters.splice(index, 1); reject(signal.reason instanceof Error ? signal.reason : Object.assign(new Error('Synthetic check was aborted.'), { code: 'ABORT_ERR' })); };
      signal?.addEventListener?.('abort', waiter.abort, { once: true });
      this.waiters.push(waiter);
    });
    this.inFlight++;
  }
  _release() { this.inFlight = Math.max(0, this.inFlight - 1); const waiter = this.waiters.shift(); if (waiter) { waiter.signal?.removeEventListener?.('abort', waiter.abort); waiter.resolve(); } }

  alertRule(id, options = {}) {
    const check = this.checks.get(String(id));
    if (!check) return null;
    return {
      id: options.id || `synthetic:${check.id}`,
      name: options.name || `Synthetic check failing: ${check.name}`,
      severity: options.severity || check.severity,
      source: 'custom',
      incident: options.incident ?? check.incident,
      runbook: options.runbook || check.runbook,
      tags: [...check.tags, 'synthetic'],
      evaluate: () => {
        const current = this.states.get(check.id);
        return { firing: current?.firing === true, value: current?.consecutiveFailures || 0, details: current || null };
      },
    };
  }

  health() {
    const states = Array.from(this.states.values());
    const criticalFailures = states.filter((entry) => entry.critical && entry.firing);
    const failures = states.filter((entry) => entry.firing);
    return deepFreeze({ ok: criticalFailures.length === 0, ready: criticalFailures.length === 0, live: !this.closed, degraded: failures.length > 0, state: this.running ? 'running' : this.closed ? 'closed' : 'idle', checks: states.length, failing: failures.map((entry) => entry.id), critical: criticalFailures.map((entry) => entry.id) });
  }

  freeze() { this.frozen = true; return this; }
  snapshot(options = {}) { return deepFreeze({ enabled: this.enabled, running: this.running, closed: this.closed, frozen: this.frozen, concurrency: this.concurrency, inFlight: this.inFlight, checks: Array.from(this.checks.values()), states: Array.from(this.states.values(), (entry) => ({ ...entry })), history: options.includeHistory ? this.history.slice(-(options.limit || 100)) : undefined, stats: { ...this.stats } }); }

  async close() {
    if (this.closed) return this.snapshot();
    this.running = false; this.closed = true;
    for (const timer of this.timers.values()) clearTimeout(timer);
    this.timers.clear();
    for (const controller of this.controllers) controller.abort(Object.assign(new Error('Synthetic monitor closed.'), { code: 'MONITOR_SYNTHETIC_CLOSED' }));
    this.controllers.clear();
    for (const waiter of this.waiters.splice(0)) waiter.reject(Object.assign(new Error('Synthetic monitor closed.'), { code: 'MONITOR_SYNTHETIC_CLOSED' }));
    this.removeAllListeners();
    return this.snapshot();
  }
}

async function execute(check, context) {
  if (check.type === 'http') return await httpCheck(check.options, context);
  if (check.type === 'tcp') return await tcpCheck(check.options, context);
  if (check.type === 'dns') return await dnsCheck(check.options, context);
  return await check.options.run({ check, signal: context.signal });
}

async function httpCheck(options, context, redirects = 0) {
  const url = new URL(options.url);
  const transport = url.protocol === 'https:' ? https : http;
  const timings = Object.create(null);
  const started = performance.now();
  return await new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    let settled = false; const chunks = []; let bytes = 0;
    const finish = (error, value) => { if (settled) return; settled = true; context.signal?.removeEventListener?.('abort', onAbort); error ? reject(error) : resolve(value); };
    const request = transport.request({ protocol: url.protocol, hostname: url.hostname, port: url.port || undefined, path: `${url.pathname}${url.search}`, method: String(options.method || 'GET').toUpperCase(), headers: options.headers || {}, rejectUnauthorized: options.rejectUnauthorized !== false, family: options.family, servername: options.servername }, (response) => {
      timings.ttfbMs = performance.now() - started;
      const status = Number(response.statusCode || 0);
      if (isRedirect(status) && options.followRedirects !== false && response.headers.location && redirects < Math.max(0, Number(options.maxRedirects) || 3)) {
        response.resume();
        const next = new URL(response.headers.location, url);
        if (!['http:', 'https:'].includes(next.protocol) || next.username || next.password) return finish(Object.assign(new Error('Synthetic redirect target is unsafe.'), { code: 'MONITOR_SYNTHETIC_REDIRECT_UNSAFE' }));
        return finish(null, httpCheck({ ...options, url: next.href }, context, redirects + 1));
      }
      response.on('data', (chunk) => { bytes += chunk.length; if (bytes <= context.maxResponseBytes) chunks.push(Buffer.from(chunk)); else request.destroy(Object.assign(new Error('Synthetic HTTP response exceeded the configured byte limit.'), { code: 'MONITOR_SYNTHETIC_RESPONSE_TOO_LARGE' })); });
      response.on('aborted', () => finish(Object.assign(new Error('Synthetic HTTP response aborted.'), { code: 'MONITOR_SYNTHETIC_ABORTED' })));
      response.on('end', () => {
        const body = Buffer.concat(chunks).toString(options.encoding || 'utf8');
        const okStatus = expectedStatus(status, options.expectedStatus ?? '2xx');
        const okBody = options.bodyIncludes == null || body.includes(String(options.bodyIncludes));
        if (!okStatus || !okBody) return finish(Object.assign(new Error(!okStatus ? `Unexpected HTTP status ${status}.` : 'Expected response content was not found.'), { code: !okStatus ? 'MONITOR_SYNTHETIC_STATUS' : 'MONITOR_SYNTHETIC_BODY', statusCode: status }));
        finish(null, { statusCode: status, headers: safeHeaders(response.headers), bytes, redirects, timings: { ...timings, totalMs: performance.now() - started }, bodyMatched: okBody });
      });
    });
    request.on('socket', (socket) => {
      const mark = (name) => { timings[name] = performance.now() - started; };
      socket.once('lookup', () => mark('dnsMs'));
      socket.once('connect', () => mark('connectMs'));
      socket.once('secureConnect', () => mark('tlsMs'));
    });
    request.once('error', (error) => finish(error));
    const onAbort = () => request.destroy(context.signal.reason instanceof Error ? context.signal.reason : Object.assign(new Error('Synthetic HTTP check aborted.'), { code: 'ABORT_ERR' }));
    context.signal?.addEventListener?.('abort', onAbort, { once: true });
    if (options.body != null) request.write(Buffer.isBuffer(options.body) ? options.body : String(options.body));
    request.end();
  });
}

async function tcpCheck(options, context) {
  const started = performance.now();
  return await new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    let settled = false; const chunks = []; let bytes = 0;
    const socket = net.createConnection({ host: options.host, port: Number(options.port), family: options.family });
    const finish = (error, value) => { if (settled) return; settled = true; context.signal?.removeEventListener?.('abort', onAbort); socket.destroy(); error ? reject(error) : resolve(value); };
    socket.once('connect', () => {
      if (options.write != null) socket.write(Buffer.isBuffer(options.write) ? options.write : String(options.write));
      if (options.expect == null) finish(null, { host: options.host, port: Number(options.port), connected: true, bytes: 0, durationMs: performance.now() - started });
    });
    socket.on('data', (chunk) => { bytes += chunk.length; if (bytes > context.maxResponseBytes) return finish(Object.assign(new Error('Synthetic TCP response exceeded the configured byte limit.'), { code: 'MONITOR_SYNTHETIC_RESPONSE_TOO_LARGE' })); chunks.push(Buffer.from(chunk)); const body = Buffer.concat(chunks).toString(options.encoding || 'utf8'); if (body.includes(String(options.expect))) finish(null, { host: options.host, port: Number(options.port), connected: true, bytes, matched: true, durationMs: performance.now() - started }); });
    socket.once('error', (error) => finish(error));
    socket.once('end', () => { if (options.expect != null) finish(Object.assign(new Error('Synthetic TCP expectation was not observed.'), { code: 'MONITOR_SYNTHETIC_EXPECTATION' })); });
    const onAbort = () => finish(context.signal.reason instanceof Error ? context.signal.reason : Object.assign(new Error('Synthetic TCP check aborted.'), { code: 'ABORT_ERR' }));
    context.signal?.addEventListener?.('abort', onAbort, { once: true });
  });
}

async function dnsCheck(options, context) {
  context.signal?.throwIfAborted?.();
  const hostname = String(options.hostname || options.host);
  if (options.recordType) {
    const values = await dns.resolve(hostname, String(options.recordType));
    return { hostname, recordType: String(options.recordType), records: values.length, values: options.includeValues === true ? values.slice(0, 100) : undefined };
  }
  const values = await dns.lookup(hostname, { all: true, family: options.family || 0, verbatim: true });
  return { hostname, records: values.length, addresses: options.includeValues === true ? values.slice(0, 100) : undefined };
}

function createResult(check, ok, startedAt, durationMs, details, error) { return deepFreeze({ id: randomId('synthetic'), checkId: check.id, check: check.name, type: check.type, ok, startedAt, completedAt: Date.now(), durationMs, severity: check.severity, critical: check.critical, tags: check.tags, details, error }); }
function state(check) { return { id: check.id, name: check.name, type: check.type, critical: check.critical, firing: false, consecutiveFailures: 0, consecutiveSuccesses: 0, lastRunAt: null, firedAt: null, recoveredAt: null, last: null }; }
function randomJitter(max) { return max > 0 ? Math.floor(Math.random() * (max + 1)) : 0; }
function isRedirect(status) { return [301, 302, 303, 307, 308].includes(status); }
function expectedStatus(status, expected) { if (Array.isArray(expected)) return expected.map(Number).includes(status); if (typeof expected === 'number') return status === expected; if (typeof expected === 'string' && /^\dxx$/i.test(expected)) return Math.floor(status / 100) === Number(expected[0]); if (expected && typeof expected === 'object') return status >= Number(expected.min || 100) && status <= Number(expected.max || 599); return status >= 200 && status < 300; }
function safeHeaders(headers) { const output = {}; for (const key of ['content-type', 'content-length', 'server', 'date', 'location']) if (headers[key] != null) output[key] = String(headers[key]).slice(0, 1024); return output; }
function registerInstruments(registry) { return { runs: registry.counter('monitor_synthetic_checks_total'), duration: registry.histogram('monitor_synthetic_duration_seconds', { unit: 'seconds' }), up: registry.gauge('monitor_synthetic_up') }; }
export default SyntheticMonitor;


================================================
FILE PATH: ./exporters/EventExporter.js
================================================

'use strict';

import { dispatchEvent } from '../support.js';

export class EventExporter {
  constructor(events, options = {}) { this.events = events; this.eventName = options.eventName || 'monitor:signals'; this.perSignal = options.perSignal === true; this.closed = false; this.stats = { batches: 0, signals: 0, rejected: 0 }; }
  async export(signals) {
    if (this.closed || !this.events) return false;
    let accepted = true;
    if (this.perSignal) for (const signal of signals) accepted = dispatchEvent(this.events, `monitor:signal:${signal.kind}`, signal) && accepted;
    else accepted = dispatchEvent(this.events, this.eventName, signals);
    this.stats.batches++; this.stats.signals += signals.length; if (!accepted) this.stats.rejected++;
    return accepted;
  }
  async close() { this.closed = true; }
  snapshot() { return Object.freeze({ eventName: this.eventName, perSignal: this.perSignal, closed: this.closed, stats: Object.freeze({ ...this.stats }) }); }
}
export default EventExporter;

'use strict';
export * from './CallbackExporter.js';
export * from './EventExporter.js';
export * from './HttpJsonExporter.js';
export * from './OtlpJsonExporter.js';
export * from './StatsdExporter.js';

'use strict';

import HttpJsonExporter from './HttpJsonExporter.js';

export class OtlpJsonExporter extends HttpJsonExporter {
  constructor(options = {}) { super({ ...options, format: options.format || ((signals) => formatOtlpJson(signals, options.resource || {})) }); }
}

export function formatOtlpJson(signals = [], resource = {}) {
  const spans = signals.filter((signal) => signal.kind === 'span').map((signal) => signal.payload || signal);
  const metrics = signals.filter((signal) => signal.kind === 'metrics').flatMap((signal) => toOtlpMetrics(signal.payload || signal));
  const logs = signals.filter((signal) => ['event', 'log', 'alert', 'incident', 'health', 'resource'].includes(signal.kind)).map(toOtlpLog);
  const output = { resource };
  if (spans.length) output.resourceSpans = [{ resource: { attributes: attributes(resource) }, scopeSpans: [{ scope: { name: 'rawnode-monitor', version: '1.0.0' }, spans: spans.map(toOtlpSpan) }] }];
  if (metrics.length) output.resourceMetrics = [{ resource: { attributes: attributes(resource) }, scopeMetrics: [{ scope: { name: 'rawnode-monitor', version: '1.0.0' }, metrics }] }];
  if (logs.length) output.resourceLogs = [{ resource: { attributes: attributes(resource) }, scopeLogs: [{ scope: { name: 'rawnode-monitor', version: '1.0.0' }, logRecords: logs }] }];
  return output;
}

function toOtlpSpan(span) {
  return {
    traceId: span.traceId,
    spanId: span.spanId,
    parentSpanId: span.parentSpanId || undefined,
    name: span.name,
    kind: spanKind(span.kind),
    startTimeUnixNano: span.startTimeUnixNano,
    endTimeUnixNano: span.endTimeUnixNano,
    attributes: attributes(span.attributes),
    events: (span.events || []).map((event) => ({ name: event.name, timeUnixNano: String(BigInt(event.timestamp) * 1_000_000n), attributes: attributes(event.attributes) })),
    links: (span.links || []).map((link) => ({ traceId: link.traceId, spanId: link.spanId, attributes: attributes(link.attributes) })),
    status: { code: span.status?.code === 'error' ? 2 : span.status?.code === 'ok' ? 1 : 0, message: span.status?.message || undefined },
  };
}
function toOtlpMetrics(snapshot) {
  const output = [];
  for (const metric of Object.values(snapshot?.metrics || {})) {
    if (metric.type === 'histogram' || metric.type === 'timer') {
      output.push({ name: metric.name, description: metric.description, unit: metric.unit, histogram: { aggregationTemporality: 2, dataPoints: (metric.series || []).map((series) => ({ attributes: attributes(series.labels), timeUnixNano: String(BigInt(series.updatedAt || Date.now()) * 1_000_000n), count: String(series.count), sum: series.sum, bucketCounts: (series.buckets || []).map((bucket, index, all) => String(index ? bucket.count - all[index - 1].count : bucket.count)), explicitBounds: (series.buckets || []).map((bucket) => bucket.upperBound), min: series.min, max: series.max })) } });
    } else {
      output.push({ name: metric.name, description: metric.description, unit: metric.unit, gauge: { dataPoints: (metric.series || []).map((series) => ({ attributes: attributes(series.labels), timeUnixNano: String(BigInt(series.updatedAt || Date.now()) * 1_000_000n), asDouble: series.value })) } });
    }
  }
  return output;
}
function toOtlpLog(signal) { return { timeUnixNano: String(BigInt(signal.timestamp || Date.now()) * 1_000_000n), observedTimeUnixNano: String(BigInt(signal.observedAt || Date.now()) * 1_000_000n), severityText: signal.payload?.severity || signal.payload?.level || signal.kind.toUpperCase(), body: { stringValue: JSON.stringify(signal.payload) }, attributes: attributes({ kind: signal.kind, service: signal.service, environment: signal.environment, tenantId: signal.tenantId }), traceId: signal.traceId || undefined, spanId: signal.spanId || undefined }; }
function attributes(values = {}) { return Object.entries(values || {}).filter(([, value]) => value != null).map(([key, value]) => ({ key, value: anyValue(value) })); }
function anyValue(value) { if (typeof value === 'boolean') return { boolValue: value }; if (typeof value === 'number') return Number.isInteger(value) ? { intValue: String(value) } : { doubleValue: value }; if (Array.isArray(value)) return { arrayValue: { values: value.map(anyValue) } }; if (value && typeof value === 'object') return { kvlistValue: { values: attributes(value) } }; return { stringValue: String(value) }; }
function spanKind(kind) { return { internal: 1, server: 2, client: 3, producer: 4, consumer: 5 }[kind] || 1; }
export default OtlpJsonExporter;

'use strict';

import { errorRecord } from '../support.js';

export class ExporterRegistry {
  constructor(options = {}) {
    this.exporters = new Map();
    this.frozen = false;
    this.errorPolicy = options.errorPolicy || 'isolate';
  }

  register(name, exporter, options = {}) {
    if (this.frozen) throw new Error('Monitor exporter registry is frozen.');
    if (!name || !exporter) throw new TypeError('register(name, exporter) requires both values.');
    const key = String(name);
    if (this.exporters.has(key) && options.replace !== true) throw new Error(`Monitor exporter '${name}' is already registered.`);
    this.exporters.set(key, {
      name: key,
      exporter,
      required: options.required === true,
      signals: options.signals || null,
      priority: Number(options.priority || 0),
    });
    return exporter;
  }

  remove(name) { return this.exporters.delete(String(name)); }
  get(name) { return this.exporters.get(String(name))?.exporter || null; }
  list() { return Object.freeze(Array.from(this.exporters.values(), (entry) => ({ name: entry.name, required: entry.required, signals: entry.signals, priority: entry.priority, snapshot: entry.exporter.snapshot?.() || null }))); }
  freeze() { this.frozen = true; return this; }
  attach(pipeline) { for (const entry of this.exporters.values()) pipeline.addSink(`exporter:${entry.name}`, entry.exporter, entry); return pipeline; }
  async flush(options = {}) { return await this._lifecycle('flush', Array.from(this.exporters.values()), options); }
  async close(options = {}) { return await this._lifecycle('close', Array.from(this.exporters.values()).reverse(), options); }
  snapshot() { return Object.freeze({ frozen: this.frozen, errorPolicy: this.errorPolicy, exporters: this.list() }); }

  async _lifecycle(method, entries, options) {
    const settled = await Promise.allSettled(entries.map((entry) => entry.exporter[method]?.(options)));
    const failures = [];
    for (let index = 0; index < settled.length; index++) {
      const result = settled[index];
      if (result.status !== 'rejected') continue;
      failures.push({
        name: entries[index].name,
        required: entries[index].required,
        error: result.reason,
      });
    }
    const fatal = failures.filter((failure) => failure.required || options.throwOnError === true || this.errorPolicy === 'propagate');
    if (fatal.length) {
      const error = new AggregateError(fatal.map((failure) => failure.error), `One or more required Monitor exporters failed during ${method}().`);
      error.code = `MONITOR_EXPORTER_${method.toUpperCase()}_FAILED`;
      error.failures = fatal.map((failure) => ({ name: failure.name, required: failure.required, error: errorRecord(failure.error) }));
      throw error;
    }
    return Object.freeze({
      ok: failures.length === 0,
      operation: method,
      exporters: entries.length,
      failures: Object.freeze(failures.map((failure) => ({ name: failure.name, required: failure.required, error: errorRecord(failure.error) }))),
    });
  }
}
export default ExporterRegistry;

FILE PATH: ./exporters/StatsdExporter.js

'use strict';

import dgram from 'node:dgram';
import { sanitizeMetricName } from '../support.js';

export class StatsdExporter {
  constructor(options = {}) {
    this.host = options.host || '127.0.0.1';
    this.port = Math.max(1, Number(options.port) || 8125);
    this.prefix = sanitizeMetricName(options.prefix || 'rawnode').replace(/[:]/g, '.');
    this.maxPacketBytes = Math.max(512, Number(options.maxPacketBytes) || 8_192);
    this.socket = dgram.createSocket(options.type || 'udp4');
    this.closed = false;
    this.stats = { packets: 0, lines: 0, bytes: 0, errors: 0 };
    this.socket.on('error', () => { this.stats.errors++; });
    this.socket.unref?.();
  }

  async export(signals) {
    if (this.closed) return false;
    const lines = [];
    for (const signal of signals) if (signal.kind === 'metrics') lines.push(...metricsToStatsd(signal.payload || signal, this.prefix));
    if (!lines.length) return true;
    let packet = '';
    for (const line of lines) {
      if (Buffer.byteLength(packet ? `${packet}\n${line}` : line) > this.maxPacketBytes && packet) { await this._send(packet); packet = line; }
      else packet = packet ? `${packet}\n${line}` : line;
    }
    if (packet) await this._send(packet);
    this.stats.lines += lines.length;
    return true;
  }

  _send(text) {
    const buffer = Buffer.from(text);
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => this.socket.send(buffer, this.port, this.host, (error) => {
      if (error) { this.stats.errors++; reject(error); }
      else { this.stats.packets++; this.stats.bytes += buffer.length; resolve(true); }
    }));
  }
  async close() { if (this.closed) return; this.closed = true; await new Promise((resolve) => { try { this.socket.close(resolve); } catch { resolve(); } }); }
  snapshot() { return Object.freeze({ host: this.host, port: this.port, prefix: this.prefix, closed: this.closed, stats: Object.freeze({ ...this.stats }) }); }
}
function metricsToStatsd(snapshot, prefix) {
  const lines = [];
  for (const metric of Object.values(snapshot?.metrics || {})) for (const series of metric.series || []) {
    const name = `${prefix}.${sanitizeMetricName(metric.name).replace(/[:]/g, '.')}`;
    const tags = Object.entries(series.labels || {}).map(([key, value]) => `${key}:${String(value).replace(/[|,@]/g, '_')}`).join(',');
    const suffix = tags ? `|#${tags}` : '';
    if (metric.type === 'counter') lines.push(`${name}:${series.value}|c${suffix}`);
    else if (metric.type === 'histogram' || metric.type === 'timer') lines.push(`${name}:${series.mean || 0}|h${suffix}`);
    else lines.push(`${name}:${series.value}|g${suffix}`);
  }
  return lines;
}
export default StatsdExporter;


================================================
FILE PATH: ./exporters/HttpJsonExporter.js
================================================

'use strict';

import http from 'node:http';
import https from 'node:https';
import { promisify } from 'node:util';
import { gzip } from 'node:zlib';
import { createHmac } from 'node:crypto';
import { bytesOf, retry } from '../support.js';

const gzipAsync = promisify(gzip);
const SUPPORTED_PROTOCOLS = new Set(['http:', 'https:']);

export class HttpJsonExporter {
  constructor(options = {}) {
    if (!options.url) throw new TypeError('HttpJsonExporter requires url.');
    this.url = new URL(options.url);
    if (!SUPPORTED_PROTOCOLS.has(this.url.protocol)) throw new TypeError(`Unsupported Monitor HTTP exporter protocol '${this.url.protocol}'.`);
    if (this.url.username || this.url.password) throw new TypeError('Monitor HTTP exporter URLs must not contain embedded credentials. Use headers or signing instead.');
    this.timeoutMs = Math.max(100, Number(options.timeoutMs) || 10_000);
    this.compression = options.compression !== false;
    this.compressionThresholdBytes = Math.max(256, Number(options.compressionThresholdBytes) || 1_024);
    this.maxAttempts = Math.max(1, Number(options.maxAttempts) || 3);
    this.retryBaseMs = Math.max(0, Number(options.retryBaseMs) || 100);
    this.retryMaxMs = Math.max(this.retryBaseMs, Number(options.retryMaxMs) || 5_000);
    this.maxPayloadBytes = Math.max(1_024, Number(options.maxPayloadBytes) || 8 * 1024 * 1024);
    this.maxResponseBytes = Math.max(1_024, Number(options.maxResponseBytes) || 64 * 1024);
    this.maxBatchSignals = Math.max(1, Number(options.maxBatchSignals) || 256);
    this.rejectUnauthorized = options.rejectUnauthorized !== false;
    this.headers = Object.freeze({ 'content-type': 'application/json', 'user-agent': 'rawnode-monitor/1.0', ...(options.headers || {}) });
    this.secret = options.secret || null;
    this.format = options.format || ((signals) => ({ schemaVersion: 1, sentAt: Date.now(), signals }));
    this.agent = this.url.protocol === 'https:'
      ? new https.Agent({ keepAlive: true, maxSockets: Number(options.maxSockets) || 16, maxFreeSockets: Number(options.maxFreeSockets) || 8, rejectUnauthorized: this.rejectUnauthorized })
      : new http.Agent({ keepAlive: true, maxSockets: Number(options.maxSockets) || 16, maxFreeSockets: Number(options.maxFreeSockets) || 8 });
    this.closed = false;
    this.stats = {
      batches: 0,
      signals: 0,
      bytes: 0,
      uncompressedBytes: 0,
      splitBatches: 0,
      oversizedSignals: 0,
      errors: 0,
      retries: 0,
      responses: Object.create(null),
    };
  }

  async export(signals = [], options = {}) {
    if (this.closed) return false;
    if (!Array.isArray(signals) || !signals.length) return true;
    const chunks = partitionSignals(signals, this.maxBatchSignals, this.maxPayloadBytes);
    if (chunks.length > 1) this.stats.splitBatches += chunks.length - 1;
    for (const chunk of chunks) await this._exportChunk(chunk, options);
    return true;
  }

  async _exportChunk(signals, options) {
    let body;
    try { body = Buffer.from(JSON.stringify(this.format(signals, options))); }
    catch (cause) {
      const error = Object.assign(new Error('Monitor HTTP exporter could not serialize a telemetry batch.'), { code: 'MONITOR_EXPORT_SERIALIZATION_FAILED', retryable: false, cause });
      this.stats.errors++;
      throw error;
    }

    if (body.length > this.maxPayloadBytes) {
      if (signals.length > 1) {
        const middle = Math.ceil(signals.length / 2);
        this.stats.splitBatches++;
        await this._exportChunk(signals.slice(0, middle), options);
        await this._exportChunk(signals.slice(middle), options);
        return;
      }
      this.stats.oversizedSignals++;
      this.stats.errors++;
      throw Object.assign(new Error(`Monitor HTTP export signal exceeds ${this.maxPayloadBytes} bytes after formatting.`), {
        code: 'MONITOR_EXPORT_PAYLOAD_TOO_LARGE',
        retryable: false,
        bytes: body.length,
        maxPayloadBytes: this.maxPayloadBytes,
      });
    }

    const payload = this.compression && body.length >= this.compressionThresholdBytes ? await gzipAsync(body) : body;
    const headers = { ...this.headers, 'content-length': String(payload.length) };
    if (payload !== body) headers['content-encoding'] = 'gzip';
    if (this.secret) {
      const timestamp = String(Date.now());
      headers['x-rawnode-monitor-timestamp'] = timestamp;
      headers['x-rawnode-monitor-signature'] = `sha256=${createHmac('sha256', this.secret).update(timestamp).update('.').update(payload).digest('hex')}`;
    }

    try {
      await retry(async (attempt) => {
        if (attempt > 1) this.stats.retries++;
        const response = await this._request(payload, headers, options.signal);
        if (response.statusCode < 200 || response.statusCode >= 300) {
          throw Object.assign(new Error(`Monitor exporter received HTTP ${response.statusCode}.`), {
            statusCode: response.statusCode,
            retryable: response.statusCode >= 500 || response.statusCode === 429,
          });
        }
        return response;
      }, {
        attempts: this.maxAttempts,
        baseMs: this.retryBaseMs,
        maxMs: this.retryMaxMs,
        signal: options.signal,
        retryWhen: (error) => error?.retryable !== false,
      });
    } catch (error) {
      this.stats.errors++;
      throw error;
    }

    this.stats.batches++;
    this.stats.signals += signals.length;
    this.stats.bytes += payload.length;
    this.stats.uncompressedBytes += body.length;
  }

  _request(payload, headers, signal) {
    const transport = this.url.protocol === 'https:' ? https : http;
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      let settled = false;
      let abortHandler = null;
      const finish = (error, value) => {
        if (settled) return;
        settled = true;
        if (abortHandler) signal?.removeEventListener?.('abort', abortHandler);
        if (error) reject(error);
        else resolve(value);
      };
      const request = transport.request({
        protocol: this.url.protocol,
        hostname: this.url.hostname,
        port: this.url.port || undefined,
        path: `${this.url.pathname}${this.url.search}`,
        method: 'POST',
        headers,
        agent: this.agent,
        rejectUnauthorized: this.rejectUnauthorized,
      }, (response) => {
        let bytes = 0;
        response.on('data', (chunk) => {
          bytes += chunk.length;
          if (bytes > this.maxResponseBytes) {
            const error = Object.assign(new Error(`Monitor exporter response exceeds ${this.maxResponseBytes} bytes.`), {
              code: 'MONITOR_EXPORT_RESPONSE_TOO_LARGE',
              retryable: false,
            });
            finish(error);
            response.destroy(error);
          }
        });
        response.once('aborted', () => finish(Object.assign(new Error('Monitor exporter response was aborted.'), { code: 'MONITOR_EXPORT_RESPONSE_ABORTED', retryable: true })));
        response.once('error', (error) => finish(error));
        response.once('end', () => {
          const statusCode = Number(response.statusCode || 0);
          this.stats.responses[statusCode] = (this.stats.responses[statusCode] || 0) + 1;
          finish(null, { statusCode, bytes });
        });
      });
      request.setTimeout(this.timeoutMs, () => request.destroy(Object.assign(new Error(`Monitor HTTP export exceeded ${this.timeoutMs}ms.`), { code: 'MONITOR_EXPORT_TIMEOUT', retryable: true })));
      request.once('error', (error) => finish(error));
      if (signal) {
        abortHandler = () => {
          const error = signal.reason instanceof Error
            ? signal.reason
            : Object.assign(new Error('Monitor export aborted.'), { code: 'ABORT_ERR' });
          request.destroy(error);
          finish(error);
        };
        if (signal.aborted) abortHandler();
        else signal.addEventListener('abort', abortHandler, { once: true });
      }
      if (!settled) request.end(payload);
    });
  }

  async flush() {}
  async close() { this.closed = true; this.agent.destroy(); }
  snapshot() {
    return Object.freeze({
      url: `${this.url.origin}${this.url.pathname}`,
      closed: this.closed,
      compression: this.compression,
      maxPayloadBytes: this.maxPayloadBytes,
      maxResponseBytes: this.maxResponseBytes,
      maxBatchSignals: this.maxBatchSignals,
      stats: Object.freeze({ ...this.stats, responses: Object.freeze({ ...this.stats.responses }) }),
    });
  }
}

function partitionSignals(signals, maxSignals, maxBytes) {
  const output = [];
  let batch = [];
  let estimated = 128;
  for (const signal of signals) {
    const size = bytesOf(signal, maxBytes + 1) + 1;
    if (size > maxBytes) {
      if (batch.length) { output.push(batch); batch = []; estimated = 128; }
      output.push([signal]);
      continue;
    }
    if (batch.length && (batch.length >= maxSignals || estimated + size > Math.floor(maxBytes * 0.9))) {
      output.push(batch);
      batch = [];
      estimated = 128;
    }
    batch.push(signal);
    estimated += size;
  }
  if (batch.length) output.push(batch);
  return output;
}

export default HttpJsonExporter;

'use strict';
export class CallbackExporter {
  constructor(callback, options = {}) { if (typeof callback !== 'function') throw new TypeError('CallbackExporter requires a callback.'); this.callback = callback; this.name = options.name || 'callback'; this.closed = false; this.stats = { batches: 0, signals: 0, errors: 0 }; }
  async export(signals, options = {}) { if (this.closed) return false; try { await this.callback(signals, options); this.stats.batches++; this.stats.signals += signals.length; return true; } catch (error) { this.stats.errors++; throw error; } }
  async flush() {}
  async close() { this.closed = true; }
  snapshot() { return Object.freeze({ name: this.name, closed: this.closed, stats: Object.freeze({ ...this.stats }) }); }
}
export default CallbackExporter;


================================================
FILE PATH: ./health/HealthRegistry.js
================================================

'use strict';

import { EventEmitter } from 'node:events';
import { HealthStatus } from '../constants.js';
import { deepFreeze, errorRecord, withTimeout } from '../support.js';

export class HealthRegistry extends EventEmitter {
  constructor(options = {}) {
    super();
    this.timeoutMs = Math.max(1, Number(options.timeoutMs) || 5_000);
    this.concurrency = Math.max(1, Number(options.concurrency) || 16);
    this.historyLimit = Math.max(1, Number(options.historyLimit) || 256);
    this.cacheTtlMs = Math.max(0, Number(options.cacheTtlMs) || 0);
    this.checks = new Map();
    this.history = [];
    this.cache = new Map();
    this.frozen = false;
    this.stats = { runs: 0, checks: 0, failures: 0, timeouts: 0, cacheHits: 0 };
  }

  register(name, check, options = {}) {
    if (!name || typeof check !== 'function') throw new TypeError('HealthRegistry.register(name, check) requires a function.');
    if (this.frozen) throw new Error('Health registry is frozen.');
    const key = String(name);
    if (this.checks.has(key) && options.replace !== true) throw new Error(`Health check '${key}' is already registered.`);
    const record = Object.freeze({
      name: key,
      check,
      kind: options.kind || 'dependency',
      critical: options.critical !== false,
      timeoutMs: Math.max(1, Number(options.timeoutMs) || this.timeoutMs),
      tags: Object.freeze(Array.from(new Set(options.tags || []))),
      description: options.description || '',
      cacheTtlMs: Math.max(0, Number(options.cacheTtlMs ?? this.cacheTtlMs) || 0),
      enabled: options.enabled !== false,
    });
    this.checks.set(key, record);
    this.emit('registered', publicCheck(record));
    return () => this.unregister(key);
  }

  unregister(name) { this.cache.delete(String(name)); return this.checks.delete(String(name)); }
  freeze() { this.frozen = true; return this; }
  list() { return Object.freeze(Array.from(this.checks.values(), publicCheck)); }

  async ready(context = {}) { return await this.run({ ...context, kinds: ['readiness', 'dependency'] }); }
  async live(context = {}) { return await this.run({ ...context, kinds: ['liveness'] }); }
  async all(context = {}) { return await this.run(context); }

  async run(options = {}) {
    const started = process.hrtime.bigint();
    const kinds = options.kinds ? new Set(options.kinds) : null;
    const tags = options.tags ? new Set(options.tags) : null;
    const selected = Array.from(this.checks.values()).filter((record) => record.enabled && (!kinds || kinds.has(record.kind)) && (!tags || record.tags.some((tag) => tags.has(tag))));
    const results = new Array(selected.length);
    let cursor = 0;
    this.stats.runs++;
    await Promise.all(Array.from({ length: Math.min(this.concurrency, selected.length || 1) }, async () => {
      while (cursor < selected.length) {
        const index = cursor++;
        results[index] = await this._runCheck(selected[index], options);
      }
    }));

    const criticalFailures = results.filter((item) => item.critical && item.status === HealthStatus.UNHEALTHY);
    const degraded = results.filter((item) => item.status === HealthStatus.DEGRADED || (!item.critical && item.status === HealthStatus.UNHEALTHY));
    const report = deepFreeze({
      ok: criticalFailures.length === 0,
      ready: criticalFailures.length === 0,
      live: criticalFailures.length === 0,
      status: criticalFailures.length ? HealthStatus.UNHEALTHY : degraded.length ? HealthStatus.DEGRADED : HealthStatus.HEALTHY,
      timestamp: Date.now(),
      durationMs: Number(process.hrtime.bigint() - started) / 1e6,
      counts: {
        total: results.length,
        healthy: results.filter((item) => item.status === HealthStatus.HEALTHY).length,
        degraded: degraded.length,
        unhealthy: results.filter((item) => item.status === HealthStatus.UNHEALTHY).length,
        skipped: results.filter((item) => item.status === HealthStatus.SKIPPED).length,
      },
      checks: results,
    });
    this.history.push(report);
    if (this.history.length > this.historyLimit) this.history.splice(0, this.history.length - this.historyLimit);
    this.emit('report', report);
    return report;
  }

  async _runCheck(record, options) {
    const cached = this.cache.get(record.name);
    if (cached && cached.expiresAt > Date.now() && options.fresh !== true) { this.stats.cacheHits++; return cached.value; }
    const started = process.hrtime.bigint();
    this.stats.checks++;
    let result;
    try {
      const value = await withTimeout((signal) => record.check({ ...options, signal, name: record.name, kind: record.kind }), record.timeoutMs, { signal: options.signal, message: `Health check '${record.name}' exceeded ${record.timeoutMs}ms.` });
      result = normalizeResult(record, value, Number(process.hrtime.bigint() - started) / 1e6);
    } catch (error) {
      this.stats.failures++;
      if (error?.code === 'MONITOR_TIMEOUT') this.stats.timeouts++;
      result = deepFreeze({
        name: record.name,
        kind: record.kind,
        critical: record.critical,
        status: HealthStatus.UNHEALTHY,
        ok: false,
        ready: false,
        live: false,
        durationMs: Number(process.hrtime.bigint() - started) / 1e6,
        timestamp: Date.now(),
        message: error?.message || String(error),
        error: errorRecord(error, { stack: options.includeStack === true }),
        details: null,
        tags: record.tags,
      });
    }
    if (record.cacheTtlMs > 0) this.cache.set(record.name, { value: result, expiresAt: Date.now() + record.cacheTtlMs });
    this.emit('check', result);
    return result;
  }

  latest() { return this.history.at(-1) || null; }
  historySnapshot(options = {}) { return Object.freeze(this.history.slice(-(Math.max(1, Number(options.limit) || this.historyLimit)))); }
  snapshot() { return deepFreeze({ frozen: this.frozen, checks: this.list(), latest: this.latest(), history: this.history.length, stats: { ...this.stats } }); }
}

function normalizeResult(record, value, durationMs) {
  if (value === false) value = { ok: false };
  else if (value === true || value == null) value = { ok: true };
  else if (typeof value !== 'object') value = { ok: Boolean(value), value };
  let status = value.status;
  if (!Object.values(HealthStatus).includes(status)) status = value.ok === false || value.ready === false || value.live === false ? HealthStatus.UNHEALTHY : value.degraded === true ? HealthStatus.DEGRADED : HealthStatus.HEALTHY;
  return deepFreeze({
    name: record.name,
    kind: record.kind,
    critical: record.critical,
    status,
    ok: status !== HealthStatus.UNHEALTHY,
    ready: value.ready ?? status !== HealthStatus.UNHEALTHY,
    live: value.live ?? status !== HealthStatus.UNHEALTHY,
    durationMs,
    timestamp: Date.now(),
    message: value.message || null,
    details: value.details ?? value.snapshot ?? value.value ?? null,
    tags: record.tags,
  });
}

function publicCheck(record) { return Object.freeze({ name: record.name, kind: record.kind, critical: record.critical, timeoutMs: record.timeoutMs, tags: record.tags, description: record.description, enabled: record.enabled }); }

export default HealthRegistry;

'use strict';

import { EventEmitter } from 'node:events';
import { RAWNODE_RESOURCE_CATALOG } from './RawNodeResourceCatalog.js';
import { callFirst, deepFreeze, errorRecord, safeResolve, withTimeout } from '../support.js';

export class ResourceRegistry extends EventEmitter {
  constructor(options = {}) {
    super();
    this.app = options.app || null;
    this.timeoutMs = Math.max(1, Number(options.timeoutMs) || 5_000);
    this.maxPayloadBytes = Math.max(1_024, Number(options.maxPayloadBytes) || 512 * 1024);
    this.serializer = options.serializer || null;
    this.healthRegistry = options.healthRegistry || null;
    this.resources = new Map();
    this.latest = new Map();
    this.frozen = false;
    this.stats = { registered: 0, discovered: 0, collections: 0, errors: 0, oversized: 0 };
  }

  register(name, resource, options = {}) {
    if (!name || !resource) throw new TypeError('ResourceRegistry.register(name, resource) requires both values.');
    if (this.frozen) throw new Error('Resource registry is frozen.');
    const key = String(name).toLowerCase();
    if (this.resources.has(key) && options.replace !== true) return this.resources.get(key);
    const record = {
      name: key,
      resource,
      critical: options.critical === true,
      required: options.required === true,
      health: options.health || null,
      metrics: options.metrics || null,
      diagnostics: options.diagnostics || null,
      timeoutMs: Math.max(1, Number(options.timeoutMs) || this.timeoutMs),
      tags: Object.freeze(Array.from(new Set(options.tags || []))),
      sourceBinding: options.sourceBinding || null,
    };
    this.resources.set(key, record);
    this.stats.registered++;
    if (this.healthRegistry && (record.health || hasMethod(resource, ['health', 'readiness', 'liveness', 'ping']))) {
      this.healthRegistry.register(`resource.${key}`, async () => {
        const snapshot = await this.collectOne(key, { metrics: false, health: true });
        return snapshot.health || { ok: !snapshot.error, details: snapshot };
      }, { kind: 'dependency', critical: record.critical || record.required, timeoutMs: record.timeoutMs, tags: ['resource', key] });
    }
    this.emit('registered', publicRecord(record));
    return record;
  }

  unregister(name) { this.latest.delete(String(name).toLowerCase()); return this.resources.delete(String(name).toLowerCase()); }
  freeze() { this.frozen = true; return this; }
  list() { return Object.freeze(Array.from(this.resources.values(), publicRecord)); }

  discover(app = this.app, options = {}) {
    const include = options.include?.length ? new Set(options.include.map((name) => String(name).toLowerCase())) : null;
    const exclude = new Set((options.exclude || []).map((name) => String(name).toLowerCase()));
    const required = new Set((options.required || []).map((name) => String(name).toLowerCase()));
    const discovered = [];
    for (const descriptor of options.catalog || RAWNODE_RESOURCE_CATALOG) {
      if ((include && !include.has(descriptor.name)) || exclude.has(descriptor.name) || this.resources.has(descriptor.name)) continue;
      let resource = null;
      let binding = null;
      for (const candidate of descriptor.bindings) {
        resource = safeResolve(app, candidate, null);
        if (resource != null) { binding = candidate; break; }
      }
      if (resource == null) continue;
      const record = this.register(descriptor.name, resource, {
        required: required.has(descriptor.name),
        critical: required.has(descriptor.name),
        sourceBinding: binding,
        health: () => callResource(resource, descriptor.healthMethods),
        metrics: () => callResource(resource, descriptor.metricMethods),
      });
      discovered.push(publicRecord(record));
      this.stats.discovered++;
    }
    return Object.freeze(discovered);
  }

  async collect(options = {}) {
    const output = Object.create(null);
    const names = options.names ? new Set(options.names.map((name) => String(name).toLowerCase())) : null;
    for (const name of this.resources.keys()) {
      if (!names || names.has(name)) output[name] = await this.collectOne(name, options);
    }
    return deepFreeze({ timestamp: Date.now(), resources: output });
  }

  async collectOne(name, options = {}) {
    const key = String(name).toLowerCase();
    const record = this.resources.get(key);
    if (!record) return null;
    this.stats.collections++;
    const started = process.hrtime.bigint();
    const snapshot = { name: key, timestamp: Date.now(), sourceBinding: record.sourceBinding, health: null, metrics: null, diagnostics: null, error: null };
    try {
      await withTimeout(async (signal) => {
        if (options.health !== false) snapshot.health = await invoke(record.health, record.resource, ['health', 'readiness', 'liveness', 'ping'], signal);
        if (options.metrics !== false) snapshot.metrics = await invoke(record.metrics, record.resource, ['metrics', 'snapshot', 'stats', 'getStats'], signal);
        if (options.diagnostics === true) snapshot.diagnostics = await invoke(record.diagnostics, record.resource, ['diagnostics', 'inspect'], signal);
      }, record.timeoutMs, { signal: options.signal, message: `Monitor resource '${key}' collection exceeded ${record.timeoutMs}ms.` });
    } catch (error) {
      this.stats.errors++;
      snapshot.error = errorRecord(error, { stack: options.includeStack === true });
      if (record.required && options.throwOnRequired !== false) throw error;
    }
    snapshot.durationMs = Number(process.hrtime.bigint() - started) / 1e6;
    const sanitized = this.serializer?.sanitize ? this.serializer.sanitize(snapshot, { maxPayloadBytes: this.maxPayloadBytes }) : snapshot;
    this.latest.set(key, sanitized);
    this.emit('snapshot', sanitized);
    return sanitized;
  }

  latestSnapshot(name = null) { return name ? this.latest.get(String(name).toLowerCase()) || null : deepFreeze(Object.fromEntries(this.latest)); }
  snapshot() { return deepFreeze({ frozen: this.frozen, resources: this.list(), latest: Object.fromEntries(this.latest), stats: { ...this.stats } }); }
}

async function invoke(explicit, resource, methods, signal) {
  if (typeof explicit === 'function') return await explicit({ signal, resource });
  return await Promise.resolve(callFirst(resource, methods, { signal }));
}
async function callResource(resource, methods) { return await Promise.resolve(callFirst(resource, methods)); }
function hasMethod(value, methods) { return methods.some((name) => typeof value?.[name] === 'function'); }
function publicRecord(record) { return Object.freeze({ name: record.name, critical: record.critical, required: record.required, timeoutMs: record.timeoutMs, tags: record.tags, sourceBinding: record.sourceBinding }); }

export function flattenResourceNumbers(value, options = {}, prefix = '', output = []) {
  const maxDepth = Number(options.maxDepth) || 6;
  const maxItems = Number(options.maxItems) || 1_000;
  function visit(current, path, depth) {
    if (output.length >= maxItems || depth > maxDepth || current == null) return;
    if (typeof current === 'number' && Number.isFinite(current)) { output.push({ path, value: current }); return; }
    if (typeof current !== 'object' || Array.isArray(current)) return;
    for (const [key, item] of Object.entries(current)) visit(item, path ? `${path}.${key}` : key, depth + 1);
  }
  visit(value, prefix, 0);
  return output;
}

export default ResourceRegistry;
