'use strict';

import { createHash } from 'node:crypto';
import { setTimeout as delay } from 'node:timers/promises';
import { BLOCKED_OBJECT_KEYS } from './constants.js';
import { MonitorTimeoutError } from './errors.js';

export const noop = () => {};
export const nowNs = () => process.hrtime.bigint();
export const elapsedMs = (started) => Number(process.hrtime.bigint() - started) / 1e6;
export const epochNanos = () => BigInt(Date.now()) * 1_000_000n;

// Trace and telemetry identifiers require global uniqueness, not secrecy. A
// process-seeded xoshiro generator avoids a synchronous operating-system entropy
// request on every request/span hot path. Authentication keys, signatures and
// operator tokens continue to use node:crypto in their dedicated components.
const RANDOM_SEED = `${Date.now()}:${process.hrtime.bigint()}:${process.pid}:${process.ppid}:${Math.random()}:${process.uptime()}`;
let randomState0 = seedWord(RANDOM_SEED, 0x243f6a88);
let randomState1 = seedWord(RANDOM_SEED, 0x85a308d3);
let randomState2 = seedWord(RANDOM_SEED, 0x13198a2e);
let randomState3 = seedWord(RANDOM_SEED, 0x03707344);
if ((randomState0 | randomState1 | randomState2 | randomState3) === 0) randomState3 = 1;
let identifierCounter = 0;
const PROCESS_IDENTIFIER = nextRandomHex(6);

export const randomId = (prefix = '') => {
  identifierCounter = identifierCounter >= Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER ? 1 : identifierCounter + 1;
  const head = prefix ? `${prefix}_` : '';
  return `${head}${Date.now().toString(36)}-${process.pid.toString(36)}-${identifierCounter.toString(36)}-${PROCESS_IDENTIFIER}`;
};
export const randomHex = (bytes) => nextRandomHex(bytes);
export const sha256 = (value) => createHash('sha256').update(typeof value === 'string' || Buffer.isBuffer(value) ? value : stableJson(value)).digest('hex');

function seedWord(seed, salt) {
  let hash = (0x811c9dc5 ^ salt) >>> 0;
  for (let index = 0; index < seed.length; index++) {
    hash ^= seed.charCodeAt(index);
    hash = Math.imul(hash, 0x01000193) >>> 0;
  }
  return hash || (salt >>> 0) || 1;
}
function rotateLeft(value, shift) { return ((value << shift) | (value >>> (32 - shift))) >>> 0; }
function nextRandomWord() {
  const result = Math.imul(rotateLeft(Math.imul(randomState1, 5) >>> 0, 7), 9) >>> 0;
  const temporary = (randomState1 << 9) >>> 0;
  randomState2 ^= randomState0;
  randomState3 ^= randomState1;
  randomState1 ^= randomState2;
  randomState0 ^= randomState3;
  randomState2 ^= temporary;
  randomState3 = rotateLeft(randomState3, 11);
  return result;
}
function nextRandomHex(bytes) {
  const length = Number(bytes);
  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(length) || length < 1 || length > 1_048_576) throw new TypeError('randomHex(bytes) requires a positive safe byte count no greater than 1 MiB.');
  let output = '';
  let remaining = length;
  while (remaining >= 4) {
    output += nextRandomWord().toString(16).padStart(8, '0');
    remaining -= 4;
  }
  if (remaining > 0) output += nextRandomWord().toString(16).padStart(8, '0').slice(0, remaining * 2);
  return output;
}

export function isPlainObject(value) {
  if (!value || typeof value !== 'object' || Array.isArray(value)) return false;
  const prototype = Object.getPrototypeOf(value);
  return prototype === Object.prototype || prototype === null;
}

export function deepFreeze(value, seen = new WeakSet()) {
  if (!value || typeof value !== 'object' || seen.has(value)) return value;
  seen.add(value);
  for (const item of Object.values(value)) deepFreeze(item, seen);
  return Object.freeze(value);
}

export function cloneValue(value) {
  if (typeof structuredClone === 'function') {
    try { return structuredClone(value); } catch {}
  }
  return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(value));
}

export function stableValue(value, seen = new WeakSet()) {
  if (value == null || typeof value !== 'object') return value;
  if (seen.has(value)) return '[Circular]';
  seen.add(value);
  if (Array.isArray(value)) return value.map((item) => stableValue(item, seen));
  const output = Object.create(null);
  for (const key of Object.keys(value).sort()) {
    if (BLOCKED_OBJECT_KEYS.has(key)) continue;
    output[key] = stableValue(value[key], seen);
  }
  return output;
}

export function stableJson(value) { return JSON.stringify(stableValue(value)); }

export function safeGet(value, path, fallback = undefined) {
  if (!path) return value === undefined ? fallback : value;
  let current = value;
  for (const part of Array.isArray(path) ? path : String(path).split('.')) {
    if (BLOCKED_OBJECT_KEYS.has(part) || current == null || typeof current !== 'object') return fallback;
    current = current[part];
  }
  return current === undefined ? fallback : current;
}

export function safeResolve(app, key, fallback = null) {
  try {
    if (typeof app?.tryResolve === 'function') return app.tryResolve(key, fallback);
    if (typeof app?.resolve === 'function') {
      const value = app.resolve(key);
      return value === undefined ? fallback : value;
    }
    if (typeof app?.container?.resolve === 'function') {
      const value = app.container.resolve(key);
      return value === undefined ? fallback : value;
    }
  } catch {}
  return fallback;
}

export function hasBinding(app, key) {
  try {
    return app?.bound?.(key) === true || app?.has?.(key) === true || app?.container?.bound?.(key) === true || app?.container?.has?.(key) === true;
  } catch { return false; }
}

export function bytesOf(value, max = Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER) {
  if (value == null) return 0;
  if (Buffer.isBuffer(value) || value instanceof Uint8Array) return Math.min(max, value.byteLength);
  if (value instanceof ArrayBuffer) return Math.min(max, value.byteLength);
  if (typeof value === 'string') return Math.min(max, Buffer.byteLength(value));
  if (typeof value === 'number' || typeof value === 'bigint' || typeof value === 'boolean') return 16;
  try { return Math.min(max, Buffer.byteLength(JSON.stringify(value))); }
  catch { return Math.min(max, 512); }
}

export function boundedInteger(value, fallback, minimum = 0, maximum = Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER) {
  const number = Number(value);
  return Number.isSafeInteger(number) && number >= minimum && number <= maximum ? number : fallback;
}

export function boundedNumber(value, fallback, minimum = -Infinity, maximum = Infinity) {
  const number = Number(value);
  return Number.isFinite(number) ? Math.max(minimum, Math.min(maximum, number)) : fallback;
}

export function bool(value, fallback = false) {
  if (value === true || value === false) return value;
  if (value == null || value === '') return fallback;
  const normalized = String(value).trim().toLowerCase();
  if (['1', 'true', 'yes', 'on', 'enabled'].includes(normalized)) return true;
  if (['0', 'false', 'no', 'off', 'disabled'].includes(normalized)) return false;
  return fallback;
}

export function list(value, fallback = []) {
  if (Array.isArray(value)) return Array.from(new Set(value.map(String).map((item) => item.trim()).filter(Boolean)));
  if (value == null || value === '') return [...fallback];
  return Array.from(new Set(String(value).split(',').map((item) => item.trim()).filter(Boolean)));
}

export function parseDuration(value, fallback, minimum = 0, maximum = Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER) {
  if (typeof value === 'number') return boundedInteger(Math.trunc(value), fallback, minimum, maximum);
  const match = /^(-?\d+(?:\.\d+)?)\s*(ms|s|m|h|d)?$/i.exec(String(value ?? '').trim());
  if (!match) return fallback;
  const scale = { ms: 1, s: 1_000, m: 60_000, h: 3_600_000, d: 86_400_000 }[(match[2] || 'ms').toLowerCase()];
  return boundedInteger(Math.trunc(Number(match[1]) * scale), fallback, minimum, maximum);
}

export function parseBytes(value, fallback, minimum = 0, maximum = Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER) {
  if (typeof value === 'number') return boundedInteger(Math.trunc(value), fallback, minimum, maximum);
  const match = /^(-?\d+(?:\.\d+)?)\s*(b|kb|kib|mb|mib|gb|gib)?$/i.exec(String(value ?? '').trim());
  if (!match) return fallback;
  const scale = { b: 1, kb: 1_000, kib: 1_024, mb: 1_000_000, mib: 1_048_576, gb: 1_000_000_000, gib: 1_073_741_824 }[(match[2] || 'b').toLowerCase()];
  return boundedInteger(Math.trunc(Number(match[1]) * scale), fallback, minimum, maximum);
}

export function sanitizeMetricName(value, fallback = 'rawnode_monitor_metric') {
  const name = String(value || '').trim().replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_:]/g, '_').replace(/^[^a-zA-Z_:]+/, '');
  return name || fallback;
}

export function sanitizeLabelName(value) {
  return String(value || '').trim().replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_]/g, '_').replace(/^[^a-zA-Z_]+/, '') || 'label';
}

export function normalizeLabels(labels = {}, options = {}) {
  const output = Object.create(null);
  const maxLabels = boundedInteger(options.maxLabels, 32, 0, 256);
  const maxValueBytes = boundedInteger(options.maxValueBytes, 256, 1, 16_384);
  let count = 0;
  for (const key of Object.keys(labels || {}).sort()) {
    if (count >= maxLabels || BLOCKED_OBJECT_KEYS.has(key)) break;
    const name = sanitizeLabelName(key);
    const value = String(labels[key] ?? '');
    if (Buffer.byteLength(value) > maxValueBytes) continue;
    output[name] = value;
    count++;
  }
  return Object.freeze(output);
}

export function labelsKey(labels = {}) {
  return Object.entries(labels).map(([key, value]) => `${key.length}:${key}=${String(value).length}:${value}`).join('|');
}

export function errorRecord(error, options = {}) {
  if (!error) return null;
  const record = {
    name: error.name || 'Error',
    code: error.code || null,
    message: error.message || String(error),
    retryable: error.retryable === true,
  };
  if (options.stack === true && error.stack) record.stack = String(error.stack).slice(0, options.maxStackBytes || 64 * 1024);
  if (error.details && typeof error.details === 'object') record.details = error.details;
  return record;
}

export async function withTimeout(task, timeoutMs, options = {}) {
  const ms = boundedInteger(timeoutMs, 5_000, 1, 24 * 60 * 60_000);
  const controller = new AbortController();
  const external = options.signal;
  const onAbort = () => controller.abort(external.reason);
  external?.addEventListener?.('abort', onAbort, { once: true });
  let timer;
  const timeout = new Promise((_, reject) => {
    timer = setTimeout(() => {
      const error = new MonitorTimeoutError(options.message || `Operation exceeded ${ms}ms.`, { details: { timeoutMs: ms } });
      controller.abort(error);
      reject(error);
    }, ms);
    if (options.ref === false) timer.unref?.();
  });
  try {
    const operation = typeof task === 'function' ? task(controller.signal) : task;
    return await Promise.race([Promise.resolve(operation), timeout]);
  } finally {
    clearTimeout(timer);
    external?.removeEventListener?.('abort', onAbort);
  }
}

export async function cooperativeYield(signal = null) {
  signal?.throwIfAborted?.();
  await new Promise((resolve) => setImmediate(resolve));
  signal?.throwIfAborted?.();
}

export async function retry(operation, options = {}) {
  const attempts = boundedInteger(options.attempts, 3, 1, 100);
  const baseMs = boundedInteger(options.baseMs, 50, 0, 60_000);
  const maxMs = boundedInteger(options.maxMs, 5_000, baseMs, 300_000);
  let last;
  for (let attempt = 1; attempt <= attempts; attempt++) {
    options.signal?.throwIfAborted?.();
    try { return await operation(attempt, options.signal); }
    catch (error) {
      last = error;
      if (attempt >= attempts || options.retryWhen?.(error, attempt) === false) break;
      const wait = Math.min(maxMs, baseMs * 2 ** (attempt - 1));
      await delay(wait + Math.floor(Math.random() * Math.max(1, wait / 4)), undefined, { signal: options.signal, ref: options.ref !== false });
    }
  }
  throw last;
}

export function dispatchEvent(events, name, payload) {
  if (!events) return false;
  try {
    const result = typeof events.dispatch === 'function' ? events.dispatch(name, payload) : events.emit?.(name, payload);
    result?.catch?.(noop);
    return true;
  } catch { return false; }
}

export function callFirst(value, methods, ...args) {
  for (const name of methods) {
    if (typeof value?.[name] === 'function') return value[name](...args);
  }
  return undefined;
}

'use strict';

import { AlertRuleError } from '../errors.js';
import { deepFreeze, randomId, safeGet } from '../support.js';

const COMPARATORS = Object.freeze({
  gt: (value, threshold) => value > threshold,
  gte: (value, threshold) => value >= threshold,
  lt: (value, threshold) => value < threshold,
  lte: (value, threshold) => value <= threshold,
  eq: (value, threshold) => value === threshold,
  neq: (value, threshold) => value !== threshold,
  between: (value, threshold) => value >= threshold[0] && value <= threshold[1],
  outside: (value, threshold) => value < threshold[0] || value > threshold[1],
});

export class AlertRule {
  constructor(options = {}) {
    if (!options.name && !options.id) throw new AlertRuleError('Alert rule requires a stable name or id.');
    this.id = String(options.id || slug(options.name) || randomId('rule'));
    this.name = String(options.name || this.id);
    this.description = options.description || '';
    this.severity = options.severity || 'warning';
    this.enabled = options.enabled !== false;
    this.source = options.source || inferSource(options);
    this.metric = options.metric || null;
    this.path = options.path || null;
    this.labels = Object.freeze({ ...(options.labels || {}) });
    this.aggregate = options.aggregate || 'sum';
    this.comparator = options.comparator || options.operator || 'gt';
    this.threshold = options.threshold ?? 0;
    this.recoveryThreshold = options.recoveryThreshold ?? null;
    this.pendingForMs = Math.max(0, Number(options.pendingForMs ?? options.forMs ?? 0) || 0);
    this.resolveAfterMs = Math.max(0, Number(options.resolveAfterMs ?? 0) || 0);
    this.cooldownMs = Math.max(0, Number(options.cooldownMs ?? 5 * 60_000) || 0);
    this.consecutive = Math.max(1, Number(options.consecutive) || 1);
    this.missing = options.missing || 'ignore';
    this.groupBy = Object.freeze([...(options.groupBy || [])]);
    this.annotations = deepFreeze({ ...(options.annotations || {}) });
    this.tags = Object.freeze(Array.from(new Set(options.tags || [])));
    this.runbook = options.runbook || options.runbookUrl || null;
    this.query = options.query || null;
    this.evaluate = options.evaluate || null;
    this.incident = options.incident;
    this.channels = Object.freeze([...(options.channels || [])]);
    this.validate();
    Object.freeze(this);
  }

  validate() {
    if (this.evaluate != null && typeof this.evaluate !== 'function') throw new AlertRuleError(`Alert rule '${this.id}' evaluate must be a function.`);
    if (!this.evaluate && !COMPARATORS[this.comparator]) throw new AlertRuleError(`Alert rule '${this.id}' uses unsupported comparator '${this.comparator}'.`);
    if (!this.evaluate && this.source === 'metric' && !this.metric) throw new AlertRuleError(`Metric alert rule '${this.id}' requires metric.`);
    if (['between', 'outside'].includes(this.comparator) && (!Array.isArray(this.threshold) || this.threshold.length !== 2)) throw new AlertRuleError(`Alert rule '${this.id}' comparator '${this.comparator}' requires [min,max] threshold.`);
  }

  async test(context = {}) {
    if (this.evaluate) return normalizeEvaluation(await this.evaluate(context, this));
    const value = resolveValue(this, context);
    if (value == null || Number.isNaN(Number(value))) {
      if (this.missing === 'fire') return { firing: true, value: null, reason: 'missing' };
      if (this.missing === 'error') throw new AlertRuleError(`Alert rule '${this.id}' source value is missing.`);
      return { firing: false, value: null, reason: 'missing', skipped: true };
    }
    const firing = COMPARATORS[this.comparator](value, this.threshold);
    return { firing, value, threshold: this.threshold, comparator: this.comparator, reason: firing ? 'threshold-breached' : 'threshold-ok' };
  }

  recovered(value) {
    if (this.recoveryThreshold == null) return !COMPARATORS[this.comparator](value, this.threshold);
    return !COMPARATORS[this.comparator](value, this.recoveryThreshold);
  }

  toJSON() {
    return {
      id: this.id, name: this.name, description: this.description, severity: this.severity, enabled: this.enabled,
      source: this.source, metric: this.metric, path: this.path, labels: this.labels, aggregate: this.aggregate,
      comparator: this.comparator, threshold: this.threshold, recoveryThreshold: this.recoveryThreshold,
      pendingForMs: this.pendingForMs, resolveAfterMs: this.resolveAfterMs, cooldownMs: this.cooldownMs,
      consecutive: this.consecutive, missing: this.missing, groupBy: this.groupBy, annotations: this.annotations,
      tags: this.tags, runbook: this.runbook, incident: this.incident, channels: this.channels,
    };
  }
}

function inferSource(options) { if (options.metric) return 'metric'; if (options.slo) return 'slo'; if (options.health) return 'health'; if (options.resource) return 'resource'; return 'custom'; }
function slug(value) { return String(value || '').trim().toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '-').replace(/^-|-$/g, ''); }
function normalizeEvaluation(value) { if (typeof value === 'boolean') return { firing: value, value: value ? 1 : 0 }; if (typeof value === 'number') return { firing: value !== 0, value }; if (!value || typeof value !== 'object') return { firing: false, value: null, skipped: true }; return { firing: value.firing === true || value.ok === false, ...value }; }

function resolveValue(rule, context) {
  if (rule.source === 'metric') return metricValue(context.metrics, rule);
  if (rule.source === 'health') return healthValue(context.health, rule);
  if (rule.source === 'resource') return safeGet(context.resources, rule.path || rule.query, null);
  if (rule.source === 'slo') return safeGet(context.slos, rule.path || `${rule.query || rule.metric}.burnRate`, null);
  return safeGet(context, rule.path || rule.query, null);
}

export function metricValue(snapshot, rule) {
  const metric = snapshot?.metrics?.[rule.metric] || snapshot?.metrics?.metrics?.[rule.metric] || snapshot?.[rule.metric];
  if (!metric) return null;
  const series = (metric.series || []).filter((entry) => Object.entries(rule.labels || {}).every(([key, value]) => String(entry.labels?.[key]) === String(value)));
  const values = series.map((entry) => entry.value ?? entry.mean ?? entry.sum ?? entry.count).map(Number).filter(Number.isFinite);
  if (!values.length) return null;
  switch (rule.aggregate) {
    case 'max': return Math.max(...values);
    case 'min': return Math.min(...values);
    case 'avg': case 'mean': return values.reduce((sum, value) => sum + value, 0) / values.length;
    case 'count': return values.length;
    case 'last': return values.at(-1);
    default: return values.reduce((sum, value) => sum + value, 0);
  }
}
function healthValue(report, rule) { const check = (report?.checks || []).find((entry) => entry.name === (rule.query || rule.path)); if (check) return check.ok === false ? 0 : 1; return report?.ok === false ? 0 : 1; }

export { COMPARATORS as ALERT_COMPARATORS };
export default AlertRule;

'use strict';

import { EventEmitter } from 'node:events';
import { AlertState, SignalKind } from '../constants.js';
import AlertRule from './AlertRule.js';
import SilenceManager from './SilenceManager.js';
import NotificationRouter from './NotificationRouter.js';
import { deepFreeze, errorRecord, randomId } from '../support.js';

export class AlertEngine extends EventEmitter {
  constructor(options = {}) {
    super();
    this.intervalMs = Math.max(250, Number(options.intervalMs) || 15_000);
    this.concurrency = Math.max(1, Number(options.concurrency) || 8);
    this.historyLimit = Math.max(1, Number(options.historyLimit) || 5_000);
    this.defaultPendingForMs = Math.max(0, Number(options.defaultPendingForMs) || 0);
    this.defaultResolveAfterMs = Math.max(0, Number(options.defaultResolveAfterMs) || 0);
    this.defaultCooldownMs = Math.max(0, Number(options.defaultCooldownMs) || 5 * 60_000);
    this.rules = new Map();
    this.states = new Map();
    this.history = [];
    this.silences = options.silences || new SilenceManager(options);
    this.notifications = options.notifications || new NotificationRouter(options);
    this.contextProvider = options.contextProvider || (() => ({}));
    this.signal = options.signal || null;
    this.incidents = options.incidents || null;
    this.autoIncidentSeverities = new Set(options.autoIncidentSeverities || ['critical']);
    this.leader = options.leader || null;
    this.timer = null;
    this.running = false;
    this.closed = false;
    this.evaluating = false;
    this.frozen = false;
    this.stats = { evaluations: 0, errors: 0, transitions: 0, notifications: 0, skippedNotLeader: 0 };
  }

  register(input, options = {}) {
    if (this.frozen) throw new Error('Alert engine is frozen.');
    const rule = input instanceof AlertRule ? input : new AlertRule({ pendingForMs: this.defaultPendingForMs, resolveAfterMs: this.defaultResolveAfterMs, cooldownMs: this.defaultCooldownMs, ...input });
    if (this.rules.has(rule.id) && options.replace !== true) throw new Error(`Alert rule '${rule.id}' is already registered.`);
    this.rules.set(rule.id, rule);
    if (!this.states.has(rule.id)) this.states.set(rule.id, initialState(rule));
    this.emit('registered', rule.toJSON());
    return rule;
  }

  unregister(id) { this.states.delete(String(id)); return this.rules.delete(String(id)); }
  freeze() { this.frozen = true; return this; }
  start() { if (this.running || this.closed) return this; this.running = true; this.timer = setInterval(() => void this.evaluate().catch((error) => this.emit('error', error)), this.intervalMs); this.timer.unref?.(); return this; }

  async evaluate(options = {}) {
    if (this.evaluating || this.closed) return this.snapshot();
    if (this.leader && await this.leader.isLeader?.() === false) { this.stats.skippedNotLeader++; return this.snapshot(); }
    this.evaluating = true;
    const context = await this.contextProvider(options);
    const rules = Array.from(this.rules.values()).filter((rule) => rule.enabled);
    let cursor = 0;
    try {
      await Promise.all(Array.from({ length: Math.min(this.concurrency, rules.length || 1) }, async () => {
        while (cursor < rules.length) await this._evaluateRule(rules[cursor++], context, options);
      }));
    } finally { this.evaluating = false; }
    return this.snapshot();
  }

  async _evaluateRule(rule, context, options) {
    const now = Number(options.now || Date.now());
    const state = this.states.get(rule.id) || initialState(rule);
    this.stats.evaluations++;
    try {
      const evaluation = await rule.test({ ...context, now, state: { ...state } });
      state.lastEvaluationAt = now;
      state.lastValue = evaluation.value ?? null;
      state.lastEvaluation = evaluation;
      state.error = null;
      if (evaluation.skipped) return;
      const silence = this.silences.match(rule, now);
      if (silence) {
        if (state.state !== AlertState.SILENCED) await this._transition(rule, state, AlertState.SILENCED, evaluation, { silence }, now);
        return;
      }
      if (state.state === AlertState.SILENCED) await this._transition(rule, state, AlertState.INACTIVE, evaluation, null, now);

      if (evaluation.firing) {
        state.consecutiveFiring++;
        state.consecutiveHealthy = 0;
        state.pendingSince ||= now;
        if (state.state === AlertState.INACTIVE || state.state === AlertState.RESOLVED) await this._transition(rule, state, AlertState.PENDING, evaluation, null, now);
        const pendingMs = now - state.pendingSince;
        if (state.consecutiveFiring >= rule.consecutive && pendingMs >= rule.pendingForMs && state.state !== AlertState.FIRING && state.state !== AlertState.ACKNOWLEDGED) {
          await this._transition(rule, state, AlertState.FIRING, evaluation, null, now);
        }
      } else {
        state.consecutiveHealthy++;
        state.consecutiveFiring = 0;
        state.healthySince ||= now;
        if (state.state === AlertState.PENDING) await this._transition(rule, state, AlertState.INACTIVE, evaluation, null, now);
        else if ([AlertState.FIRING, AlertState.ACKNOWLEDGED].includes(state.state)) {
          const recovered = rule.evaluate
            ? evaluation.firing === false && evaluation.recovered !== false
            : rule.recovered(evaluation.value);
          if (recovered && now - state.healthySince >= rule.resolveAfterMs) {
            await this._transition(rule, state, AlertState.RESOLVED, evaluation, null, now);
          }
        }
      }
      this.states.set(rule.id, state);
    } catch (error) {
      this.stats.errors++;
      state.error = errorRecord(error, { stack: options.includeStack === true });
      await this._transition(rule, state, AlertState.ERROR, { firing: false, value: null, error: state.error }, null, now);
    }
  }

  async _transition(rule, state, next, evaluation, meta, now) {
    if (state.state === next) return;
    const previous = state.state;
    state.state = next;
    state.updatedAt = now;
    state.transitions++;
    if (next === AlertState.FIRING) { state.firedAt ||= now; state.resolvedAt = null; state.healthySince = null; }
    if (next === AlertState.RESOLVED || next === AlertState.INACTIVE) { state.resolvedAt = next === AlertState.RESOLVED ? now : state.resolvedAt; state.pendingSince = null; state.healthySince = null; if (next === AlertState.INACTIVE) state.firedAt = null; }
    const event = deepFreeze({
      id: randomId('alert'), rule: rule.toJSON(), ruleId: rule.id, type: next === AlertState.FIRING ? 'firing' : next === AlertState.RESOLVED ? 'resolved' : 'transition', previous, state: next,
      severity: rule.severity, timestamp: now, value: evaluation.value ?? null, evaluation, meta, annotations: rule.annotations, tags: rule.tags,
    });
    this.history.push(event); if (this.history.length > this.historyLimit) this.history.splice(0, this.history.length - this.historyLimit);
    this.stats.transitions++;
    this.emit('transition', event);
    this.signal?.(SignalKind.ALERT, event, { priority: next === AlertState.FIRING ? 20 : 10 });

    const notify = next === AlertState.FIRING || next === AlertState.RESOLVED;
    if (notify && (!state.lastNotifiedAt || now - state.lastNotifiedAt >= rule.cooldownMs || next === AlertState.RESOLVED)) {
      await this.notifications.notify({ ...event, title: `${rule.name} ${next}`, channels: rule.channels }, { channels: rule.channels });
      state.lastNotifiedAt = now; this.stats.notifications++;
    }
    if (next === AlertState.FIRING && this.incidents && (rule.incident === true || this.autoIncidentSeverities.has(rule.severity))) {
      state.incidentId = (await this.incidents.openFromAlert(event))?.id || state.incidentId;
    }
    if (next === AlertState.RESOLVED && state.incidentId) await this.incidents?.resolve?.(state.incidentId, { source: 'alert', alert: event }).catch(() => {});
  }

  acknowledge(ruleId, actor = null) {
    const state = this.states.get(String(ruleId));
    if (!state || state.state !== AlertState.FIRING) return false;
    state.state = AlertState.ACKNOWLEDGED; state.acknowledgedAt = Date.now(); state.acknowledgedBy = actor; state.updatedAt = Date.now(); return true;
  }

  async close() { clearInterval(this.timer); this.timer = null; this.running = false; this.closed = true; await this.notifications.close(); }
  current(options = {}) { let rows = Array.from(this.states.values(), (state) => ({ ...state, rule: this.rules.get(state.ruleId)?.toJSON() || null })); if (options.state) rows = rows.filter((row) => row.state === options.state); if (options.severity) rows = rows.filter((row) => row.rule?.severity === options.severity); return deepFreeze(rows); }
  historySnapshot(options = {}) { return deepFreeze(this.history.slice(-(Math.max(1, Number(options.limit) || 100)))); }
  snapshot() { return deepFreeze({ running: this.running, closed: this.closed, frozen: this.frozen, rules: Array.from(this.rules.values(), (rule) => rule.toJSON()), states: this.current(), silences: this.silences.snapshot(), notifications: this.notifications.snapshot(), history: this.history.length, stats: { ...this.stats } }); }
}

function initialState(rule) { return { ruleId: rule.id, state: AlertState.INACTIVE, createdAt: Date.now(), updatedAt: Date.now(), pendingSince: null, firedAt: null, healthySince: null, resolvedAt: null, acknowledgedAt: null, acknowledgedBy: null, lastEvaluationAt: null, lastNotifiedAt: null, lastValue: null, lastEvaluation: null, consecutiveFiring: 0, consecutiveHealthy: 0, transitions: 0, incidentId: null, error: null }; }
export default AlertEngine;

'use strict';

import { EventEmitter } from 'node:events';

export class NotificationRouter extends EventEmitter {
  constructor(options = {}) { super(); this.channels = new Map(); this.routes = []; this.errorPolicy = options.errorPolicy || 'isolate'; this.stats = { sent: 0, errors: 0, skipped: 0 }; }
  register(name, channel, options = {}) { if (!name || !channel) throw new TypeError('Notification channel name and implementation are required.'); this.channels.set(String(name), { name: String(name), channel, required: options.required === true }); return channel; }
  route(match = {}, channels = []) { this.routes.push(Object.freeze({ match: { ...match }, channels: Object.freeze([...channels]) })); return this; }
  async notify(notification, options = {}) {
    const selected = options.channels?.length ? options.channels : this._channels(notification);
    const results = [];
    for (const name of selected) {
      const entry = this.channels.get(String(name));
      if (!entry) { this.stats.skipped++; results.push({ channel: name, skipped: true, reason: 'missing' }); continue; }
      try {
        const channel = entry.channel;
        const value = typeof channel.send === 'function'
          ? await channel.send(notification, options)
          : typeof channel.notify === 'function'
            ? await channel.notify(notification, options)
            : typeof channel === 'function'
              ? await channel(notification, options)
              : false;
        this.stats.sent++; results.push({ channel: name, ok: value !== false, result: value });
      } catch (error) {
        this.stats.errors++; results.push({ channel: name, ok: false, error: error?.message || String(error) }); this.emit('error', error, name, notification);
        if (entry.required || this.errorPolicy === 'propagate') throw error;
      }
    }
    return Object.freeze(results);
  }
  _channels(notification) {
    const output = new Set();
    for (const route of this.routes) if (matches(route.match, notification)) for (const name of route.channels) output.add(name);
    if (!output.size) for (const name of this.channels.keys()) output.add(name);
    return [...output];
  }
  async close() { await Promise.allSettled(Array.from(this.channels.values()).map((entry) => entry.channel.close?.())); }
  snapshot() { return Object.freeze({ channels: Object.freeze(Array.from(this.channels.keys())), routes: Object.freeze(this.routes.slice()), stats: Object.freeze({ ...this.stats }) }); }
}
function matches(match, value) { return Object.entries(match || {}).every(([key, expected]) => Array.isArray(expected) ? expected.includes(value?.[key]) : value?.[key] === expected); }

export class LoggerNotificationChannel {
  constructor(logger) { this.logger = logger; }
  async send(notification) { const method = notification.severity === 'critical' || notification.severity === 'error' ? 'error' : notification.severity === 'warning' ? 'warn' : 'info'; this.logger?.[method]?.(`[Monitor] ${notification.title || notification.rule?.name || notification.type}`, notification); return true; }
}
export class EventNotificationChannel {
  constructor(events, event = 'monitor:notification') { this.events = events; this.event = event; }
  async send(notification) { const value = this.events?.dispatch?.(this.event, notification) ?? this.events?.emit?.(this.event, notification); await value; return true; }
}
export class CallbackNotificationChannel { constructor(callback) { this.callback = callback; } async send(notification, options) { return await this.callback(notification, options); } }
export default NotificationRouter;

'use strict';

import { randomId } from '../support.js';

export class SilenceManager {
  constructor(options = {}) { this.maxSilences = Math.max(1, Number(options.maxSilences) || 10_000); this.silences = new Map(); this.stats = { created: 0, expired: 0, matched: 0 }; }
  create(options = {}) {
    if (this.silences.size >= this.maxSilences) throw new Error('Monitor silence limit exceeded.');
    const now = Date.now();
    const record = Object.freeze({ id: options.id || randomId('silence'), ruleIds: Object.freeze([...(options.ruleIds || [])]), severities: Object.freeze([...(options.severities || [])]), tags: Object.freeze({ ...(options.tags || {}) }), startsAt: Number(options.startsAt || now), endsAt: Number(options.endsAt || now + Number(options.durationMs || 60 * 60_000)), reason: options.reason || '', createdBy: options.createdBy || null, createdAt: now });
    this.silences.set(record.id, record); this.stats.created++; return record;
  }
  remove(id) { return this.silences.delete(String(id)); }
  match(rule, now = Date.now()) {
    this.prune(now);
    for (const silence of this.silences.values()) {
      if (now < silence.startsAt || now >= silence.endsAt) continue;
      if (silence.ruleIds.length && !silence.ruleIds.includes(rule.id)) continue;
      if (silence.severities.length && !silence.severities.includes(rule.severity)) continue;
      if (Object.keys(silence.tags).length && !Object.entries(silence.tags).every(([key, value]) => rule.annotations?.[key] === value || rule.tags?.includes(`${key}:${value}`))) continue;
      this.stats.matched++; return silence;
    }
    return null;
  }
  prune(now = Date.now()) { let count = 0; for (const [id, record] of this.silences) if (record.endsAt <= now) { this.silences.delete(id); count++; } this.stats.expired += count; return count; }
  snapshot() { return Object.freeze({ silences: Object.freeze([...this.silences.values()]), stats: Object.freeze({ ...this.stats }) }); }
}
export default SilenceManager;

'use strict';

import { SpanKind, SpanStatus } from '../constants.js';

/**
 * Explicit opt-in proxy for async clients (RawMongo, Redis, mail, payment, etc.).
 * The original object is never mutated. Methods not selected are passed through.
 */
export class OperationInstrumentor {
  constructor(monitor, options = {}) {
    this.monitor = monitor;
    this.name = options.name || 'operation';
    this.kind = options.kind || SpanKind.CLIENT;
    this.methods = options.methods ? new Set(options.methods) : null;
    this.exclude = new Set(options.exclude || []);
    this.attributeFactory = options.attributes || null;
    this.resultAttributes = options.resultAttributes || null;
    this.captureArguments = options.captureArguments === true;
    this.captureResults = options.captureResults === true;
    this.cache = new WeakMap();
    this.calls = monitor.metrics.counter(`${this.name}_operations_total`);
    this.duration = monitor.metrics.histogram(`${this.name}_operation_duration_seconds`, { unit: 'seconds' });
  }

  wrap(target) {
    if (!target || (typeof target !== 'object' && typeof target !== 'function')) return target;
    const existing = this.cache.get(target); if (existing) return existing;
    const instrumentor = this;
    const proxy = new Proxy(target, {
      get(object, property, receiver) {
        const value = Reflect.get(object, property, receiver);
        if (typeof value !== 'function' || typeof property !== 'string' || !instrumentor._selected(property)) return value;
        return function monitoredOperation(...args) { return instrumentor.invoke(object, property, value, args, this); };
      },
    });
    this.cache.set(target, proxy); return proxy;
  }

  _selected(method) { return !this.exclude.has(method) && (!this.methods || this.methods.has(method)); }

  invoke(target, method, operation, args, receiver = target) {
    const labels = { operation: method };
    return this.monitor.tracer.trace(`${this.name}.${method}`, {
      kind: this.kind,
      attributes: {
        'rawnode.component': this.name,
        'rawnode.operation': method,
        ...(typeof this.attributeFactory === 'function' ? this.attributeFactory({ target, method, args }) : this.attributeFactory || {}),
        ...(this.captureArguments ? { 'rawnode.arguments': this.monitor.serializer.sanitize(args) } : {}),
      },
    }, async (span) => {
      const started = process.hrtime.bigint();
      try {
        const result = await Reflect.apply(operation, receiver, args);
        this.calls.inc({ ...labels, outcome: 'success' });
        if (this.captureResults) span.setAttribute('rawnode.result', this.monitor.serializer.sanitize(result));
        if (typeof this.resultAttributes === 'function') span.setAttributes(this.resultAttributes(result) || {});
        span.setStatus(SpanStatus.OK);
        return result;
      } catch (error) {
        this.calls.inc({ ...labels, outcome: 'error' });
        span.recordException(error).setStatus(SpanStatus.ERROR, error?.message);
        throw error;
      } finally { this.duration.record(Number(process.hrtime.bigint() - started) / 1e9, labels); }
    });
  }
}

export function instrumentOperations(target, monitor, options = {}) { return new OperationInstrumentor(monitor, options).wrap(target); }
export default OperationInstrumentor;

FILE PATH: ./integrations/EventMonitorBridge.js

'use strict';

import { SignalKind } from '../constants.js';

/** Passive event observer; it does not replace or wrap dispatcher methods. */
export class EventMonitorBridge {
  constructor(events, monitor, options = {}) {
    this.events = events; this.monitor = monitor; this.options = options;
    this.include = options.include || ['**']; this.exclude = options.exclude || ['monitor:**', 'logger:**'];
    this.maxPayloadBytes = Math.max(1_024, Number(options.maxPayloadBytes) || 128 * 1024);
    this.unsubscribe = null; this.started = false; this.stats = { observed: 0, excluded: 0, rejected: 0 };
    this.metric = monitor.metrics.counter('events_observed_total');
  }
  start() {
    if (this.started || !this.events) return this;
    const handler = (...args) => this._observe(...args);
    if (typeof this.events.onAny === 'function') this.unsubscribe = normalizeUnsubscribe(this.events.onAny(handler), () => this.events.offAny?.(handler));
    else if (typeof this.events.onPattern === 'function') this.unsubscribe = normalizeUnsubscribe(this.events.onPattern('**', handler), () => this.events.offPattern?.('**', handler));
    else if (typeof this.events.on === 'function' && Array.isArray(this.options.names)) {
      for (const name of this.options.names) this.events.on(name, (...args) => this._observe(name, ...args));
      this.unsubscribe = () => {};
    }
    this.started = true; return this;
  }
  _observe(name, ...args) {
    const event = typeof name === 'string' ? name : name?.name || name?.event || 'event';
    if (!allowed(event, this.include, this.exclude)) { this.stats.excluded++; return; }
    const context = this.monitor.context.active();
    const payload = this.monitor.serializer.sanitize(args.length <= 1 ? args[0] : args, { maxPayloadBytes: this.maxPayloadBytes });
    const result = this.monitor.emitSignal(SignalKind.EVENT, { name: event, payload }, { context, attributes: { source: 'events' } });
    this.metric.inc({ event: normalizeEventLabel(event) }, { context });
    this.stats.observed++; if (result?.accepted === false) this.stats.rejected++;
  }
  async close() { try { await this.unsubscribe?.(); } catch {} this.unsubscribe = null; this.started = false; }
  snapshot() { return Object.freeze({ started: this.started, stats: Object.freeze({ ...this.stats }) }); }
}
function normalizeUnsubscribe(result, fallback) { return typeof result === 'function' ? result : result?.unsubscribe ? () => result.unsubscribe() : fallback; }
function allowed(name, include, exclude) { return include.some((pattern) => match(pattern, name)) && !exclude.some((pattern) => match(pattern, name)); }
function match(pattern, value) { if (pattern instanceof RegExp) return pattern.test(value); const text = String(pattern); if (text === '**' || text === '*') return true; if (text.endsWith('**')) return value.startsWith(text.slice(0, -2)); if (text.endsWith('*')) return value.startsWith(text.slice(0, -1)); return value === text; }
function normalizeEventLabel(value) { return String(value).replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_.:-]/g, '_').slice(0, 128); }
export default EventMonitorBridge;

FILE PATH: ./integrations/HttpMonitorMiddleware.js
================================================

'use strict';

import { SpanKind, SpanStatus } from '../constants.js';
import { extractTraceContext, injectTraceContext } from '../tracing/TraceContext.js';
import { errorRecord } from '../support.js';

/** RawNode/Express-compatible HTTP server middleware with no request-body capture. */
export function createMonitorHttpMiddleware(monitor, options = {}) {
  if (!monitor) throw new TypeError('createMonitorHttpMiddleware() requires a MonitorRuntime.');
  const requests = monitor.metrics.counter('http_server_requests_total', { description: 'HTTP requests completed by the application.' });
  const duration = monitor.metrics.histogram('http_server_request_duration_seconds', { unit: 'seconds', description: 'HTTP server request latency.' });
  const active = monitor.metrics.upDownCounter('http_server_active_requests', { description: 'HTTP requests currently executing.' });
  const responseBytes = monitor.metrics.histogram('http_server_response_bytes', { unit: 'bytes' });

  return function rawNodeMonitorHttp(req, res, next) {
    if (monitor.configuration.get('enabled') === false || ignored(req, options)) return next();
    const started = process.hrtime.bigint();
    const method = String(req.method || 'GET').toUpperCase();
    const route = routeName(req, options);
    const path = safePath(req, options);
    const parent = extractTraceContext(req.headers || {}, { strict: options.strictTraceContext === true });
    const span = monitor.tracer.startSpan(`${method} ${route || path || '/'}`, {
      kind: SpanKind.SERVER,
      parent,
      attributes: {
        'http.request.method': method,
        'url.path': path,
        'http.route': route,
        'network.protocol.version': req.httpVersion || null,
        'server.address': req.headers?.host || null,
        'client.address': clientAddress(req, options),
        'user_agent.original': options.captureUserAgent === true ? req.headers?.['user-agent'] : undefined,
      },
    });
    const context = monitor.context.createRequestContext(req, {
      traceId: span.traceId,
      spanId: span.spanId,
      traceContext: span.spanContext(),
      span,
    });
    req.monitor = monitor;
    req.monitorContext = context;
    req.traceId = req.traceId || span.traceId;
    req.spanId = req.spanId || span.spanId;
    setResponseHeader(res, 'x-request-id', context.requestId);
    if (options.exposeTraceId === true) setResponseHeader(res, 'x-trace-id', span.traceId);
    if (options.propagateResponseTrace === true) injectTraceContext(span.spanContext(), res, { set: (target, key, value) => setResponseHeader(target, key, value) });

    const labels = { method, route: route || path || 'unmatched' };
    active.add(1, labels);
    let settled = false;
    const finish = (error = null, event = 'finish') => {
      if (settled) return;
      settled = true;
      const status = Number(res.statusCode || res.raw?.statusCode || (error ? 500 : 200));
      const elapsed = Number(process.hrtime.bigint() - started) / 1e9;
      const finalLabels = { ...labels, status: String(status), status_class: `${Math.floor(status / 100)}xx` };
      active.add(-1, labels);
      requests.inc(finalLabels, { context });
      duration.record(elapsed, finalLabels, { context });
      const bytes = responseSize(res);
      if (bytes != null) responseBytes.record(bytes, finalLabels, { context });
      span.setAttribute('http.response.status_code', status)
        .setAttribute('rawnode.response.event', event)
        .setAttribute('http.response.body.size', bytes);
      if (error) span.recordException(error);
      span.setStatus(error || status >= 500 ? SpanStatus.ERROR : SpanStatus.OK, error?.message || null).end();
      monitor.emitEvent('http.request.completed', {
        requestId: context.requestId,
        method,
        route: route || null,
        path,
        status,
        durationMs: elapsed * 1_000,
        error: error ? errorRecord(error) : null,
      }, { traceId: span.traceId, spanId: span.spanId, priority: status >= 500 ? 10 : 0 });
    };
    const raw = res.raw || res;
    raw.once?.('finish', () => finish(null, 'finish'));
    raw.once?.('close', () => finish(null, 'close'));
    raw.once?.('error', (error) => finish(error, 'error'));

    return monitor.context.run(context, async () => {
      try {
        const result = next();
        if (result && typeof result.then === 'function') await result;
        if (!raw.once && (res.finished || res.headersSent)) finish();
        return result;
      } catch (error) {
        finish(error, 'middleware-error');
        throw error;
      }
    });
  };
}

function ignored(req, options) { const value = safePath(req, options); return (options.exclude || []).some((entry) => typeof entry === 'function' ? entry(req) : entry instanceof RegExp ? entry.test(value) : value.startsWith(String(entry))); }
function routeName(req, options) { return options.routeResolver?.(req) || req.route?.name || req.routeName || req.matchedRoute?.name || req.route?.path || req.matchedRoute?.path || null; }
function safePath(req, options) { const source = options.pathResolver?.(req) || (typeof req.path === 'function' ? req.path() : req.path) || req.url || '/'; try { return new URL(source, 'http://rawnode.local').pathname; } catch { return String(source).split('?')[0]; } }
function clientAddress(req, options) { if (options.trustProxy === true) return String(req.headers?.['x-forwarded-for'] || '').split(',')[0].trim() || req.ip || req.socket?.remoteAddress || null; return req.ip || req.socket?.remoteAddress || null; }
function setResponseHeader(res, name, value) { try { if (value != null) (res.setHeader || res.raw?.setHeader)?.call(res.setHeader ? res : res.raw, name, String(value)); } catch {} }
function responseSize(res) { const value = res.getHeader?.('content-length') ?? res.raw?.getHeader?.('content-length'); const number = Number(value); return Number.isFinite(number) ? number : null; }
export default createMonitorHttpMiddleware;

'use strict';

import { SignalKind } from '../constants.js';
import { errorRecord } from '../support.js';

/** Passive EventEmitter bridge for WS/TCP/UDP/Unified lifecycle telemetry. */
export class ProtocolMonitorBridge {
  constructor(protocol, monitor, options = {}) {
    this.protocol = protocol; this.monitor = monitor; this.name = options.name || 'protocol'; this.options = options;
    this.events = options.events || ['connection', 'disconnect', 'close', 'error', 'message', 'packet', 'sendError', 'rejected', 'upgrade'];
    this.listeners = []; this.started = false;
    this.counter = monitor.metrics.counter(`${this.name}_events_total`);
  }
  start() {
    if (this.started || typeof this.protocol?.on !== 'function') return this;
    for (const event of this.events) {
      const listener = (...args) => this.observe(event, args);
      this.protocol.on(event, listener); this.listeners.push([event, listener]);
    }
    this.started = true; return this;
  }
  observe(event, args) {
    const isError = event === 'error' || event.endsWith('Error');
    this.counter.inc({ event, outcome: isError ? 'error' : 'ok' });
    const payload = isError ? { error: errorRecord(args[0]) } : this.monitor.serializer.sanitize(summarize(args), { maxPayloadBytes: this.options.maxPayloadBytes || 64 * 1024 });
    this.monitor.emitSignal(SignalKind.EVENT, { name: `${this.name}.${event}`, payload }, { attributes: { protocol: this.name }, priority: isError ? 10 : 0 });
  }
  async close() { for (const [event, listener] of this.listeners) this.protocol?.off?.(event, listener); this.listeners = []; this.started = false; }
  snapshot() { return Object.freeze({ name: this.name, started: this.started, events: Object.freeze([...this.events]) }); }
}
function summarize(args) { return args.slice(0, 4).map((value) => { if (value == null || typeof value !== 'object') return value; return { id: value.id || value.connectionId || value.socketId || null, type: value.type || value.event || value.constructor?.name || 'object', bytes: value.byteLength || value.length || value.bytes || null, address: value.address || value.remoteAddress || value.socket?.remoteAddress || null }; }); }
export default ProtocolMonitorBridge;

FILE PATH: ./integrations/MonitorFetch.js

'use strict';

import { SpanKind, SpanStatus } from '../constants.js';
import { injectTraceContext } from '../tracing/TraceContext.js';

/** Return an instrumented fetch function without replacing globalThis.fetch. */
export function createMonitorFetch(monitor, fetchImpl = globalThis.fetch, options = {}) {
  if (typeof fetchImpl !== 'function') throw new TypeError('createMonitorFetch requires a fetch implementation.');
  const requests = monitor.metrics.counter('http_client_requests_total');
  const duration = monitor.metrics.histogram('http_client_request_duration_seconds', { unit: 'seconds' });
  return async function monitorFetch(input, init = {}) {
    const url = input instanceof URL ? input : new URL(typeof input === 'string' ? input : input.url);
    const method = String(init.method || (typeof input === 'object' ? input.method : '') || 'GET').toUpperCase();
    return await monitor.tracer.trace(`${method} ${url.hostname}`, { kind: SpanKind.CLIENT, attributes: { 'http.request.method': method, 'url.full': options.captureQuery === true ? url.toString() : `${url.origin}${url.pathname}`, 'server.address': url.hostname, 'server.port': url.port || null } }, async (span) => {
      const headers = new Headers(init.headers || (typeof input === 'object' ? input.headers : undefined) || {});
      injectTraceContext(span.spanContext(), headers, { set: (target, key, value) => target.set(key, value) });
      const started = process.hrtime.bigint();
      try {
        const response = await fetchImpl(input, { ...init, headers });
        const labels = { method, host: url.hostname, status: String(response.status), status_class: `${Math.floor(response.status / 100)}xx` };
        requests.inc(labels); duration.record(Number(process.hrtime.bigint() - started) / 1e9, labels);
        span.setAttribute('http.response.status_code', response.status).setStatus(response.status >= 500 ? SpanStatus.ERROR : SpanStatus.OK);
        return response;
      } catch (error) {
        const labels = { method, host: url.hostname, status: '0', status_class: 'network_error' };
        requests.inc(labels); duration.record(Number(process.hrtime.bigint() - started) / 1e9, labels);
        span.recordException(error).setStatus(SpanStatus.ERROR, error?.message); throw error;
      }
    });
  };
}
export default createMonitorFetch;

'use strict';

import MonitorLogTransport from './MonitorLogTransport.js';

export class LoggerMonitorBridge {
  constructor(logger, monitor, options = {}) { this.logger = logger; this.monitor = monitor; this.options = options; this.transport = options.transport || new MonitorLogTransport(monitor, options); this.detach = null; this.started = false; }
  start() {
    if (this.started || !this.logger) return this;
    if (typeof this.logger.addTransport === 'function') { this.logger.addTransport(this.transport); this.detach = () => this.logger.removeTransport?.(this.transport); }
    else if (typeof this.logger.use === 'function') { const result = this.logger.use(this.transport); this.detach = typeof result === 'function' ? result : null; }
    else if (typeof this.logger.on === 'function') { const handler = (record) => this.transport.write(record); this.logger.on('record', handler); this.detach = () => this.logger.off?.('record', handler); }
    this.started = true; return this;
  }
  async close() { try { await this.detach?.(); } catch {} await this.transport.close(); this.started = false; }
  snapshot() { return Object.freeze({ started: this.started, attached: !!this.detach, transport: this.transport.snapshot() }); }
}
export default LoggerMonitorBridge;

'use strict';

/** Registers Monitor as a PM-observable resource without making either module depend on the other. */
export class PMMonitorBridge {
  constructor(pm, monitor, options = {}) { this.pm = pm; this.monitor = monitor; this.name = options.name || 'monitor'; this.started = false; this.unregister = null; }
  start() {
    if (this.started || !this.pm) return this;
    const resource = { health: () => this.monitor.health(), metrics: () => this.monitor.metricsSnapshot(), diagnostics: () => this.monitor.diagnostics() };
    if (typeof this.pm.registerResource === 'function') {
      const result = this.pm.registerResource(this.name, resource);
      this.unregister = typeof result === 'function' ? result : () => this.pm.unregisterResource?.(this.name);
      this.started = true;
    } else if (typeof this.pm.runtime?.registerResource === 'function') {
      this.pm.runtime.registerResource(this.name, resource); this.unregister = () => this.pm.runtime.unregisterResource?.(this.name); this.started = true;
    } else if (typeof this.pm.adapter?.registerResource === 'function') {
      this.pm.adapter.registerResource(this.name, resource); this.unregister = () => this.pm.adapter.unregisterResource?.(this.name); this.started = true;
    }
    return this;
  }
  async close() { try { await this.unregister?.(); } catch {} this.unregister = null; this.started = false; }
  snapshot() { return Object.freeze({ name: this.name, started: this.started }); }
}
export default PMMonitorBridge;

FILE PATH: ./integrations/RawNodeIntegrationManager.js
================================================

'use strict';

import { safeResolve } from '../support.js';
import EventMonitorBridge from './EventMonitorBridge.js';
import LoggerMonitorBridge from './LoggerMonitorBridge.js';
import PMMonitorBridge from './PMMonitorBridge.js';
import ProtocolMonitorBridge from './ProtocolMonitorBridge.js';

export class RawNodeIntegrationManager {
  constructor(app, monitor, options = {}) { this.app = app; this.monitor = monitor; this.options = options; this.bridges = new Map(); this.started = false; this.stats = { attached: 0, skipped: 0, errors: 0 }; }

  attach(name, bridge) { if (!bridge) return null; try { bridge.start?.(); this.bridges.set(name, bridge); this.stats.attached++; return bridge; } catch (error) { this.stats.errors++; if (this.options.failFast) throw error; return null; } }

  start() {
    if (this.started) return this;
    const settings = this.monitor.configuration.get('integrations', {});
    if (settings.events !== false) {
      const events = safeResolve(this.app, 'events');
      events ? this.attach('events', new EventMonitorBridge(events, this.monitor, typeof settings.events === 'object' ? settings.events : {})) : this.stats.skipped++;
    }
    if (settings.logger !== false) {
      const logger = safeResolve(this.app, 'logger') || this.app?.logger;
      logger ? this.attach('logger', new LoggerMonitorBridge(logger, this.monitor, typeof settings.logger === 'object' ? settings.logger : {})) : this.stats.skipped++;
    }
    if (settings.pm !== false) {
      const pm = safeResolve(this.app, 'pm.manager') || safeResolve(this.app, 'pm.runtime');
      pm ? this.attach('pm', new PMMonitorBridge(pm, this.monitor, typeof settings.pm === 'object' ? settings.pm : {})) : this.stats.skipped++;
    }
    for (const [name, keys] of Object.entries({ websocket: ['ws', 'websocket', 'ws.server'], tcp: ['tcp', 'tcp.server'], udp: ['udp', 'udp.server'], unified: ['unified', 'unified.server'] })) {
      if (settings[name] === false) continue;
      const resource = keys.map((key) => safeResolve(this.app, key)).find(Boolean);
      if (resource) this.attach(name, new ProtocolMonitorBridge(resource, this.monitor, { name, ...(typeof settings[name] === 'object' ? settings[name] : {}) }));
      else this.stats.skipped++;
    }
    this.started = true; return this;
  }

  get(name) { return this.bridges.get(String(name)) || null; }
  async close() { for (const bridge of Array.from(this.bridges.values()).reverse()) { try { await bridge.close?.(); } catch { this.stats.errors++; } } this.bridges.clear(); this.started = false; }
  snapshot() { return Object.freeze({ started: this.started, bridges: Object.freeze(Array.from(this.bridges.entries(), ([name, bridge]) => ({ name, snapshot: bridge.snapshot?.() || null }))), stats: Object.freeze({ ...this.stats }) }); }
}

export function registerRawNodeIntegrations(app, monitor, options = {}) { return new RawNodeIntegrationManager(app, monitor, options).start(); }
export default RawNodeIntegrationManager;

'use strict';

import { SignalKind } from '../constants.js';

/** Logger transport adapter. It accepts common write/log/handle contracts. */
export class MonitorLogTransport {
  constructor(monitor, options = {}) { this.monitor = monitor; this.name = options.name || 'monitor'; this.level = options.level || 'trace'; this.closed = false; this.stats = { written: 0, rejected: 0 }; }
  write(record) { return this._record(record); }
  log(record, callback) { try { this._record(record); callback?.(); } catch (error) { callback?.(error); } }
  handle(record) { return this._record(record); }
  _record(record = {}) {
    if (this.closed) return false;
    const context = this.monitor.context.active();
    const result = this.monitor.emitSignal(SignalKind.LOG, {
      timestamp: record.timestamp || record.time || Date.now(),
      level: record.level || record.severity || 'info',
      message: record.message || record.msg || '',
      channel: record.channel || record.logger || null,
      context: record.context || record.meta || null,
      error: record.error || record.exception || null,
    }, { context, traceId: record.traceId || context.traceId, spanId: record.spanId || context.spanId, priority: levelPriority(record.level) });
    this.stats.written++; if (result?.accepted === false) this.stats.rejected++; return result;
  }
  async flush() {}
  async close() { this.closed = true; }
  snapshot() { return Object.freeze({ name: this.name, closed: this.closed, stats: Object.freeze({ ...this.stats }) }); }
}
function levelPriority(level) { return ['fatal', 'emergency', 'alert', 'critical'].includes(String(level).toLowerCase()) ? 20 : ['error'].includes(String(level).toLowerCase()) ? 10 : 0; }
export default MonitorLogTransport;

'use strict';

import { createMonitor } from '../MonitorRuntime.js';
import MemoryTelemetryStore from '../storage/MemoryTelemetryStore.js';

/** Deterministic test harness that keeps every signal in bounded memory. */
export class MonitorRecorder {
  constructor(options = {}) {
    this.store = options.store || new MemoryTelemetryStore({
      maxItems: options.maxItems || 10_000,
      maxBytes: options.maxBytes || 32 * 1024 * 1024,
    });
    this.monitor = options.monitor || createMonitor({
      ...options,
      config: {
        profile: 'test',
        environment: 'testing',
        collectionIntervalMs: 3_600_000,
        resourceIntervalMs: 3_600_000,
        tracing: { sampleRate: 1, ...(options.config?.tracing || {}) },
        storage: { enabled: true, driver: 'memory', ...(options.config?.storage || {}) },
        studio: { enabled: false, ...(options.config?.studio || {}) },
        cluster: { enabled: false, ...(options.config?.cluster || {}) },
        ...options.config,
      },
      store: this.store,
    });
  }
  async start() { await this.monitor.start(); return this; }
  async flush() { await this.monitor.flush(); return this.all(); }
  async all(filter = {}) { return await this.store.query({ limit: 10_000, ...filter }); }
  async kind(kind) { return await this.all({ kind }); }
  async traces() { return this.monitor.tracer.query({ limit: 10_000 }); }
  async close() { await this.monitor.close(); }
  async [Symbol.asyncDispose]() { await this.close(); }
}

export class FakeMonitorClock {
  constructor(now = 1_700_000_000_000) { this.value = Number(now); }
  now = () => this.value;
  advance(ms) { this.value += Number(ms) || 0; return this.value; }
  set(value) { this.value = Number(value); return this.value; }
}

export default MonitorRecorder;

FILE PATH: ./system/ProcessMetricsCollector.js

'use strict';

import os from 'node:os';
import { EventEmitter } from 'node:events';
import { PerformanceObserver, constants as perfConstants, monitorEventLoopDelay, performance } from 'node:perf_hooks';
import { SignalKind } from '../constants.js';
import { deepFreeze, errorRecord } from '../support.js';

const GC_KINDS = Object.freeze({
  [perfConstants.NODE_PERFORMANCE_GC_MAJOR]: 'major',
  [perfConstants.NODE_PERFORMANCE_GC_MINOR]: 'minor',
  [perfConstants.NODE_PERFORMANCE_GC_INCREMENTAL]: 'incremental',
  [perfConstants.NODE_PERFORMANCE_GC_WEAKCB]: 'weakcb',
});

/**
 * Collects process, host, event-loop, active-resource, and GC telemetry using
 * Node.js primitives only. Collection is delta-based and does no synchronous I/O.
 */
export class ProcessMetricsCollector extends EventEmitter {
  constructor(options = {}) {
    super();
    this.registry = options.registry || null;
    this.signal = options.signal || null;
    this.intervalMs = Math.max(250, Number(options.intervalMs) || 10_000);
    this.eventLoopResolutionMs = Math.max(10, Number(options.eventLoopResolutionMs) || 20);
    this.captureGc = options.gc !== false;
    this.captureActiveResources = options.activeResources !== false;
    this.captureResourceUsage = options.resourceUsage !== false;
    this.captureHost = options.host !== false;
    this.unref = options.unref !== false;
    this.running = false;
    this.closed = false;
    this.timer = null;
    this.loop = null;
    this.gcObserver = null;
    this.lastCpu = process.cpuUsage();
    this.lastCpuAt = process.hrtime.bigint();
    this.lastElu = performance.eventLoopUtilization();
    this.gc = new Map();
    this.last = null;
    this.stats = { collections: 0, errors: 0, gcEvents: 0, signalsRejected: 0 };
    this.instruments = this.registry ? registerInstruments(this.registry) : null;
  }

  start() {
    if (this.running || this.closed) return this;
    this.running = true;
    this.loop = monitorEventLoopDelay({ resolution: this.eventLoopResolutionMs });
    this.loop.enable();
    if (this.captureGc) this._startGcObserver();
    this.timer = setInterval(() => { void this.collect().catch((error) => this._error(error)); }, this.intervalMs);
    if (this.unref) this.timer.unref?.();
    return this;
  }

  _startGcObserver() {
    try {
      this.gcObserver = new PerformanceObserver((list) => {
        for (const entry of list.getEntries()) {
          const kind = GC_KINDS[entry.detail?.kind ?? entry.kind] || 'unknown';
          const current = this.gc.get(kind) || { count: 0, durationMs: 0, maxDurationMs: 0 };
          current.count++;
          current.durationMs += Number(entry.duration || 0);
          current.maxDurationMs = Math.max(current.maxDurationMs, Number(entry.duration || 0));
          this.gc.set(kind, current);
          this.stats.gcEvents++;
        }
      });
      this.gcObserver.observe({ entryTypes: ['gc'], buffered: false });
    } catch (error) { this._error(error); }
  }

  async collect(options = {}) {
    if (this.closed) return this.last;
    const timestamp = Date.now();
    const nowNs = process.hrtime.bigint();
    const elapsedMicros = Math.max(1, Number(nowNs - this.lastCpuAt) / 1_000);
    const cpuDelta = process.cpuUsage(this.lastCpu);
    this.lastCpu = process.cpuUsage();
    this.lastCpuAt = nowNs;
    const elu = performance.eventLoopUtilization(this.lastElu);
    this.lastElu = performance.eventLoopUtilization();
    const memory = process.memoryUsage();
    const resourceUsage = this.captureResourceUsage && typeof process.resourceUsage === 'function'
      ? process.resourceUsage()
      : null;
    const loop = this.loop ? {
      minMs: finite(this.loop.min / 1e6),
      meanMs: finite(this.loop.mean / 1e6),
      maxMs: finite(this.loop.max / 1e6),
      stddevMs: finite(this.loop.stddev / 1e6),
      p50Ms: finite(this.loop.percentile(50) / 1e6),
      p95Ms: finite(this.loop.percentile(95) / 1e6),
      p99Ms: finite(this.loop.percentile(99) / 1e6),
      utilization: finite(elu.utilization),
      activeMs: finite(elu.active),
      idleMs: finite(elu.idle),
    } : null;
    this.loop?.reset?.();

    const activeResources = this.captureActiveResources && typeof process.getActiveResourcesInfo === 'function'
      ? count(process.getActiveResourcesInfo())
      : null;
    const host = this.captureHost ? hostSnapshot() : null;
    const snapshot = deepFreeze({
      timestamp,
      pid: process.pid,
      ppid: process.ppid,
      uptimeSeconds: process.uptime(),
      title: process.title,
      node: process.versions.node,
      platform: process.platform,
      arch: process.arch,
      memory: { ...memory },
      cpu: {
        userMicros: cpuDelta.user,
        systemMicros: cpuDelta.system,
        totalMicros: cpuDelta.user + cpuDelta.system,
        percent: finite(((cpuDelta.user + cpuDelta.system) / elapsedMicros) * 100),
      },
      eventLoop: loop,
      resourceUsage,
      activeResources,
      gc: Object.fromEntries(Array.from(this.gc, ([kind, value]) => [kind, { ...value }])),
      host,
    });
    this.last = snapshot;
    this.stats.collections++;
    this._record(snapshot);
    const admission = this.signal?.(SignalKind.METRICS, { source: 'system', snapshot }, { timestamp, priority: 1 });
    if (admission?.accepted === false) this.stats.signalsRejected++;
    if (options.resetGc !== false) this.gc.clear();
    return snapshot;
  }

  _record(snapshot) {
    const i = this.instruments;
    if (!i) return;
    const labels = { pid: String(process.pid) };
    i.processUptime.set(snapshot.uptimeSeconds, labels);
    i.cpuPercent.set(snapshot.cpu.percent, labels);
    i.cpuUser.add(snapshot.cpu.userMicros / 1e6, labels);
    i.cpuSystem.add(snapshot.cpu.systemMicros / 1e6, labels);
    for (const [name, value] of Object.entries(snapshot.memory)) i.memory.set(value, { ...labels, kind: name });
    if (snapshot.eventLoop) {
      i.loopUtilization.set(snapshot.eventLoop.utilization, labels);
      i.loopDelay.set(snapshot.eventLoop.p99Ms / 1_000, { ...labels, quantile: 'p99' });
      i.loopDelay.set(snapshot.eventLoop.p95Ms / 1_000, { ...labels, quantile: 'p95' });
      i.loopDelay.set(snapshot.eventLoop.meanMs / 1_000, { ...labels, quantile: 'mean' });
    }
    for (const [kind, value] of Object.entries(snapshot.gc)) {
      i.gcCount.add(value.count, { kind });
      i.gcDuration.add(value.durationMs / 1_000, { kind });
    }
    for (const [kind, value] of Object.entries(snapshot.activeResources || {})) i.activeResources.set(value, { kind });
    if (snapshot.resourceUsage) {
      i.fsReads.set(snapshot.resourceUsage.fsRead || 0, labels);
      i.fsWrites.set(snapshot.resourceUsage.fsWrite || 0, labels);
      i.contextSwitches.set((snapshot.resourceUsage.voluntaryContextSwitches || 0) + (snapshot.resourceUsage.involuntaryContextSwitches || 0), labels);
      i.pageFaults.set((snapshot.resourceUsage.minorPageFault || 0) + (snapshot.resourceUsage.majorPageFault || 0), labels);
    }
    if (snapshot.host) {
      i.hostLoad.set(snapshot.host.loadAverage[0], { window: '1m' });
      i.hostLoad.set(snapshot.host.loadAverage[1], { window: '5m' });
      i.hostLoad.set(snapshot.host.loadAverage[2], { window: '15m' });
      i.hostMemory.set(snapshot.host.totalMemoryBytes, { kind: 'total' });
      i.hostMemory.set(snapshot.host.freeMemoryBytes, { kind: 'free' });
    }
  }

  _error(error) { this.stats.errors++; if (this.listenerCount('error') > 0) this.emit('error', error); else this.emit('collectorError', errorRecord(error)); }

  snapshot() { return deepFreeze({ running: this.running, closed: this.closed, intervalMs: this.intervalMs, current: this.last, stats: { ...this.stats } }); }

  async close() {
    if (this.closed) return this.snapshot();
    clearInterval(this.timer); this.timer = null;
    this.gcObserver?.disconnect?.(); this.gcObserver = null;
    this.loop?.disable?.(); this.loop = null;
    this.running = false; this.closed = true;
    return this.snapshot();
  }
}

function registerInstruments(registry) {
  return {
    processUptime: registry.gauge('monitor_process_uptime_seconds', { unit: 'seconds', description: 'Process uptime.' }),
    cpuPercent: registry.gauge('monitor_process_cpu_percent', { unit: 'percent', description: 'Process CPU use over the collection interval.' }),
    cpuUser: registry.counter('monitor_process_cpu_user_seconds_total', { unit: 'seconds' }),
    cpuSystem: registry.counter('monitor_process_cpu_system_seconds_total', { unit: 'seconds' }),
    memory: registry.gauge('monitor_process_memory_bytes', { unit: 'bytes' }),
    loopUtilization: registry.gauge('monitor_event_loop_utilization_ratio', { unit: 'ratio' }),
    loopDelay: registry.gauge('monitor_event_loop_delay_seconds', { unit: 'seconds' }),
    gcCount: registry.counter('monitor_gc_collections_total'),
    gcDuration: registry.counter('monitor_gc_duration_seconds_total', { unit: 'seconds' }),
    activeResources: registry.gauge('monitor_active_resources', { unit: 'resources' }),
    fsReads: registry.gauge('monitor_resource_fs_reads_total'),
    fsWrites: registry.gauge('monitor_resource_fs_writes_total'),
    contextSwitches: registry.gauge('monitor_resource_context_switches_total'),
    pageFaults: registry.gauge('monitor_resource_page_faults_total'),
    hostLoad: registry.gauge('monitor_host_load_average'),
    hostMemory: registry.gauge('monitor_host_memory_bytes', { unit: 'bytes' }),
  };
}

function count(values = []) { const output = Object.create(null); for (const value of values) output[value] = (output[value] || 0) + 1; return output; }
function finite(value) { return Number.isFinite(value) ? value : 0; }
function hostSnapshot() {
  return {
    hostname: os.hostname(),
    uptimeSeconds: os.uptime(),
    loadAverage: os.loadavg(),
    totalMemoryBytes: os.totalmem(),
    freeMemoryBytes: os.freemem(),
    cpuCount: os.cpus()?.length || 0,
    machine: typeof os.machine === 'function' ? os.machine() : process.arch,
    release: os.release(),
  };
}

export default ProcessMetricsCollector;

'use strict';

export class MonitorError extends Error {
  constructor(message, options = {}) {
    super(message, options.cause ? { cause: options.cause } : undefined);
    this.name = options.name || this.constructor.name;
    this.code = options.code || 'MONITOR_ERROR';
    this.status = Number(options.status || 500);
    this.retryable = options.retryable === true;
    this.details = options.details || null;
    if (options.cause && !this.cause) this.cause = options.cause;
  }

  toJSON() {
    return {
      name: this.name,
      code: this.code,
      status: this.status,
      retryable: this.retryable,
      message: this.message,
      details: this.details,
    };
  }
}

export class MonitorConfigurationError extends MonitorError {
  constructor(message, options = {}) { super(message, { ...options, code: options.code || 'MONITOR_CONFIGURATION_ERROR', status: 500 }); }
}
export class MonitorCapacityError extends MonitorError {
  constructor(message, options = {}) { super(message, { ...options, code: options.code || 'MONITOR_CAPACITY_EXCEEDED', status: 429, retryable: true }); }
}
export class MonitorSecurityError extends MonitorError {
  constructor(message, options = {}) { super(message, { ...options, code: options.code || 'MONITOR_SECURITY_ERROR', status: options.status || 403 }); }
}
export class MonitorTimeoutError extends MonitorError {
  constructor(message, options = {}) { super(message, { ...options, code: options.code || 'MONITOR_TIMEOUT', status: 504, retryable: true }); }
}
export class MonitorClosedError extends MonitorError {
  constructor(message = 'RawNode Monitor is closed.', options = {}) { super(message, { ...options, code: 'MONITOR_CLOSED', status: 503 }); }
}
export class MetricDefinitionError extends MonitorError {
  constructor(message, options = {}) { super(message, { ...options, code: options.code || 'MONITOR_METRIC_DEFINITION_ERROR', status: 422 }); }
}
export class CardinalityLimitError extends MonitorCapacityError {
  constructor(message, options = {}) { super(message, { ...options, code: 'MONITOR_CARDINALITY_LIMIT' }); }
}
export class TraceContextError extends MonitorError {
  constructor(message, options = {}) { super(message, { ...options, code: options.code || 'MONITOR_TRACE_CONTEXT_ERROR', status: 400 }); }
}
export class AlertRuleError extends MonitorError {
  constructor(message, options = {}) { super(message, { ...options, code: options.code || 'MONITOR_ALERT_RULE_ERROR', status: 422 }); }
}

'use strict';

import { access, mkdir, writeFile, unlink } from 'node:fs/promises';
import path from 'node:path';
import { deepFreeze, errorRecord } from '../support.js';

export class MonitorDoctor {
  constructor(monitor) { this.monitor = monitor; }
  async inspect(options = {}) {
    const checks = [];
    const add = (name, ok, severity = 'error', details = null) => checks.push(deepFreeze({ name, ok: ok === true, severity, details }));
    const configuration = this.monitor.configuration;
    add('configuration', configuration.validation?.ok !== false, 'error', configuration.validation);
    add('runtime state', ['running', 'idle', 'stopped'].includes(this.monitor.state), 'error', { state: this.monitor.state });
    add('signal pipeline', this.monitor.pipeline?.closed !== true, 'error', this.monitor.pipeline?.snapshot());
    add('metric registry', this.monitor.metrics?.options?.enabled !== false, 'warning', this.monitor.metrics?.snapshot());
    add('tracer', this.monitor.tracer?.options?.enabled !== false, 'warning', this.monitor.tracer?.snapshot());

    const store = this.monitor.store?.snapshot?.() || null;
    const storageEnabled = configuration.get('storage.enabled') === true;
    const storageRequired = configuration.get('storage.required') === true;
    const configuredDriver = String(configuration.get('storage.driver', 'none'));
    add('telemetry store', !!store && store.closed !== true, storageEnabled ? 'error' : 'warning', store);
    if (storageEnabled) {
      const expected = configuredDriver === 'none' ? 'none' : configuredDriver;
      const actual = String(store?.driver || 'unknown');
      const compatible = expected === actual || (expected === 'composite' && actual === 'composite');
      add('telemetry store driver', compatible, storageRequired ? 'error' : 'warning', { configured: expected, actual, fallbackFrom: store?.fallbackFrom || null, fallbackReason: store?.fallbackReason || null });
    }
    const storagePath = configuration.get('storage.path');
    if (storageEnabled && ['file', 'composite'].includes(configuredDriver) && storagePath) add('storage path writable', await writable(storagePath), storageRequired ? 'error' : 'warning', { path: storagePath });

    const health = await this.monitor.healthRegistry.all({ fresh: true, signal: options.signal }).catch((error) => ({ ok: false, error: errorRecord(error) }));
    add('registered health checks', health.ok !== false, 'error', health);
    const resources = await this.monitor.resources.collect({ diagnostics: false, throwOnRequired: false, signal: options.signal }).catch((error) => ({ error: errorRecord(error) }));
    const requiredFailures = Object.values(resources.resources || {}).filter((item) => item?.error).length;
    add('resource collection', requiredFailures === 0, requiredFailures ? 'error' : 'warning', resources);
    const errors = checks.filter((check) => !check.ok && check.severity === 'error');
    const warnings = checks.filter((check) => !check.ok && check.severity !== 'error');
    return deepFreeze({ ok: errors.length === 0, checkedAt: new Date().toISOString(), service: configuration.get('service'), environment: configuration.get('environment'), state: this.monitor.state, summary: { total: checks.length, passed: checks.filter((check) => check.ok).length, failed: errors.length, warnings: warnings.length }, checks, errors, warnings });
  }
}
async function writable(directory) { const file = path.join(directory, `.monitor-doctor-${process.pid}-${Date.now()}`); try { await mkdir(directory, { recursive: true, mode: 0o700 }); await writeFile(file, 'ok', { mode: 0o600 }); await unlink(file); await access(directory); return true; } catch { return false; } }
export default MonitorDoctor;

'use strict';

import { createGzip } from 'node:zlib';
import { createWriteStream } from 'node:fs';
import { mkdir, rename, unlink } from 'node:fs/promises';
import path from 'node:path';
import { pipeline } from 'node:stream/promises';
import { Readable } from 'node:stream';
import { deepFreeze, randomId, sha256 } from '../support.js';

export class DiagnosticBundleBuilder {
  constructor(monitor, options = {}) { this.monitor = monitor; this.root = path.resolve(options.path || monitor.configuration.get('incidents.path', 'storage/framework/monitor/diagnostics')); this.mode = options.mode ?? 0o600; }
  async build(options = {}) {
    const id = options.id || randomId('diag'); const createdAt = new Date().toISOString();
    const data = this.monitor.serializer.sanitize({ schemaVersion: 1, id, createdAt, reason: options.reason || 'manual', runtime: this.monitor.snapshot({ includeRecent: true }), health: await this.monitor.health(), diagnostics: await this.monitor.diagnostics({ includeSignals: options.includeSignals !== false, signalLimit: options.signalLimit || 500 }), process: { pid: process.pid, versions: process.versions, argv: process.argv.slice(0, 32), execPath: process.execPath, cwd: process.cwd(), env: safeEnvironment(options.environmentAllowlist || ['APP_ENV', 'NODE_ENV', 'RAWNODE_APP_PROFILE']) } }, { maxPayloadBytes: options.maxBytes || 32 * 1024 * 1024, includeErrorStack: options.includeStacks === true });
    const json = `${JSON.stringify(data, null, 2)}\n`;
    await mkdir(this.root, { recursive: true, mode: 0o700 });
    const filename = `rawnode-monitor-${createdAt.replace(/[:.]/g, '-')}-${id}.json.gz`;
    const target = path.join(this.root, filename); const temporary = `${target}.${process.pid}.tmp`;
    try { await pipeline(Readable.from([json]), createGzip({ level: 6 }), createWriteStream(temporary, { mode: this.mode })); await rename(temporary, target); }
    catch (error) { await unlink(temporary).catch(() => {}); throw error; }
    return deepFreeze({ ok: true, id, createdAt, path: target, filename, bytesUncompressed: Buffer.byteLength(json), sha256: sha256(json) });
  }
}
function safeEnvironment(allowlist) { return Object.fromEntries(allowlist.filter((key) => process.env[key] != null).map((key) => [key, process.env[key]])); }
export default DiagnosticBundleBuilder;
