'use strict';

import MonitorRuntime from './MonitorRuntime.js';

/** Laravel-style manager facade around the default runtime and named runtimes. */
export class MonitorManager {
  constructor(options = {}) { this.app = options.app || null; this.options = options; this.runtimes = new Map(); this.defaultName = options.default || 'default'; }
  create(name = this.defaultName, options = {}) { const key = String(name); if (this.runtimes.has(key) && options.replace !== true) return this.runtimes.get(key); const runtime = options.runtime || new MonitorRuntime({ ...this.options, ...options, app: options.app || this.app }); this.runtimes.set(key, runtime); return runtime; }
  runtime(name = this.defaultName) { return this.runtimes.get(String(name)) || this.create(name); }
  default() { return this.runtime(this.defaultName); }
  register(name, runtime, options = {}) { return this.create(name, { ...options, runtime }); }
  names() { return Object.freeze([...this.runtimes.keys()]); }
  async start(options = {}) { await this.default().start(options); return this; }
  async close(options = {}) { await Promise.allSettled(Array.from(this.runtimes.values()).reverse().map((runtime) => runtime.close(options))); }
  health(options = {}) { return this.default().health(options); }
  metrics(options = {}) { return this.default().metricsSnapshot(options); }
  prometheus(options = {}) { return this.default().prometheus(options); }
  snapshot(options = {}) { return Object.freeze({ default: this.defaultName, runtimes: Object.fromEntries(Array.from(this.runtimes, ([name, runtime]) => [name, runtime.snapshot(options)])) }); }
  get tracer() { return this.default().tracer; }
  get alerts() { return this.default().alerts; }
  get incidents() { return this.default().incidents; }
  get slos() { return this.default().slos; }
  get query() { return this.default().query; }
}
export default MonitorManager;

FILE PATH: ./slo/SloDefinition.js

'use strict';

import { deepFreeze, randomId } from '../support.js';
import { metricValue } from '../alerts/AlertRule.js';

export class SloDefinition {
  constructor(options = {}) {
    if (!options.name && !options.id) throw new TypeError('SLO requires a stable id or name.');
    this.id = String(options.id || slug(options.name) || randomId('slo'));
    this.name = String(options.name || this.id);
    this.description = options.description || '';
    this.objective = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, Number(options.objective ?? 0.999)));
    this.windowMs = Math.max(60_000, Number(options.windowMs || 30 * 24 * 60 * 60_000));
    this.indicator = normalizeIndicator(options.indicator || options);
    this.enabled = options.enabled !== false;
    this.tags = Object.freeze(Array.from(new Set(options.tags || [])));
    this.annotations = deepFreeze({ ...(options.annotations || {}) });
    this.burnRates = Object.freeze(options.burnRates || [1, 2, 6, 14]);
    Object.freeze(this);
  }

  async measure(context = {}) {
    if (typeof this.indicator.measure === 'function') return normalizeMeasurement(await this.indicator.measure(context, this));
    if (this.indicator.type === 'ratio') {
      const good = metricValue(context.metrics, metricRule(this.indicator.good, this.indicator.goodLabels));
      const total = metricValue(context.metrics, metricRule(this.indicator.total, this.indicator.totalLabels));
      return normalizeMeasurement({ good, total });
    }
    if (this.indicator.type === 'error-ratio') {
      const bad = metricValue(context.metrics, metricRule(this.indicator.bad, this.indicator.badLabels));
      const total = metricValue(context.metrics, metricRule(this.indicator.total, this.indicator.totalLabels));
      return normalizeMeasurement({ bad, total, good: total != null && bad != null ? Math.max(0, total - bad) : null });
    }
    if (this.indicator.type === 'availability') {
      const report = context.health;
      return normalizeMeasurement({ good: report?.ok === false ? 0 : 1, total: 1 });
    }
    return normalizeMeasurement({ good: null, total: null });
  }

  toJSON() { return { id: this.id, name: this.name, description: this.description, objective: this.objective, windowMs: this.windowMs, indicator: { ...this.indicator, measure: this.indicator.measure ? '[Function]' : undefined }, enabled: this.enabled, tags: this.tags, annotations: this.annotations, burnRates: this.burnRates }; }
}
function normalizeIndicator(input) { if (typeof input === 'function') return Object.freeze({ type: 'custom', measure: input }); if (input.measure) return Object.freeze({ ...input, type: input.type || 'custom' }); if (input.good && input.total) return Object.freeze({ ...input, type: input.type || 'ratio' }); if (input.bad && input.total) return Object.freeze({ ...input, type: 'error-ratio' }); return Object.freeze({ ...input, type: input.type || 'availability' }); }
function normalizeMeasurement(value) { if (typeof value === 'number') return { good: value, total: 1, ratio: value }; const good = Number(value?.good); const bad = Number(value?.bad); const total = Number(value?.total); const hasGood = Number.isFinite(good); const hasBad = Number.isFinite(bad); const hasTotal = Number.isFinite(total) && total >= 0; const normalizedGood = hasGood ? good : hasBad && hasTotal ? Math.max(0, total - bad) : null; const normalizedBad = hasBad ? bad : normalizedGood != null && hasTotal ? Math.max(0, total - normalizedGood) : null; const ratio = normalizedGood != null && hasTotal && total > 0 ? normalizedGood / total : null; return { good: normalizedGood, bad: normalizedBad, total: hasTotal ? total : null, ratio, details: value?.details || null }; }
function metricRule(metric, labels) { return { metric, labels: labels || {}, aggregate: 'sum' }; }
function slug(value) { return String(value || '').trim().toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '-').replace(/^-|-$/g, ''); }
export default SloDefinition;

FILE PATH: ./slo/SloRegistry.js

'use strict';

import { EventEmitter } from 'node:events';
import { SignalKind } from '../constants.js';
import SloDefinition from './SloDefinition.js';
import { deepFreeze } from '../support.js';

export class SloRegistry extends EventEmitter {
  constructor(options = {}) {
    super();
    this.intervalMs = Math.max(1_000, Number(options.intervalMs) || 60_000);
    this.historyLimit = Math.max(1, Number(options.historyLimit) || 44_640);
    this.definitions = new Map();
    this.points = new Map();
    this.latest = new Map();
    this.contextProvider = options.contextProvider || (() => ({}));
    this.signal = options.signal || null;
    this.timer = null;
    this.running = false;
    this.closed = false;
    this.frozen = false;
    this.stats = { evaluations: 0, errors: 0, pruned: 0 };
  }

  register(input, options = {}) { if (this.frozen) throw new Error('SLO registry is frozen.'); const definition = input instanceof SloDefinition ? input : new SloDefinition(input); if (this.definitions.has(definition.id) && options.replace !== true) throw new Error(`SLO '${definition.id}' is already registered.`); this.definitions.set(definition.id, definition); if (!this.points.has(definition.id)) this.points.set(definition.id, []); return definition; }
  unregister(id) { this.points.delete(String(id)); this.latest.delete(String(id)); return this.definitions.delete(String(id)); }
  freeze() { this.frozen = true; return this; }
  start() { if (this.running || this.closed) return this; this.running = true; this.timer = setInterval(() => void this.evaluate().catch((error) => this.emit('error', error)), this.intervalMs); this.timer.unref?.(); return this; }

  async evaluate(options = {}) {
    const context = await this.contextProvider(options);
    const now = Number(options.now || Date.now());
    for (const definition of this.definitions.values()) {
      if (!definition.enabled) continue;
      try {
        const measurement = await definition.measure({ ...context, now });
        if (measurement.total == null || measurement.total < 0) continue;
        const point = Object.freeze({ timestamp: now, good: measurement.good || 0, bad: measurement.bad ?? Math.max(0, measurement.total - (measurement.good || 0)), total: measurement.total, details: measurement.details || null });
        const history = this.points.get(definition.id) || [];
        history.push(point);
        const cutoff = now - definition.windowMs;
        let removed = 0;
        while (history.length && (history[0].timestamp < cutoff || history.length > this.historyLimit)) { history.shift(); removed++; }
        this.stats.pruned += removed;
        this.points.set(definition.id, history);
        const snapshot = calculate(definition, history, now);
        this.latest.set(definition.id, snapshot);
        this.stats.evaluations++;
        this.signal?.(SignalKind.SLO, snapshot, { priority: snapshot.withinBudget ? 0 : 10 });
        this.emit('evaluated', snapshot);
      } catch (error) { this.stats.errors++; this.emit('evaluationError', error, definition); }
    }
    return this.snapshot();
  }

  get(id) { return this.latest.get(String(id)) || null; }
  all() { return deepFreeze(Object.fromEntries(this.latest)); }
  burnRate(id, windowMs = null, now = Date.now()) { const definition = this.definitions.get(String(id)); if (!definition) return null; const points = (this.points.get(definition.id) || []).filter((point) => !windowMs || point.timestamp >= now - windowMs); return calculate(definition, points, now).burnRate; }

  alertRules(options = {}) {
    const output = [];
    for (const definition of this.definitions.values()) {
      for (const rate of options.rates || definition.burnRates) output.push({
        id: `slo-${definition.id}-burn-${String(rate).replace('.', '_')}`,
        name: `${definition.name} burn rate ${rate}x`,
        severity: rate >= 6 ? 'critical' : rate >= 2 ? 'warning' : 'info',
        source: 'custom',
        threshold: rate,
        comparator: 'gt',
        pendingForMs: options.pendingForMs || 0,
        evaluate: () => ({ firing: (this.get(definition.id)?.burnRate || 0) > rate, value: this.get(definition.id)?.burnRate || 0, threshold: rate }),
        annotations: { sloId: definition.id, objective: definition.objective },
        incident: rate >= 6,
      });
    }
    return output;
  }

  async close() { clearInterval(this.timer); this.timer = null; this.running = false; this.closed = true; }
  snapshot() { return deepFreeze({ running: this.running, closed: this.closed, frozen: this.frozen, definitions: Array.from(this.definitions.values(), (definition) => definition.toJSON()), latest: Object.fromEntries(this.latest), stats: { ...this.stats } }); }
}

function calculate(definition, points, now) {
  const good = points.reduce((sum, point) => sum + Number(point.good || 0), 0);
  const bad = points.reduce((sum, point) => sum + Number(point.bad || 0), 0);
  const total = points.reduce((sum, point) => sum + Number(point.total || 0), 0);
  const achieved = total > 0 ? good / total : 1;
  const allowedBadRatio = Math.max(Number.EPSILON, 1 - definition.objective);
  const actualBadRatio = total > 0 ? bad / total : 0;
  const burnRate = actualBadRatio / allowedBadRatio;
  const budgetTotal = total * allowedBadRatio;
  const budgetRemaining = budgetTotal - bad;
  return deepFreeze({ id: definition.id, name: definition.name, objective: definition.objective, windowMs: definition.windowMs, timestamp: now, windowStartedAt: now - definition.windowMs, points: points.length, good, bad, total, achieved, compliance: achieved >= definition.objective, withinBudget: budgetRemaining >= 0, errorBudget: { total: budgetTotal, consumed: bad, remaining: budgetRemaining, remainingRatio: budgetTotal > 0 ? budgetRemaining / budgetTotal : 1 }, burnRate, tags: definition.tags, annotations: definition.annotations });
}
export default SloRegistry;

'use strict';

import { deepFreeze, safeGet } from '../support.js';

/** Safe bounded query/aggregation layer over telemetry stores. */
export class MonitorQueryEngine {
  constructor(options = {}) {
    this.store = options.store;
    this.tracer = options.tracer || null;
    this.metrics = options.metrics || null;
    this.alerts = options.alerts || null;
    this.incidents = options.incidents || null;
    this.slos = options.slos || null;
    this.resources = options.resources || null;
    this.maxLimit = Math.max(1, Number(options.maxLimit) || 10_000);
    this.maxGroups = Math.max(1, Number(options.maxGroups) || 10_000);
    this.stats = { queries: 0, aggregations: 0, rejected: 0 };
  }

  async signals(options = {}) {
    this.stats.queries++;
    const limit = boundedLimit(options.limit, this.maxLimit);
    return await this.store.query({ ...options, limit });
  }
  spans(options = {}) { this.stats.queries++; return this.tracer?.query({ ...options, limit: boundedLimit(options.limit, this.maxLimit) }) || []; }
  trace(traceId) { this.stats.queries++; return this.tracer?.traceById(traceId) || []; }
  metric(name = null) { this.stats.queries++; const snapshot = this.metrics?.snapshot() || null; return name ? snapshot?.metrics?.[name] || this.metrics?.get?.(name)?.snapshot?.() || null : snapshot; }
  alertState(options = {}) { this.stats.queries++; return this.alerts?.current(options) || []; }
  incidentState(options = {}) { this.stats.queries++; return this.incidents?.list({ ...options, limit: boundedLimit(options.limit, this.maxLimit) }) || []; }
  sloState(id = null) { this.stats.queries++; return id ? this.slos?.get(id) || null : this.slos?.all() || {}; }
  resourceState(name = null) { this.stats.queries++; return name ? this.resources?.latestSnapshot(name) || null : this.resources?.latestSnapshot() || {}; }

  async aggregate(options = {}) {
    this.stats.aggregations++;
    const result = await this.signals({ ...options, limit: boundedLimit(options.scanLimit || options.limit || this.maxLimit, this.maxLimit) });
    const rows = result.items || [];
    const field = options.field || 'payload.value';
    const groupBy = Array.isArray(options.groupBy) ? options.groupBy : options.groupBy ? [options.groupBy] : [];
    const operation = String(options.operation || 'count').toLowerCase();
    const groups = new Map();
    for (const row of rows) {
      const keyValues = groupBy.map((path) => safeGet(row, path, null));
      const key = JSON.stringify(keyValues);
      if (!groups.has(key) && groups.size >= this.maxGroups) { this.stats.rejected++; break; }
      const current = groups.get(key) || { key: keyValues, count: 0, sum: 0, min: Infinity, max: -Infinity, values: [] };
      const value = Number(safeGet(row, field));
      current.count++;
      if (Number.isFinite(value)) { current.sum += value; current.min = Math.min(current.min, value); current.max = Math.max(current.max, value); if (operation.startsWith('p')) current.values.push(value); }
      groups.set(key, current);
    }
    const output = Array.from(groups.values(), (entry) => ({ labels: Object.fromEntries(groupBy.map((name, index) => [name, entry.key[index]])), value: aggregateValue(entry, operation), count: entry.count }));
    output.sort((a, b) => Number(b.value || 0) - Number(a.value || 0));
    return deepFreeze({ operation, field, groupBy, scanned: rows.length, groups: output.slice(0, boundedLimit(options.groupLimit, this.maxGroups)) });
  }

  async timeSeries(options = {}) {
    const intervalMs = Math.max(1_000, Number(options.intervalMs) || 60_000);
    const result = await this.signals({ ...options, limit: boundedLimit(options.scanLimit || this.maxLimit, this.maxLimit), order: 'asc' });
    const field = options.field || 'payload.value';
    const buckets = new Map();
    for (const row of result.items || []) {
      const timestamp = Number(row.timestamp || 0);
      const start = Math.floor(timestamp / intervalMs) * intervalMs;
      const current = buckets.get(start) || { timestamp: start, count: 0, sum: 0, min: Infinity, max: -Infinity };
      const value = Number(safeGet(row, field));
      current.count++;
      if (Number.isFinite(value)) { current.sum += value; current.min = Math.min(current.min, value); current.max = Math.max(current.max, value); }
      buckets.set(start, current);
    }
    return deepFreeze({ intervalMs, points: Array.from(buckets.values(), (bucket) => ({ ...bucket, avg: bucket.count ? bucket.sum / bucket.count : 0, min: bucket.min === Infinity ? null : bucket.min, max: bucket.max === -Infinity ? null : bucket.max })).sort((a, b) => a.timestamp - b.timestamp) });
  }

  snapshot() { return Object.freeze({ maxLimit: this.maxLimit, maxGroups: this.maxGroups, stats: Object.freeze({ ...this.stats }) }); }
}
function boundedLimit(value, maximum) { const number = Number(value); return Number.isSafeInteger(number) && number > 0 ? Math.min(number, maximum) : Math.min(100, maximum); }
function aggregateValue(entry, operation) {
  if (operation === 'count') return entry.count;
  if (operation === 'sum') return entry.sum;
  if (operation === 'avg' || operation === 'average') return entry.count ? entry.sum / entry.count : 0;
  if (operation === 'min') return entry.min === Infinity ? null : entry.min;
  if (operation === 'max') return entry.max === -Infinity ? null : entry.max;
  const percentile = /^p(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)$/.exec(operation);
  if (percentile) { const values = entry.values.sort((a, b) => a - b); if (!values.length) return null; const rank = Math.min(values.length - 1, Math.max(0, Math.ceil((Number(percentile[1]) / 100) * values.length) - 1)); return values[rank]; }
  return entry.count;
}
export default MonitorQueryEngine;

'use strict';

import { EventEmitter } from 'node:events';
import { IncidentState, SignalKind } from '../constants.js';
import RunbookRegistry from './RunbookRegistry.js';
import { deepFreeze, errorRecord, randomId, withTimeout } from '../support.js';

export class IncidentManager extends EventEmitter {
  constructor(options = {}) {
    super();
    this.maxOpen = Math.max(1, Number(options.maxOpen) || 10_000);
    this.historyLimit = Math.max(1, Number(options.historyLimit) || 20_000);
    this.captureDiagnostics = options.captureDiagnostics !== false;
    this.diagnosticsTimeoutMs = Math.max(100, Number(options.diagnosticsTimeoutMs) || 10_000);
    this.diagnosticsProvider = options.diagnosticsProvider || null;
    this.signal = options.signal || null;
    this.runbooks = options.runbooks || new RunbookRegistry();
    this.incidents = new Map();
    this.byDedupe = new Map();
    this.history = [];
    this.stats = { opened: 0, deduplicated: 0, acknowledged: 0, resolved: 0, diagnosticsErrors: 0 };
  }

  async open(options = {}) {
    const dedupeKey = String(options.dedupeKey || `${options.source || 'manual'}:${options.alert?.ruleId || options.title || 'incident'}`);
    const existingId = this.byDedupe.get(dedupeKey);
    const existing = existingId ? this.incidents.get(existingId) : null;
    if (existing && ![IncidentState.RESOLVED, IncidentState.CLOSED].includes(existing.state)) {
      this.stats.deduplicated++;
      await this.annotate(existing.id, { type: 'duplicate', message: options.message || options.title || 'Repeated incident signal', data: options.data || options.alert || null });
      return this.public(existing);
    }
    const openCount = Array.from(this.incidents.values()).filter((incident) => ![IncidentState.RESOLVED, IncidentState.CLOSED].includes(incident.state)).length;
    if (openCount >= this.maxOpen) throw new Error('Monitor open incident limit exceeded.');
    const now = Date.now();
    const incident = {
      id: options.id || randomId('inc'),
      dedupeKey,
      title: String(options.title || options.alert?.rule?.name || 'RawNode incident'),
      description: options.description || options.message || '',
      severity: options.severity || options.alert?.severity || 'warning',
      state: IncidentState.OPEN,
      source: options.source || 'manual',
      alertId: options.alert?.id || null,
      ruleId: options.alert?.ruleId || null,
      runbook: options.runbook || options.alert?.rule?.runbook || null,
      owner: options.owner || null,
      tenantId: options.tenantId || options.alert?.tenantId || null,
      traceId: options.traceId || options.alert?.traceId || null,
      tags: Object.freeze([...(options.tags || options.alert?.tags || [])]),
      createdAt: now,
      updatedAt: now,
      acknowledgedAt: null,
      mitigatedAt: null,
      resolvedAt: null,
      closedAt: null,
      timeline: [],
      diagnostics: null,
      metadata: options.metadata || null,
    };
    incident.timeline.push(timeline('opened', options.actor, options.message || incident.description, options.data || options.alert || null, now));
    this.incidents.set(incident.id, incident); this.byDedupe.set(dedupeKey, incident.id); this.stats.opened++;
    if (this.captureDiagnostics && this.diagnosticsProvider) {
      try { incident.diagnostics = await withTimeout(() => this.diagnosticsProvider({ incident: this.public(incident), options }), this.diagnosticsTimeoutMs); }
      catch (error) { this.stats.diagnosticsErrors++; incident.diagnostics = { ok: false, error: errorRecord(error) }; }
    }
    await this._record('opened', incident);
    return this.public(incident);
  }

  async openFromAlert(alert) { return await this.open({ source: 'alert', alert, title: alert.rule?.name || 'Alert incident', description: alert.rule?.description || alert.evaluation?.reason, severity: alert.severity, runbook: alert.rule?.runbook, dedupeKey: `alert:${alert.ruleId}` }); }
  async acknowledge(id, options = {}) { return await this._transition(id, IncidentState.ACKNOWLEDGED, 'acknowledged', options); }
  async mitigate(id, options = {}) { return await this._transition(id, IncidentState.MITIGATED, 'mitigated', options); }
  async resolve(id, options = {}) { return await this._transition(id, IncidentState.RESOLVED, 'resolved', options); }
  async closeIncident(id, options = {}) { return await this._transition(id, IncidentState.CLOSED, 'closed', options); }

  async _transition(id, state, event, options) {
    const incident = this.incidents.get(String(id)); if (!incident) return null;
    const now = Date.now(); incident.state = state; incident.updatedAt = now; incident[`${event}At`] = now; incident.timeline.push(timeline(event, options.actor, options.message, options.data || options.alert || null, now));
    if (state === IncidentState.ACKNOWLEDGED) this.stats.acknowledged++;
    if (state === IncidentState.RESOLVED) { this.stats.resolved++; this.byDedupe.delete(incident.dedupeKey); }
    if (state === IncidentState.CLOSED) this.byDedupe.delete(incident.dedupeKey);
    await this._record(event, incident); return this.public(incident);
  }

  async annotate(id, options = {}) { const incident = this.incidents.get(String(id)); if (!incident) return null; incident.updatedAt = Date.now(); incident.timeline.push(timeline(options.type || 'annotation', options.actor, options.message, options.data, incident.updatedAt)); await this._record('annotated', incident); return this.public(incident); }
  get(id) { const incident = this.incidents.get(String(id)); return incident ? this.public(incident) : null; }
  list(options = {}) { let rows = Array.from(this.incidents.values()); if (options.state) rows = rows.filter((item) => item.state === options.state); if (options.severity) rows = rows.filter((item) => item.severity === options.severity); if (options.tenantId) rows = rows.filter((item) => item.tenantId === options.tenantId); rows.sort((a, b) => b.updatedAt - a.updatedAt); return deepFreeze(rows.slice(0, Math.max(1, Number(options.limit) || 100)).map((item) => this.public(item))); }
  public(incident) { return deepFreeze({ ...incident, timeline: incident.timeline.slice(), runbookDefinition: incident.runbook ? this.runbooks.get(incident.runbook) : null }); }
  async _record(event, incident) { const record = deepFreeze({ event, timestamp: Date.now(), incident: this.public(incident) }); this.history.push(record); if (this.history.length > this.historyLimit) this.history.splice(0, this.history.length - this.historyLimit); this.signal?.(SignalKind.INCIDENT, record, { priority: incident.severity === 'critical' ? 30 : 10 }); this.emit(event, record); }
  historySnapshot(options = {}) { return deepFreeze(this.history.slice(-(Math.max(1, Number(options.limit) || 100)))); }
  snapshot() { return deepFreeze({ open: Array.from(this.incidents.values()).filter((item) => ![IncidentState.RESOLVED, IncidentState.CLOSED].includes(item.state)).length, incidents: this.list({ limit: 1_000 }), history: this.history.length, runbooks: this.runbooks.snapshot(), stats: { ...this.stats } }); }
}
function timeline(type, actor, message, data, at = Date.now()) { return Object.freeze({ id: randomId('timeline'), type, at, actor: actor || null, message: message || null, data: data || null }); }
export default IncidentManager;

'use strict';
export class RunbookRegistry {
  constructor() { this.runbooks = new Map(); this.frozen = false; }
  register(id, runbook = null, options = {}) {
    if (this.frozen) throw new Error('Runbook registry is frozen.');
    if (id && typeof id === 'object') { options = runbook || {}; runbook = id; id = runbook.id || runbook.name; }
    if (!id || !runbook) throw new TypeError('Runbook id and definition are required.');
    if (this.runbooks.has(String(id)) && options.replace !== true) throw new Error(`Runbook '${id}' is already registered.`);
    const record = Object.freeze({ id: String(id), title: runbook.title || String(id), description: runbook.description || runbook.summary || '', url: runbook.url || null, steps: Object.freeze([...(runbook.steps || [])]), owner: runbook.owner || null, tags: Object.freeze([...(runbook.tags || [])]) });
    this.runbooks.set(record.id, record); return record;
  }
  get(id) { return this.runbooks.get(String(id)) || null; }
  list() { return Object.freeze([...this.runbooks.values()]); }
  freeze() { this.frozen = true; return this; }
  snapshot() { return Object.freeze({ frozen: this.frozen, runbooks: this.list() }); }
}
export default RunbookRegistry;

'use strict';

import { SignalKind } from '../constants.js';
import { deepFreeze, randomId, sha256 } from '../support.js';

export class DeploymentRegistry {
  constructor(options = {}) { this.signal = options.signal || null; this.limit = Math.max(1, Number(options.limit) || 1_000); this.deployments = []; this.current = null; this.stats = { recorded: 0, rollbacks: 0 }; }
  record(input = {}) {
    const record = deepFreeze({ id: input.id || randomId('deploy'), service: input.service || null, environment: input.environment || null, version: input.version || null, commit: input.commit || input.gitSha || null, buildId: input.buildId || null, instanceId: input.instanceId || null, region: input.region || null, actor: input.actor || null, strategy: input.strategy || null, status: input.status || 'deployed', metadata: input.metadata || null, fingerprint: input.fingerprint || sha256({ version: input.version, commit: input.commit || input.gitSha, buildId: input.buildId }), deployedAt: Number(input.deployedAt || Date.now()) });
    this.deployments.push(record); if (this.deployments.length > this.limit) this.deployments.splice(0, this.deployments.length - this.limit); this.current = record; this.stats.recorded++;
    if (record.status === 'rolled_back') this.stats.rollbacks++;
    this.signal?.(SignalKind.DEPLOYMENT, record, { priority: record.status === 'failed' ? 20 : 5 });
    return record;
  }
  latest() { return this.current; }
  around(timestamp = Date.now(), options = {}) { const at = Number(timestamp) || Date.now(); const windowMs = Math.max(0, Number(options.windowMs) || 15 * 60_000); const rows = this.deployments.filter((item) => Math.abs(item.deployedAt - at) <= windowMs).sort((left, right) => Math.abs(left.deployedAt - at) - Math.abs(right.deployedAt - at)); return deepFreeze(rows.slice(0, Math.max(1, Number(options.limit) || 20))); }
  list(options = {}) { let rows = [...this.deployments]; if (options.service) rows = rows.filter((row) => row.service === options.service); if (options.environment) rows = rows.filter((row) => row.environment === options.environment); return deepFreeze(rows.sort((a, b) => b.deployedAt - a.deployedAt).slice(0, Math.max(1, Number(options.limit) || 100))); }
  compare(leftId, rightId) { const left = this.deployments.find((item) => item.id === leftId); const right = this.deployments.find((item) => item.id === rightId); if (!left || !right) return null; return deepFreeze({ left, right, changed: Object.fromEntries(Object.keys({ ...left, ...right }).filter((key) => JSON.stringify(left[key]) !== JSON.stringify(right[key])).map((key) => [key, { left: left[key], right: right[key] }])) }); }
  snapshot() { return deepFreeze({ current: this.current, deployments: this.list({ limit: this.limit }), stats: { ...this.stats } }); }
}
export default DeploymentRegistry;

'use strict';

function safeResolve(app, key, fallback = null) {
  try { return app?.resolve?.(key) ?? app?.container?.resolve?.(key) ?? fallback; }
  catch { return fallback; }
}
function boolean(value, fallback = false) {
  if (value === true || value === false) return value;
  if (value == null) return fallback;
  return ['1', 'true', 'yes', 'on'].includes(String(value).toLowerCase());
}
function integer(value, fallback, min = 1, max = 100_000) {
  const number = Number(value);
  return Number.isSafeInteger(number) ? Math.max(min, Math.min(max, number)) : fallback;
}

export class BaseMonitorCommand {
  constructor(app) { this.app = app; this.signature = ''; this.description = ''; this.args = Object.create(null); this.flags = Object.create(null); }
  parse(argv = []) {
    this.args = Object.create(null); this.flags = Object.create(null);
    const parts = this.signature.trim().split(/\s+/);
    if (parts[0] !== argv[0]) return false;
    const positionals = [];
    for (let index = 1; index < argv.length; index++) {
      const value = argv[index];
      if (String(value).startsWith('--')) {
        const equal = value.indexOf('=');
        const key = equal === -1 ? value.slice(2) : value.slice(2, equal);
        if (equal !== -1) this.flags[key] = value.slice(equal + 1);
        else if (argv[index + 1] != null && !String(argv[index + 1]).startsWith('-')) this.flags[key] = argv[++index];
        else this.flags[key] = true;
      } else positionals.push(value);
    }
    let cursor = 0;
    for (const part of parts.slice(1)) if (part.startsWith('{') && part.endsWith('}')) this.args[part.slice(1, -1).replace(/[?*]$/, '')] = positionals[cursor++];
    return true;
  }
  argument(name, fallback = undefined) { return this.args[name] ?? fallback; }
  option(name, fallback = undefined) { return this.flags[name] ?? fallback; }
  runtime() { const value = safeResolve(this.app, 'monitor.runtime') || safeResolve(this.app, 'monitor'); if (!value) throw new Error('RawNode Monitor runtime is not registered.'); return typeof value.default === 'function' && !value.configuration ? value.default() : value; }
  output(value, options = {}) {
    const json = boolean(this.option('json'), options.json === true);
    if (typeof value === 'string' && !json) process.stdout.write(value.endsWith('\n') ? value : `${value}\n`);
    else process.stdout.write(`${JSON.stringify(value, null, json ? 2 : 2)}\n`);
    return value;
  }
}

export class MonitorStatusCommand extends BaseMonitorCommand {
  constructor(app) { super(app); this.signature = 'monitor:status'; this.description = 'Show RawNode Monitor runtime, health, pipeline, fleet, and incident status.'; }
  async handle() { return this.output(this.runtime().snapshot({ limit: integer(this.option('limit'), 25) })); }
}
export class MonitorDoctorCommand extends BaseMonitorCommand {
  constructor(app) { super(app); this.signature = 'monitor:doctor'; this.description = 'Run production-readiness diagnostics for RawNode Monitor.'; }
  async handle() { const report = await this.runtime().doctor({ deep: boolean(this.option('deep')) }); this.output(report); if (!report.ok) process.exitCode = 1; return report; }
}
export class MonitorMetricsCommand extends BaseMonitorCommand {
  constructor(app) { super(app); this.signature = 'monitor:metrics {name}'; this.description = 'Inspect metric instruments, series, and optional history.'; }
  async handle() { const value = this.runtime().metricsSnapshot({ history: boolean(this.option('history')), name: this.argument('name'), limit: integer(this.option('limit'), 100) }); return this.output(value); }
}
export class MonitorPrometheusCommand extends BaseMonitorCommand {
  constructor(app) { super(app); this.signature = 'monitor:prometheus'; this.description = 'Print Prometheus text exposition for the current runtime.'; }
  async handle() { return this.output(this.runtime().prometheus(), { json: false }); }
}
export class MonitorHealthCommand extends BaseMonitorCommand {
  constructor(app) { super(app); this.signature = 'monitor:health'; this.description = 'Run Monitor readiness, liveness, and dependency checks.'; }
  async handle() { const runtime = this.runtime(); const mode = this.option('readiness') ? 'readiness' : this.option('liveness') ? 'liveness' : 'health'; const report = mode === 'readiness' ? await runtime.readiness({ fresh: true }) : mode === 'liveness' ? await runtime.liveness({ fresh: true }) : await runtime.health({ fresh: true }); this.output(report); if (report.ok === false) process.exitCode = 1; return report; }
}
export class MonitorResourcesCommand extends BaseMonitorCommand {
  constructor(app) { super(app); this.signature = 'monitor:resources {name}'; this.description = 'Collect or inspect registered RawNode resource telemetry.'; }
  async handle() { const runtime = this.runtime(); const name = this.argument('name'); const value = boolean(this.option('collect')) ? await runtime.collectResources({ fresh: true }) : name ? runtime.resources.latestSnapshot(name) : runtime.resources.snapshot(); return this.output(value); }
}
export class MonitorTracesCommand extends BaseMonitorCommand {
  constructor(app) { super(app); this.signature = 'monitor:traces {traceId}'; this.description = 'Query recent traces or inspect one trace waterfall.'; }
  async handle() { const runtime = this.runtime(); const traceId = this.argument('traceId'); const value = traceId ? runtime.tracer.traceById(traceId) : runtime.tracer.query({ limit: integer(this.option('limit'), 100), status: this.option('status'), minDurationMs: this.option('min-duration-ms') }); return this.output(value); }
}
export class MonitorAlertsCommand extends BaseMonitorCommand {
  constructor(app) { super(app); this.signature = 'monitor:alerts {action} {id}'; this.description = 'List, evaluate, acknowledge, or silence monitor alerts.'; }
  async handle() {
    const runtime = this.runtime(); const action = this.argument('action', 'list'); const id = this.argument('id');
    let value;
    if (action === 'evaluate') value = await runtime.alerts.evaluate();
    else if (action === 'acknowledge') value = { ok: runtime.alerts.acknowledge(id, this.option('actor') || 'console') };
    else if (action === 'silence') value = runtime.alerts.silences.create({ ruleIds: id ? [id] : [], durationMs: integer(this.option('duration-ms'), 60 * 60_000, 1, 30 * 24 * 60 * 60_000), reason: this.option('reason', 'Console maintenance'), createdBy: this.option('actor', 'console') });
    else value = { current: runtime.alerts.current({ state: this.option('state'), severity: this.option('severity') }), history: runtime.alerts.historySnapshot({ limit: integer(this.option('limit'), 100) }), silences: runtime.alerts.silences.snapshot() };
    return this.output(value);
  }
}
export class MonitorSlosCommand extends BaseMonitorCommand {
  constructor(app) { super(app); this.signature = 'monitor:slos {id}'; this.description = 'Evaluate and inspect SLO compliance, error budgets, and burn rates.'; }
  async handle() { const runtime = this.runtime(); if (boolean(this.option('evaluate'))) await runtime.slos.evaluate(); const id = this.argument('id'); return this.output(id ? runtime.slos.get(id) : runtime.slos.snapshot()); }
}
export class MonitorIncidentsCommand extends BaseMonitorCommand {
  constructor(app) { super(app); this.signature = 'monitor:incidents {action} {id}'; this.description = 'List, acknowledge, annotate, resolve, or close incidents.'; }
  async handle() {
    const manager = this.runtime().incidents; const action = this.argument('action', 'list'); const id = this.argument('id'); const actor = this.option('actor', 'console'); let value;
    if (action === 'acknowledge') value = manager.acknowledge(id, { actor, message: this.option('message') });
    else if (action === 'annotate') value = manager.annotate(id, { actor, message: this.option('message', 'Console annotation') });
    else if (action === 'resolve') value = manager.resolve(id, { actor, message: this.option('message', 'Resolved from console') });
    else if (action === 'close') value = manager.closeIncident(id, { actor, message: this.option('message') });
    else value = { incidents: manager.list({ state: this.option('state'), severity: this.option('severity'), limit: integer(this.option('limit'), 100) }), history: manager.historySnapshot({ limit: integer(this.option('limit'), 100) }) };
    return this.output(value);
  }
}
export class MonitorAnomaliesCommand extends BaseMonitorCommand {
  constructor(app) { super(app); this.signature = 'monitor:anomalies {kind}'; this.description = 'Inspect adaptive anomaly baselines and leak-trend detections.'; }
  async handle() { const runtime = this.runtime(); const kind = this.argument('kind', 'all'); const options = { includeBaselines: boolean(this.option('baselines')), includeSeries: boolean(this.option('series')) }; const value = kind === 'leaks' ? runtime.leaks.snapshot(options) : kind === 'adaptive' ? runtime.anomalies.snapshot(options) : { anomalies: runtime.anomalies.snapshot(options), leaks: runtime.leaks.snapshot(options) }; return this.output(value); }
}
export class MonitorSyntheticsCommand extends BaseMonitorCommand {
  constructor(app) { super(app); this.signature = 'monitor:synthetics {action} {id}'; this.description = 'List or execute HTTP, TCP, DNS, and custom synthetic checks.'; }
  async handle() { const runtime = this.runtime(); const action = this.argument('action', 'list'); const id = this.argument('id'); let value; if (action === 'run') value = id ? await runtime.synthetics.run(id, { force: boolean(this.option('force')) }) : await runtime.synthetics.runAll({ force: boolean(this.option('force')) }); else value = runtime.synthetics.snapshot({ includeHistory: boolean(this.option('history')), limit: integer(this.option('limit'), 100) }); return this.output(value); }
}
export class MonitorCorrelateCommand extends BaseMonitorCommand {
  constructor(app) { super(app); this.signature = 'monitor:correlate {traceId}'; this.description = 'Build a ranked cross-signal timeline around a trace, request, incident, or timestamp.'; }
  async handle() { const value = await this.runtime().correlate({ traceId: this.argument('traceId'), requestId: this.option('request-id'), tenantId: this.option('tenant'), timestamp: this.option('timestamp') ? Number(this.option('timestamp')) : Date.now() }, { windowMs: this.option('window-ms') ? Number(this.option('window-ms')) : undefined, limit: integer(this.option('limit'), 500) }); return this.output(value); }
}

export class MonitorDiagnosticsCommand extends BaseMonitorCommand {
  constructor(app) { super(app); this.signature = 'monitor:diagnostics'; this.description = 'Print a redacted, bounded Monitor diagnostics snapshot.'; }
  async handle() { return this.output(await this.runtime().diagnostics({ includeSignals: boolean(this.option('signals')), signalLimit: integer(this.option('limit'), 100), includeRecentTraces: boolean(this.option('traces')) })); }
}
export class MonitorBundleCommand extends BaseMonitorCommand {
  constructor(app) { super(app); this.signature = 'monitor:bundle'; this.description = 'Create a compressed, redacted incident diagnostics bundle.'; }
  async handle() { return this.output(await this.runtime().diagnosticBundle({ reason: this.option('reason', 'console-manual'), includeSignals: boolean(this.option('signals'), true) })); }
}
export class MonitorDeploymentCommand extends BaseMonitorCommand {
  constructor(app) { super(app); this.signature = 'monitor:deployment {status}'; this.description = 'Record a deployment marker for metric, trace, and incident correlation.'; }
  async handle() { return this.output(this.runtime().recordDeployment({ status: this.argument('status', 'deployed'), version: this.option('version'), buildId: this.option('build'), commit: this.option('commit'), actor: this.option('actor'), strategy: this.option('strategy') })); }
}
export class MonitorFlushCommand extends BaseMonitorCommand {
  constructor(app) { super(app); this.signature = 'monitor:flush'; this.description = 'Flush bounded telemetry queues and exporters.'; }
  async handle() { return this.output(await this.runtime().flush({ timeoutMs: integer(this.option('timeout-ms'), 10_000, 100, 600_000) })); }
}
